nareśvaraparīkṣā 


by sadyojyoti


Kṣṭṣ 45 

śrīmadrāmakaṇṭhācāryakṛtayā

prakāśa ṭīkayā

saṃvalitā

kulapateḥ pro. rāmamūrtiśarmaṇaḥ prastāvanayā vibhūṣitā

sampādakaḥ

ācārya śrīrāmajīmālavīyaḥ

ācāryo'dhyakṣaśca
sāṃkhya- yogatantrāgamavibhāgasya
evaṃ
prati- kulapatiḥ
sampūrṇānandasaṃskṛtaviśvavidyālayasya
vārāṇasī

vārāṇasyām

2056 tame vaikramābde	1921 tame śakābde	2000 tame khraistābde



śrīmadācāryasadyojyotirviracitā

nareśvaraparīkṣā

śrīnārāyaṇakaṇṭhasūnuśrīmadrāgakaṇṭhācāryaviracita-
prakāśākhyaṭīkayā saṃvalitā

prathamaṃ kāṇḍam

atha meyābdhiratnasya śaṅkarasyāmitadyuteḥ |
parīkṣāṃ leśato vacmi puṃsparīkṣāpurassaram || 1 ||

taṃ śaktiśaktimadrūpaṃ natvā nāreśvaraṃ param |
nareśvaraparīkṣāyāmakṣarārthaḥ prakāśyate || 2 ||

	prakaraṇasya tāvadasya prayojanamāha atha iti, īśvaraparīkṣā 
vaktavyatā adhikriyamāṇābhidheyā tasyāḥ prayojanaṃ tu 
parameśvaraviṣayajñānotpādaḥ parasya śreyasaḥ sādhanam- iti vakṣyate 
prakaraṇasamāptau atra tu samastatattvabhuvanādiprameyasāgarasāratvena 
bhagavato ratnarūpatayā, śaṅkarapadena tadevāha śreyaskaro hi 
sarvaiścintāmaṇiriva parīkṣyante | 
sambandhaścopāyopeyalakṣaṇo'rthasiddhiḥ- ityevaṃ sarvasya 
prekṣāvato'tra pravṛttisiddhiḥ | anena darśanāntarasiddhānāṃ 
hariharahiraṇyagarbhādīnāmīśvaratve'pi aśaṅkaratvāt na prekṣāvantaḥ 
parīkṣāyāṃ pravartanta iti darśayati | atha kathaṃ tasyaiva 
tathābhūtatvaṃ yato'mitadyutiḥ | yastu prakṛtiḥ

prakṛtīśvaravādibhiḥ, brahma brahmavidbhiḥ, nārāyaṇo vā 
pāñcarātraiḥ, īśvaro jagadupādānakāraṇatvena kalpitaḥ sa tata eva 
mṛdādivadacetanatvaprasaṅge sati mitaśaktireva, guṇātmāpi sāṅkhyaiḥ 
prakṛteḥ kartṛtvābhyupagamād īśvaro'kartābhyupagata eva, 
vikāreśvaravādibhirapi brahmaṇo hiravyagarbhākhyo mahāvibhūtiśca 
turātmyalakṣaṇaśceśvaro yo'bhyupagatastasya 
sargapralayayogānmitaśaktitvamupagatameva, yairapi puruṣaviśeṣaḥ 
prakṛṣṭatarabuddhyādiyogāt kleśādibhiraparāmarśācca īśvaro varṇyate, 
tairapi buddhyādīnāṃ vyaktatvenāvaśyaṃ vināśāt tasya 
mitaśaktitvameṣṭavyam, yaistu manaḥsaṃyogādīśvaro gīyate |

	tairmanasāmacaitanye sati anekatvād ghaṭādivat 
kāryatvenānityatvato'sāvapi mitaśaktirabhyupagantavyaḥ, nityatve'pi ca 
manaso muktātmavat tatsaṃyogasyānityatvāt

tatraiṣa prasaṅgo'nivārya eva | vyāpakatvād muktātmanāmapi manaḥ 
saṃyogo'stīti cet, tarhi te'pi īśvarā iti svāgamavirodhaḥ | saṃyoge'pi 
manaso'dṛṣṭavaśāt karaṇatvamiti cet, īśvarasya 
tadabhāvādanīśvaratvaprasaṅgaḥ | īśvarasya svaśaktyaiva sarvadā 
manoyoga iti cet, manoyogācchaktatvaṃ śakteśca manoyoga 
itītaretarāśrayatvāt na kiñcidetat | ayaṃ tu parameśvaro 
vakṣyamāṇanayena sarvatra sarvadā cāpratihataśaktiḥ, ityamitadyutitvena 
śaṅkara uktaḥ, ata eva nātra prakaraṇāntareṣviva namaskārapūrvakaṃ 
pravṛttistatparīkṣābhidhānenaiva sarvavighnanivṛtteḥ prakaraṇakaraṇa- 
sāmarthyavyaktervotpādāt | tathā ca śrutiḥ-

yasya tatparamaṃ tejaḥ śaivaṃ hṛdi virājate |
brahmādayo'pi hi surā na taṃ hiṃsanti sādhakam || iti || 1 ||

	iyaṃ ca parīkṣā puruṣaparīkṣāpūrvikaivopapadyate, yataḥ kecit 
puruṣa evedaṃsarvamataḥ kimīśvareṇa ityāhuḥ | anye tu pratikṣaṇaṃ 
svarasata evārthānāmutpattivināśādadhiṣṭhātṛśūnyatvam | pare tu 
yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ jīvet iti karmādyādhiṣṭheyābhāvo yatastat 
kimīśvareṇa ityācakṣate- iti puruṣaparīkṣāṃ vinā tatparīkṣā 
nopapadyata eva, - iti prathamaṃ saivopakramate-

jñātā kartā ca bodhena buddhvā bodhyaṃ pravartate |
pravṛttiphalabhoktā ca yaḥ pumānucyate'tra saḥ || 2 ||

	jñātā jñānaśaktiyokto na tu sāṅkhyabhedānāmiva 
naiyāyikavaiśiṣikāṇāmiva vā nityānumeyaḥ 
sarvadārthaprakāśakatvenāsya svato'vabhāsanāt svata eva 
vānumeyatvānupapatteśca- iti vakṣyāmaḥ | kartā kriyāśaktiyuktaḥ 
tacchaktyaiva śarīrādau spandādyanubhūteḥ, na tu 
sāṅkhyānāmivākartā svānubhavavirodhāt, na ca śaktiyoga eva 
pariṇāmitā ātmanaścicchakterapi abhāvato nairātmyaprasaṅgāt, api tu 
spando rūpāntarāpattirvā sā cāsya tatkartṛtvādeva na sambhavatīti 
cicchaktyeva kriyāśaktyāpi yoge'sya na virodhaḥ- iti vakṣyāmaḥ | ceti 
samuccitametadātmano rūpaṃ na pratyekam | api ca 
bodhenādhyavasāyātmanā buddhirūpeṇa pratyakṣeṇaiva buddhyā 
niścitya pravartate, na tu jaiminīyādīnāmiva nityānumeyenaiva 
tathaivānubhavāt- iti vakṣyāmaḥ | bodhyaṃ ca vyatiriktaṃ vastveva 
buddhvā na tu śūnyavādināmiva jñānam, vedāntabhedānāmiva vā 
avastveva, tasya nirupākhyatvena sarvaśaktivirahalakṣaṇatvād 
bodhyasvabhāvatvāyogaḥ, tadyoge vā na

 tathātvamiti | kiṃ ca bodhyaṃ buddhvā iti na bodhyadharmastadbodho 
jaiminīyabhedānāmiva tasya sarvān prati aviśeṣāt 
sarvaboddhṛbodhyatāprasaṅgāt, api tu boddhṛsvabhāva eva 
svaprakāśātmā, iti darśayati pravartate iti | evaṃ 
vidhāvagamapūrvakāryakāraṇādikriyāṃ dṛṣṭaphalāmadṛṣṭaphalāṃ 
ca kartumārabhate | tadakartṛkapakṣe tu prakṛtereva kartṛtvābhyupagamāt 
tatpreraṇayā vinā vivaśasyonmādādineva kāryakāraṇāni svata evāsya 
yatra tatra pravarteran, ityanubhavasiddhasvātmakartṛkatvavirodhaḥ- iti 
sarvamagre bhaviṣyati | api ca pravṛttiphalabhoktā ca ityakṣaṇikaḥ 
kṣaṇikatve hi anya eva dānādikriyānuṣṭhātānyaśca tatphalabhoktā iti 
sarvadaikarūpasthiragrāhakasvasaṃvedanātmanā 
sarvavyavahārahetubhūtenānubhavena virodhaḥ, iti vakṣyāmaḥ | evaṃ 
viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭatayā pratyātmaṃ svasaṃvedanasiddho yaḥ puruṣaḥ 
so'trāsmin darśane sarvānyavipratipattinirākaraṇena pratipādyate, iti 
pratijñārthaḥ || 2 ||

tatrāsya tāvatpratyakṣāvirodhamāha-

kartrādinā catuṣkeṇa vyavahāraḥ samāpyate |

	sarveṇa hi vādinānubhavasiddhatvenānādiprarūḍho 
lokavyavahāro'nusartavyaḥ, sa ca sarva eva lavanapacanādyātmakaḥ 
kartrādinācatuṣkeṇa ityupalakṣaṇaṃ kriyānibandhanena kārakabhedena 
yathāsambhavaṃ kvacidekena dvābhyāṃ tribhirvā 
ṣaṭparyantairniṣpādyamāno dṛśyate, kriyayā hi vinā 
kartṛkaraṇādayo'rthā eva, na tu kārakavaicitryamāsādayanti sā ca na 
tairvineti |

	atha kimanena kriyākārakaviśeṣavādena sāmagrīlakṣaṇā hi 
bījādayo'rthāstattadavasthāyuktā eva viśiṣṭamaṅkurādikāryaṃ 
prāgbhāvamātreṇa kāraṇatayā kariṣyanti, iti kāraṇaikāntavādino 
jaināḥ tadayuktam, yataḥ-

vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyo nāyamekāntavādibhiḥ || 3 ||

	evaṃ hi hantṛvadhyayordātṛyācakayorvā 
prāgbhāvamātreṇaikasmin vadhalakṣaṇe dānādike vā kārye 
kāraṇatvamaviśiṣṭamiti samānaphalatvamaniṣṭaṃ bhavatāṃ prasajyeta | 
na svarūpabhedena tasyā eva sāmagryā āntaraviśeṣakṛtatvāditi cet, na, 
kiñcidetad ubhayorapi duṣkṛtatvāt kāraṇatvācca hanturiva vadhyasyāpi, 
asau vadhaḥ para eva | svaparasantānabhedāpekṣatayaitaduktamiti cet, astu, 
upayogabhedastu nāstyeva | svaparasantānabhedāt so'pi kathañcidastīti cet, 
tarhi ayameva kartṛkarmādibhedātmakaḥ kriyākārakaviśeṣavādo 
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalahetubhūto vyavahārasiddhaḥ
 kāraṇaikāntavādibhirna vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyaḥ � iti 
tadvyavasthāpanāya kārakavādo'bhyupagantavyaḥ, ityabādhaḥ 
pratijñārthasya | etacca dānādāvapyanusandheyam || 3 ||

	nanu paramārthataḥ sarva evāyaṃ bhedāśrayo vyavahāro'satya 
ekaikasyānandātmano brahmaṇaḥ satyatvāt | taduktam-

nāyaṃ hanti na hanyate | (gītā 2\19) iti |

etadapyayuktam, tathā hi-

sarvaikatvaprasiddhau tu pramāṇaṃ nāsti kiñcana |

	pratyakṣānumānayorakṣaliṅgāśrayatvena bhedaviṣayatvataḥ 
pratyuta tadbādhakatvāt, sattāmātraviṣayatvāt tayoratrābādhakatvamiti 
cet, tarhi pratyakṣādibhedāsiddheḥ pramāṇābhāvo'stu 
paramārthenādvayasyaiva śakyatvāditi cet, na, pramāṇābhāvena 
tasyaivāsiddheriti vakṣyāmaḥ |

athātra pramāṇam-

āmnāyaścet |

tathā ca śrūyate-

ekaṃ brahma paraṃ satyaṃ neha nānāsti kiñcana |
idaṃ pheno na kiñcidvā budbudo vā na kiñcana || iti |

	kārye'rtha iva ca siddhe'pyarthe vedasya prāmāṇyaṃ 
nopaniṣadbhāgasya karmakāṇḍavākyaikavākyatayārthavādatvamapi tu 
pramāṇatvamevātra, iti cet-

na yenāsau vāsanāsamprakāśakaḥ || 4 ||

	asāvāmnāyastāvanna satyabhūtabrahmādvaitapratipādakatvena 
pramāṇaṃ tasya sarvaparikalpanātītarūpatvatastadaviṣayatvābhyupa-
gamāt, tadviṣayatve tvāgamasyāpi satyatvād 
asato'tadviṣayatvenāpramāṇatvāt na 
satyādvaitasiddhidvaitanibandhanatvātpramāṇaprameyabhāvasya, iti | yadi 
paramasyāḥ sakalabhedarūpāyāḥ 
pratipatteravidyāvāsanātvaprakāśakatvena pramāṇamabhyupagantavyaḥ 
pāramparyeṇa tvadaitātmani, yadāhuḥ neti netyātmagatiḥ iti, 
tataścāvastuviṣayatvād asya eṣa vandhyāsuto yāti ityādivākyajātasyeva 
prāmāṇyābhāvāt punarapyadvaitāsiddhireva || 4 ||

 yadyevaṃ bhedasya satyatvāt pratiśarīramiva pratyarthaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ ca 
bāhyāsyārthasyānahaṅkārāspadasyāhaṅkārāspadaṃ vijñānaṃ 
bhinnameva grāhakamanubhavasiddhamastu nānyaḥ kaścidātmā nāma, iti 
vaibhāṣikāḥ | 
tasyopalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasyānupalabdheranupalabhyasvarūpasya ca sattā 
duḥsādhyaiva, satyam, ata evendriyādiriva kāryād hi kāraṇamātraṃ 
sadṛśamevānumīyate, tacca pūrvakaṃ vijñānameva 
pūrvatarajñānajasaṃskārasahāyamubhayavādisiddhaṃ 
nīlapītādijñānānāmiva asyāḥ kāraṇamasti- iti kuto 
visadṛśatarādṛṣṭahetvantarasiddhiḥ | taduktam-

yasmin sati bhavatyeva yattato'nyasya kalpane | 
taddhetutvena sarvatra hetūnāmanavasthitiḥ ||

iti na nīlādijñānānāmiva asyāḥ kāryatvāsiddheḥ, icchā hi 
pūrvānubhūtasukhasādhanatvādyanusandhānasāmarthyasiddhatatsamāna-
kartṛtvajñānasahabhāvinī- iti jñātrantarebhya iva 
śarīravijñānāntarādibhyo'pi kāryatvena vyāvartamānā viśiṣṭaṃ 
jñātāraṃ sthiramanumāpayati- ityātmasiddhiḥ | na hi ya evāhaṃ draṣṭā 
sa evaiṣṭā ityetadanyatropapadyate, yato na asiddhatvāt, | na hi 
tatsamānakartṛtvajñānasahabhāvitvamasyāḥ siddham, tatsiddhatve hi 
jñānaviṣayatvāt pratyakṣa evātmoktaḥ syāt- iti 
nityānumeyatvābhyupagamavirodhaḥ | nanvata eva tadapi sarvātmanāṃ 
sukhasādhanatvādyanusandhānasāmarthyataḥ sādhyate- ityuktam | kimidaṃ 
sāmarthyam, yena tasya siddhiḥ, anyathānupapattireva, sā kasya, na 
tāvadicchāyāḥ, anusandhānajñānata evāsyā samutpatteḥ, atha 
tasyaivānupapattiḥ,- iti na, tasyāpi pūrvānubhavasaṃskārata eva 
bhavadbhirapi utpādābhyupagamāt, tarhi sa eva saṃskāro nityena dharmiṇā 
vinā nopapadyate- iti tatastatsiddhiḥ, na anityānāmevonmattabījānāṃ 
lākṣādisaṃskārastatpuṣpāruṇimādinā siddhaḥ- iti vijñānasantatāveva 
kramavatyāṃ sa sidhyati nānyatrātyantāsiddheḥ- iti na vidmaḥ, 
prayogāḍambaravyatirekeṇa kasyānyasya siddhiryena tadapi 
hetuviśeṣaṇabhūtaṃ jñānaṃ sādhyate | etena vaiśeṣikoktamapi 
prāṇāpānanimeṣonmeṣajīvanamanogatīndriyāntaravikāraiḥ 
smṛtyādibhiścātmānumānaṃ pratyuktam | tathā hi tiryaggatisvabhāvasya 
vāyorūrdhvādhaḥpreraṇaṃ nopapadyate bhastrādhmāteva kaścit kalpyaḥ 
| evaṃ nimeṣonmeṣādīnāṃ dehāvayavāśritānāmapi 
yantrapuruṣavatprayatnayukto'dhiṣṭhātā jīvanasyāpi 
dharmādharmakāryatayā tadguṇāśrayabhūtaḥ, manaso'pi acaitanyād 
rathāderiva prayatnavān prerako dāḍimādyamblatarasaphaladarśanena

 dantodakātmakarasanendriya vikārasyānekagavākṣavat 
sarvendriyārthānusandhātā kaścit kalpyaḥ, tacca vijñānameva 
pravāhātmakaṃ saṃskārādivaśataḥ 
pūrvoktāśeṣārthakriyānirvartakamubhayavādisiddhamasti- iti kuto'nyaḥ 
sidhyati | yadapi icchādīnāmacākṣuṣapratyakṣatvādrasādivadguṇatve sati 
ayāvaddravyabhāvitvāccharīrādiviśeṣaguṇapratiṣedhena 
pāriśeṣyādātmano guṇino'numānaṃ tadapi 
dṛṣṭāntāsiddhervyāptyabhāvādanaikāntikam, nahi rasādīnāṃ 
guṇatvamasmākaṃ sāṃkhyānāmapi vā prasiddhaṃ 
rūparasādisamudāyavyatirekeṇānyasya 
kasyacidāmraphalāderdharmiṇo'nupalambhāt, ata evāmrasya rasaḥ, iti 
bhedavyapadeśaḥ samudāyaikadeśatvakhyāpanāya vanasya dhavaḥ 
śobhanaḥ itivadupadyata eva | kasya tarhi iyamicchā iṣyamāṇasya karmaṇo 
grāmādereva | atha hetoḥ kasya iti, ucyate yato'nantaraṃ dṛśyate tasya 
viśiṣṭasyaiva jñānasya pūrvatarajñānajasaṃskāraparipākātmanaḥ | 
kathaṃ tarhi devadattasyecchā- ityādiko'tra kartṛtvavyavahāro laukikaḥ, 
kutarkadarśanābhyāsamūlopahata eva vitastāyāḥ pravāhaḥ itivat vā 
viśiṣṭajñānapravāhasambandhitāpradarśanārtho yukta eva | 
ātmavādināmapi vā devadattasya svātmaivātra pramāṇamiti kathamasau 
vyavahāraḥ, pramāṇenānupapadyamānaḥ kalpita eveti cet kṛtaṃ 
vyavahāreṇa, pramāṇameva hi satyetaratvavyavasthāpanāyānusaraṇīyaṃ 
taccetaratrāpi samānamiti jñānameva viśiṣṭamicchā, na tu guṇaḥ kasyacit 
| yadyapi sāṃkhyaiḥ saṃhatānāṃ pārārthyaṃ śayanādīnāmiva 
kāryakaraṇānāṃ parasiddhāvanumānamupanyastaṃ tadapi 
ubhayavādisiddhaṃ vijñānameva sādhayatīti siddhasādhanam | nanu 
vijñānasyānityatvāt krameṇa saṃhatatvamiti tato'pi anyo'saṃhataḥ 
paro'numeyaḥ | satyam, yadi tathābhūtena vyāptiḥ siddhā bhavet, 
saṃhatānāṃ hi dṛṣṭānte saṃhataśarīrādiviṣayameva parārthyaṃ 
siddhamiti jñānameva tathābhūtaṃ sidhyati, ante'pi 
anavasthāparihārārthaṃ kasyacit 
tathābhūtasyaivābhyupagamānnānyo'tathābhūtaḥ, nahi 
yaddṛṣṭāntāparidṛṣṭamasiddhavyāptikaṃ vastu tadddhetuḥ svaśaktyā 
sādhayituṃ śaknoti, jñāpako'yaṃ na kārako yataḥ na ca 
jñānamasmākamasiddhamiti vācyam, arthaprakāśo hyayaṃ 
sakalalokaprasiddho'nubhūyata eva, na 
cāsāvarthadharmo'rthavailakṣaṇyenādhyātmamanubhavād arthasya 
sarvān pratyaviśeṣāt sarvapratyakṣatāprasaṅgācca nāpi puṃsvabhāvaḥ 
tasyāsiddheḥ siddhau cāprakāśātmano vyatirekābhāvāt tasyāpi 
aprakāśarūpatvamityāyātamāndhyamaśeṣasya jagataḥ | taduktam-

 apratyakṣasyopalabhyasya nārthadṛṣṭiḥ prasidhyati | iti |

pradīpavat svaraprakāśaikasvabhāvatvena viśiṣṭārthaprakāśakaṃ 
vijñānamanubhavasiddhaṃ nāpahnotuṃ śakyamiti | tadidamuktam-

jñānārthau saha rūpadīpatulayā jātau vibhāṣāmate | iti |

ātmanā bhoktrādirūpeṇānyena śūnyāḥ skandhāḥ-
ityātmaśūnyavādinaḥ ||

atra ācārya āha-

nābhāvaḥ śakyate vaktuṃ pratyaye sarvasākṣiṇi |
śūnyatā tena sarveṣāṃ bhāvānāṃ bodhabādhitā || 5 ||

yadyevaṃ sarvārthasākṣātkaraṇapravṛtte jñānātmani 
grāhakasvarūpe pratyakṣasiddhe sati abhāvo nātmanaḥ śakyaḥ 
pratipādayitumanubhavasiddhatvāt, bhoktṛtvaṃ hi jñātṛtvamucyate tadeva 
ca pāramārthikamātmano rūpaṃ tacca bhavadbhirapi 
anubhavasiddhamityuktam | ataḥ kimanyat sādhyamiti | nanu pratyarthaṃ 
pratikṣaṇaṃ ca bhinnamevedaṃ grāhakarūpaṃ vijñānamasmābhiḥ 
pratipāditam na tu ātmābhidhānaḥ kaścit sarvārthasākṣibhūto'rthaḥ, 
yadi ca tadevāsau � iti ucyate bhavatu nāmabhedaḥ, paraṃ sarveṇa tu 
ātmavādinā sthirarūpo'sau darśanīyaḥ � iti cet, ucyate nātra 
bhavadabhyupagamo'ṅgatvenāsmābhiruktaḥ, na hi pratidarśanaṃ 
vyavasthāpakānāṃ sarvapramātṛṇāmanubhavabhedaḥ sambhavati, tasya 
svabhāvasiddhatvāt | yadāhuḥ-

jñānaṃ pratyabhilāpaṃ ca sadṛśau bālapaṇḍitau | iti |

	tat sa evāyaṃ sakalalokaprasiddhaḥ svānubhavo nirūpyatām | kiṃ 
pratyarthaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ cāpūrvo'pūrvaḥ 
pūrvottarābhyāmanubhavābhyāṃ bhinnakṣaṇamātrarūpāvabhāso 
grāhakaḥ prakāśata uta tatrāyaṃ sthirarūpaḥ prakāśaḥ 
sarvadaivābhinna iti | grāhyopādhibhede'pi anāsāditasvātmabhedaḥ 
kālatraye'pi tiraskṛtasvagataprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsābhāvo 
nānāvidhapramāṇādyanekacittavṛttyudayavyayasaṃvedane'pi 
akampitatadgrāhakasthairyavedano vṛttyantarāleṣvapi aviluptajyotiḥ 
suṣuptādāvapi akhaṇḍitasvasaṃvit satatameva svaprakāśatvena gamyatvād 
ātmapadapratipādyaḥ pratipuruṣaṃ svasaṃvedanasiddhaḥ, iti kimatrānyena 
sādhanena | tāmeva cābhinnāmanapāyinīṃ cātmasaṃvidamāśritya 
sarvaiḥ kālāntaraphalāni karmāṇyārabhyante, kṣaṇikatve tu tasyāḥ 
sarvavyavahārapratyastamayaḥ 

 sarvānubhavānāṃ kṣaṇamātravedanottarakālaṃ dhvaṃsatāṃ 
kṣaṇāntare sambhavāt kaḥ pravartate kutra kimarthaṃ vā, yataḥ nahi 
kṣaṇātmavedinaḥ kṣaṇāntare nāhaṃ na mama iti paśyataḥ pravṛttiḥ 
ghaṭate-iti nirīhaṃ svarūpamātraprakāśaniṣṭhaṃ 
heyopādeyabuddhivikalamamithyājñānaṃ 
vicārabodhādyanekajñānaśūnyaṃ jagadetadbhavet-iti 
sarvānubhavavirodhaḥ sthiragrāhakaprakāśapūrvakatvādevamāderiti | 
syādetat, astyayamekarūpasthiragrāhakaprakāśo'napahnavanīya eva, sa 
punarna svasaṃvedyo'pi tu grāhakakṣaṇapravāha evānubhūyamāne 
tatsādṛśyadarśanabhrāntairvikalpairadhyāropito'mbhaḥ 
pravāhasyevaikyamiti bhrānta eva, ata evāsyātmagrahatvāt 
sarvānarthamūlatvenopaśamāya bhagavatā sugatena nairātmyaviṣayaḥ 
pratipakṣabhāvanākhyo yatnaḥ prārabdhaḥ | yadāhuḥ-

mithyādhyāropahānārthaṃ yatno'satyapi bhoktari | iti |

	tadayuktam, viṣayavailakṣaṇyena karmatayādhyātmamasya 
saṃvedanāt, yadi hi ayamāropitaḥ syāt tadāropakād grāhakarūpād 
bhedena viṣayavadbhāseta devadattabodhaḥ sthira itivat, na 
caivamayamanubhavo'pi tu viṣayaprakāśakatvenāntargrāhakasvabhāvastat-
samāropakābhimattadgrahītṛrūpatvena tadāpi sthira eva saṃvedyate, 
tasyāpi hi svataḥ kṣaṇamātrarūpatvenāpratibhāsanāt tadapratibhāsitve hi 
āropānupapatteḥ, āropasya pūrvāparaparāmarśarūpatvena 
sthirabodhanivartyatvāt, na ca kṣaṇātmano'pi 
yugapatpūrvāparakālayuktadīrghaviṣayatvataḥ 
samāropakatvamayojanātmakatvenāvikalpakatvāt 
alātacakrādipratibhāsavat, ata eva yugapacchabdārthaviṣayatve'pi 
yogijñānamavikalpakamityuktaṃ bhavadbhiḥ | yojitaviṣayaṃ tat-iti cet, 
sarveṣāṃ kṣaṇikatvena yojanānupapatterna kiñcidetat, ata evānekasyāpi 
kramabhāvino vikalpakṣaṇasyānāropakatvam, 
ityāropāsambhavādastaṃgatāvikalpaḥ | na ca grāhakātmā grāhyīkartuṃ 
śaktyate yena svātmanyadhyāropeṇa sthairyamāropitamityucyate | 
svātmanyavikalpako vikalpo yataḥ, ata evāsyāhaṃpratyayaviṣayatāpi na 
sambhavati, api tu ahaṃ pratyayaprakāśarūpataiva tadāpi tena rūpeṇa 
sthiratayaiva bhāsanāt, nahi kālatraye'pi grāhakātmano dhvaṃsaḥ 
saṃvedyate-ityuktaṃ | yasya hi prāgabhāvaḥ sa utpanna ucyate, asya tu 
pradhvaṃsaḥ sa naṣṭaḥ, yasya punaḥ pūrvottarayoḥ koṭyornāsti 
abhāvasaṃvit sa pratikṣaṇamutpanno niruddho vā- iti na śakyate 

 vaktum | na cāsvasaṃvedyaḥ saṃviddharmo bhavati-iti vakṣyāmaḥ tat 
sthūlasūkṣmayoriva sthirakṣaṇikayoḥ parasparaviruddharūpatvāt yathā 
vidyudādau kṣaṇikatvaṃ pramāṇasiddhaṃ sadakṣaṇikatvaṃ vyāvartayati 
evaṃ grāhakātmanyapi āropāsambhavāt 
sthairyamavabhāsamānamasaṃśayaṃ kṣaṇikatvaṃ vyāvartayati-iti yuktam 
| na ca svasaṃvedanasya tathātve bādhaḥ sambhavati, bādhakābhimatasyāpi 
tenaiva sthirātmanā svasaṃvedanāt, anyathā bādhakatvāyogād 
bhrāntyabhāvācca, bhrāntamapi hi vijñānaṃ sarvamālambane bhrāntaṃ 
na svātmani, iti darśayiṣyāmaḥ | na ca pramāṇasiddhasya 
bādhakamantareṇa anyathābhyupagamo matimāvarjayati vipaścitām | 
sthirasyārthakriyānutpattireva bādhakamiti cet, na, tatraiva tasyā 
samutpatteḥ-iti vakṣyāmaḥ | tadevaṃ 
sarvadaikarūpasthiragrāhakaprakāśātmā nāropita eva 
sarvārthasākṣitayā yena pratipuruṣaṃ svasaṃvedana siddhaḥ, tena 
kāraṇātmaśūnyāḥ skandhāḥ-iti pakṣaḥ pratyakṣanirākṛta eva | 
tadiyatā pratijñāsūtrāt jñātā iti padaṃ vyākhyātam || 5 ||

	yadyevaṃ grāhakātmanaḥ sarvārthān pratyaviśeṣāt nīlasyeyaṃ 
saṃvit na pītasya iti pratyarthaṃ saṃvidbhedāsiddhiḥ | nahi 
dvāvupalambhau sta eko'rthasyāparaścātmanaḥ, ātmopalambhātmake ca  
viṣayopalambhe sati ātmano'viśeṣāt tasyāpi aviśeṣaḥ, 
tataścārthaviśeṣasyāpi asiddheranubhavavirodhaḥ, 
nanvarthavattatprakāśikābhinnaiva saṃvit saṃvedyate 
yadyevamātmāntarāsaṃvedanānnairātmyameva yaduktam-

	nityaṃ pramāṇaṃ naivāsti vastubhedavyavasthiteḥ | iti | nanvātmāpi 
saṃvedyata eva, tadayuktam, ekamevedaṃ saṃvidrūpaṃ 
harṣaviṣādādyanekākāravivartaṃ paśyāmaḥ, yataḥ tat cet saṃvit 
ko'nya ātmā, atha ātmaivāsau kānyā saṃvittiḥ nahi pratidarśanaṃ 
saṃvidrūpamanyathā-iti yujyate vaktuṃ yena jaiminīyānāmetadubhaya-
rūpamityucyeta, tasya nīlapītādivadvastuśaktyaiva siddhiḥ 
pratipuruṣamanyathātvāyogāt | satyam, ekamevedaṃ saṃvidrūpaṃ asya tu 
nīlamahaṃ vedmi iti parāmarśabalād arthavadātmāpi saṃvedyaḥ-iti 
brūmaḥ | ata eva pratyayāntargatatvenāhamaṃśaparāmarśāt 
sapratyabhijñaṃ pratyakṣamevātmasiddhau pramāṇamiti 

 prābhākarāḥ | yadyevam, palāśānāṃ vanaṃ māṣāṇāṃ rāśiḥ 
ityādipratyayabalāt palāśamāṣādivyatiriktasyāpi 
vanarāśyāderarthāntarasyādhyakṣasaṃvedyatvataḥ sattvaprasaṅgaḥ | na, 
tadvyatiriktasyānyasya tenāsaṃvedanāt mithyaiṣa parāmarśaḥ-iti cet, tarhi 
atrānaikāntikatvāt kṛtaṃ parāmarśanena | saṃvedyataiva nirūpyatām, 
kimarthavadātmano'sau, atha vanādivat kalpitaiveti | na 
cārthatadgrāhakasaṃvidveṣavyatirekeṇānyaḥ kaścidātmābhidhānaḥ 
saṃvedyate-iti kalpitaiva sā, saṃvedyatvaṃ ca nātmā 
saṃvidviṣayatvāddhaṭādivat, pramātṛtayaiva saṃvedanādadoṣaḥ, iti cet, 
na itaretaravirodhāt, prakāśakatvaṃ hi pramātṛtvaṃ na prakāśyatā, 
prakāśyatve ca prakāśakatānupapattiḥ tatprakāśakasyaivātmavādastu 
pramārthatastasya saṃvillakṣaṇāt phalādarūpāntaratvāt � iti cet, tarhi 
pratikarma tasya bhedānnairātmyamabhede cārthabhedāsiddhirityuktam | 
etena pratyarthamātmano viśeṣābhāve'pi tatsamavetaṃ jñānaṃ 
bhinnameva prakāśakamutapadyate tato na virodhaḥ-iti 
naiyāyikavaiśeṣikapakṣaḥ pratikṣiptaḥ ||

	jñānasya tasmādabhede pratyarthabhedānnairātmyameva, bhede tu 
prakāśyaprakāśakatvāt jñānamevāstu kimantargaḍunānyena, kiṃ 
kurmaḥ tathāpi anumānāt sudhyatyeva, na uktatvāt | nanvātmā 
svayamaparokṣatayā grahītṛtvenāyaṃ pratyarthamalabdhabhedaḥ 
prasphuratyeva, tadbalāttu arthaprakaṭatākhyo dharmaḥ pratyarthaṃ bhinna 
evotpadyate, tato'rthabhedasiddheradoṣaḥ-iti kaumārilāḥ | tadapi ayuktam, 
arthasya sarvān pratyaviśeṣāt sarvātmaprakaṭatā syāt-iti sarvasya 
sarvajñatāprasaṅgaḥ | na, viśiṣṭātmajanyatvāt prāpyakarmavadasya � iti 
cet tadayuktam, tadaprakāśe tasyāpyasiddheḥ, prakāśe tu 
grāmaprāptivadubhayaniṣṭhatā-iti saṃvitpakṣa eva, sa ca nirākṛtaḥ, na 
arthadharmatve'pi svatvasutatvādivad ulūkādiparihāreṇa 
ālokajanitārthaprakaṭādivadvā viśiṭātmasambandhī asau dharmastatra 
bhaviṣyati tato nātiprasaṅgaḥ-ityuktam | api cāsāvarthasya prakāśāmtako 
vā syāt prakāśaviṣayarūpo vā, pūrvasmin kalpe'rthaḥ svata eva tadā 
prakāśet-iti ātmanaḥ prāgvadaprakāśanādasiddha eva syāt | nanvadhunā 
prakāśamāno'rthaḥ kathamasiddhaḥ | satyam, ātmāntaravat svata iti 
ātmāntarasyāsiddha eva | nanu tadbalāt prakāśata iti | kimataḥ, tasya tu na 

 prakāśate-iti sarvadā parokṣa eva | atha dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ tatra tu arthasya 
kaḥ prakāśaḥ-iti vācyam | nanvātmaprakāśa eva, satyam, tasya 
tvaviśeṣāt arthaviśeṣavyavasthā nopapadyate-ityatraiṣa doṣaḥ sthita eva | 
nanvarthāhitākāraviśeṣasaṃvedanādātmano'rthaviśeṣavyavasthāpa-
katvam, ato naiṣa doṣaḥ, iti pañcāṅgādhikaraṇāḥ | yadāhuḥ-

	tadbhogyapratibimbasya grahaṇaṃ bhoga ātmani | iti | proktaṃ ca 
sautrāntikairapi-

arthena ghaṭayatyenāṃ nahi muktvārtharūpatām |
tasmātprameyādhigateḥ sādhanaṃ meyarūpatā || iti |

	atrāpi kimātmaprakāśād arthapratibimbaprakāśo'nyastadātmaka 
eva vā, anyatve'rthavat prokta eva doṣo'nanyatve tu grāhyagrāhakavadasya 
paramārthato'sambhavena svapnādipratyayavat kalpitatvāt 
tato'rthasyāvyavasthiteḥ kādācitkapratyayānupapatteḥ svapnādāviva 
vāsanādyupapatterarthānumitau vyabhicārāt, arthaśūnyatvenāpi 
krameṇānekākāraprakāśānyathānupapattyā nairātmyameva yaduktam-

avibhāgo'pi buddhyātmā viparyāsitadarśanaiḥ |
grāhyagrāhakasaṃvittirbhedavāniva lakṣyate || iti |

atrāpyācārya āha-

arthasyāpi na cābhāvo yatoya'manubhūyate |

	na kevalamātmaśūnyatāpakṣa pratyakṣanirākṛto 
yāvadarthaśūnyatāpakṣo'pi, ityapiśabdārthaḥ | tadāha 
yato'yamartho'nubhūyate anubhavena bahirdeśasambandhitayā prakāśyate-
iti grāhyagrāhakadvayaprakāśo'nubhavasiddha ityarthaḥ | nahi prakāśya 
eva prakāśakātmā tasya tatprakāśakatvena pratyagrūpatayā tato 
bhedenāvabhāsanāt-ityuktam, ātmaparabhāvānupapatteḥ 
sarvavyavahārābhāvācca | satyam, paramārthato'satya evāyaṃ 
grāhyagrāhakavyavahāro vaikalpikaḥ-iti cet, kuta etat, pūrvoktāt bādhakāt 
pramāṇāt-iti cet na, 
tasyāpyevamasiddherdvaitanibandhanatvādbādhyabādhakabhāvasya, 

 tadapi paramārthataḥ svātmaviṣayameva,  
tato'rthādbhedavyavacchedakamiti cet, satyaṃ yadi tathānubhūyeta, 
bhinnaviṣayameva tvanubhūyate, abhede vā prāmāṇyābhāvo 
vikalpādijñānānāmapi bāhyāsambhavena svātmaviṣayatvāt | satyam, 
nīlamahaṃ vedmi iti dvayapratibhāsāttu teṣāmapramāṇatā, tarhi 
pratibhāsamānatvāddvayamadvayavat satyameva, asatyatve hi 
pratibhāsānupapattiḥ, asato 
bhāsanāyogānnājñānātmano'vabhāsanamupapadyate- iti hi bhavatāṃ 
kṛtāntaḥ | satyam, svapratibhāsenārtho'rthādhyavasāyena 
pravṛttestasyāprāmāṇyamiti ce, astu artha evaitat, svapratibhāse tu asya 
grāhyātmani grāhakātmavat prakāśarūpatayā 
pramāṇarūpataivetyadvaitānupapattiḥ | na, tasyāpyadvayaviṣayatvāt 
paramārthata iti cet, tarhi nirvikalpādaviśeṣaḥ- iti vikalpābhāvāt 
saṃsārābhāvaḥ sarvo vikalpaḥ saṃsāraḥ ityabhyupagamāt | na 
svātmanyapyadvaye dvayādhyavasāyena pravṛttervikalpatā- iti cet, na 
svātmanyasyā vikalpatvāt vikalpatve'pi 
dvayasyādhyavaseyatvājjñānarūpataiva, iti kathamasatyatā | 
tato'rthakriyānavāpteḥ, -iti cet, duḥśikṣitametadbāhyārthavādibhyaḥ | 
vijñaptimātrasatyatve tvavabodhavyatirekeṇa kānyārthakriyā- iti 
svapnadvicandrādīnāmapi nāsatyatā nahi pratibhātamapratibhātaṃ 
bhavati, iti nyāyāt | avabodhāntarajananamiti cet, tadapi teṣāmasti iti 
nāsatyatā | vāsanāsthairyānubandhena prābandhikī sthitiḥ, iti cet, tarhi 
anādivāsanānubandhitvādvikalpajñānapratibhāsātmanaḥ 
prabandhapravṛttasya dvayasyaiva satyatā na yuṣmatparikalpitādvayasyeti na 
viruddhatvam | nanu pūrvāparaparāmarśātmakatvenāsannihitārtha-
bhedāvabhāsādvikalpānāmasatyārthatā, iti cet, yadi asannihito'satyatvāt 
kathaṃ bhāseta, bhāsate cedadvayavat satya eva | bodhātmakatvameva 
bhavatāṃ satvam, yato na bāhyārthavādināmiva 
tato'nyadarthakriyākṣamatvamityevaṃprakāramatrocyamānamasādhanaṃ 
bāhyārthavādināmevāsya sādhanatvāt | grāhyagāhakayoḥ 
paraniṣṭhatvāt svarūpānavasthiterasatyatvamiti cet, kāryakāraṇayorvā 
kathaṃ satyatvam, tayorapi neti cet, tarhi 
bhāvanābhyāsādihetvabhāvādadvayavijñaptisantaterapi 
deśakālaprakṛtiniyamāyogaḥ iti sarvamasamañjasam | atha parāpekṣā 
vyavahāryasya viśiṣṭasyaiva rūpasya tayoravasthānādadoṣaḥ-iti, 
taditaratrāpi samānamiti nāsatyatā, bāhyārthavādibhirasatyārthatvena 
vikalpasyābhyupagamāditi cet, avyabhicārī hetustadabhyupagamasya 
bhavadbhirbādhitatvāt | atrābādhane'pi tata evādvaitāsiddhirdvayasyaiva 
tatra pratibhāsanādvayasyābhidhānamātramevaṃrūpatvādvikalpasya 

 na tu pratibhāso yena satyatāprasaṅgaḥ- ityabhinavaśākyāḥ | tadayuktam, 
manorājyādyavasthāsu 
jvaladbhāsurāderviklpyākārasyāgnyādivikalpeṣvanubhavāt, na 
cānubhavamapahnutyavyavasthā vyavasthātvamāsādayati- ityuktam | 
grāhyākārāvabhāso'sau- iti cet, na, tasya vikalpyākārābhedāt bhede 
vāgacchatastṛṇādijñānākārāṇāmiva vjñānāvyatirekeṇa 
svasaṃvedyatayā vyavasāyaviṣayatvābhāvāt | yadāhuḥ-

yairyanniścīyate rūpaṃ tatteṣāṃ viṣayaḥ katham | iti |

	nanu vikalpyākārasyātmatvenāsatyatayā bāhyatvena ca 
vyavasthānupapatterasambhavaḥ, iti cet yadyevaṃ vyavasthānupapattirevāstu 
nāsambhavo'nubhūyamānatvāt, bhaved vā kācidvyavasthā, iti 
tadrūpataiva | yaduktaṃ jaratsaugataiḥ-

na vikalpānuviddhasya spaṣṭārthapratibhāsitā | iti |
vikalpo'vastunirbhāsādvisaṃvādādupaplavaḥ |
pratyakṣābhaḥ �������������- || iti ca |

	tato vikalpyasyāpi pratibhāsanāt pratibhāsamānatayaiva 
sattvāddvayameva satyamityadvaitāsiddhiḥ | tarhi nirvikalpe tat setsyati, na 
tasyapi tathaivopalambhāt, ityuktam | yadāhuḥ śabdakaṇikādau 
vitatamatayaḥ �

antastattvabahistattvarūpaṃ yugalakaṃ tu tat |
pratipattā bhavetkiñcitkiñcitkaścittu vastutaḥ || iti |

	tataśca avibhāgo'pi buddhyātmā | ityetadapyayuktameva | 
nanvevamapi grāhyagrāhakātmanā dvayarūpaṃ vijñānameva siddhyati na 
grāhyasyārthatā | yadāha-

nānyo'nubhāvyo buddhyāsti tasyāḥ nānubhavo'paraḥ |
grāhyagrāhakavaidhuryātsvayaṃ saiva prakāśate || iti |

	na jñānavailakṣaṇyenārthatayā 
viśiṣṭārthakriyāsādhanatvenārthyamānatayā ca tasya pratibhāsanāt, 
tatprāptāveva cārthakriyāsiddhestadaprāptau tvayathārthatvaṃ 
śuktikārajatādijñānānāmiti kathamasya nārthatā, tadabhyupagame tu 
dānāderhisātmanaśca dharmādharmātmanaḥ karmaṇo'siddherlokāyata 
evopāsyo bhavatām | jñānākāraviṣayatvādevamāderadoṣaḥ, iti cet, tarhi 
manorājyādau bahutarāṇyarthāharaṇadānādīni 

 śubhāni śatruvadhādīni ca hiṃsātmakāni karmāṇi siddhāni syuḥ-iti, ko 
hi nāma bahutarakleśasādhyeṣu sevākṛṣyādiṣu gajaturagaśastrādiṣu 
sādhaneṣu pravarteta- ityanubhavavirodhaḥ | tānyapi 
viśiṣṭajñānapratibhāsātmakānyeveti cet, na, arthābhāveva 
tadvaiśiṣṭyāsiddheḥ na kiñcidetat, itaḥ parasparopakāraśca dharmo 
jinasyāpyevaṃ na sambhavati parāsaṃvedanāt | sarvajñatvādasya naiṣa 
doṣaḥ, iti cet, svapnādāvivāsyāpi svākāramātrasaṃvedanāt 
kiñcijjñataivāsmin pakṣe na sambhavati kutaḥ sarvajñatā | na 
adhigatipratyayatvena sarvasya vedanāt-iti cet, ko'yamadhigatipratyayo nāma 
sannihitamātrataiva, sannihito'trārtho na vā, iti jātyandheneva kathaṃ tena 
gamyate | na caitadasyābhiḥ śakyamavasthāpayitum | yadāhuḥ 
śākyapravarāḥ bhagavatāṃ tu sarvārthapratipattiḥ acintyā iti | 
atyalpamidamucyate sarvamacintyaṃ hyaviduṣāṃ viduṣa evātrādhikārāt | 
ākāradvāreṇa vetti, iti cet, ko'yaṃ dvāradvāribhāvastasya 
svākāramātravedanataḥ 
svabhāvaviprakṛṣṭeṣvivāsmadādīnāmartheṣvanutpannapratipattitvāt | 
vastuto'rthajanyatvāt tatpratibhāsasya tasya ca yathārthavedanādasti 
dvāradvāribhāvaḥ- iti cet, tarhi astyarthānubhavo na sākṣād api tu 
evamarthānumāneneti cet, na tasyāpi jñānatvāt svākāramātravedanena 
tadviṣayatvānupapattiḥ | tarhi nirmalatvāt svajñānaśaktyaiva sarvaṃ 
bāhyaṃ vastvasau jānātīti cet 
yadyevamanirmalatvādanyeṣāmasarvārthaviṣayā tadvadeva anirākārā sā 
bhaviṣyatīti nārthābhāvaḥ | astu vaibhāṣikamatasyaiva mukhyatvāt, iti cet, 
tataśca nānyo'nubhāvyo buddhyāsti ityetadayuktamiti śūnyavādanirāsaḥ 
| nanu bhavatāmapi arthavedanasyātmavedanādabhedāt yasya ca pratyarthaṃ 
bhedābhāvādarthānubhavo nopapadyate, iti proktanayenaiṣa prasaṅga 
ityuktaṃ na sannihitārthaprakāśakatvāt sannihitārthaprakāśakatvaṃ hi 
ātmanaḥ svabhāvaḥ pradīpāderiva tathānubhavāt siddhaḥ | tathā hi yo 
yaḥ sannihito'rthastaṃ taṃ svaśaktyaiva prakāśayannayamanubhūyate- iti 
kuto'rthaviśeṣāsiddhereṣa prasaṅga iti | nanu kathametadupapadyata iti 
tadayuktam, na hi dṛṣṭe'nupapannaṃ nāma, nahi agnirdahati nākāśam 
ityatra tathā darśanādanyadupapattikāraṇaṃ śakyaṃ vaktuṃ | atha 
kastasya sannihitenārthenopakāraḥ kṛtaḥ, na kaścit, kathaṃ tarhi tameva 
prakāśayati, tathā svabhāvatvādityuktam, upakāre'pi tadasvabhāvasya 
prakāśakatvādṛṣṭeḥ | nanu tadasannidhau tarhi tadaprakāśaprakāśanāt sa 
svabhāvo'syāsti � iti kuta tatsvarūpābhedāt, 

 svarūpaṃ hi asyārthaprakāśātmakatvenāvibhinnarūpaṃ sarvadā 
vikalpātītaṃ prakāśaka ityuktam, tarhi kathaṃ na prakāśatyartham, 
tasyāsannidhānāt, sannihitaprakāśako hi kathamasannihitaṃ prakāśayet 
kastarhi nīlaprakāśāt pītaprakāśasya bhedaḥ, na kaścit yugapatprakāśa 
iva tatra hi tayorbhede viśeṣābhāvāt tadavayavabhedenāpi bhedaḥ- iti 
pratiparamāṇubhedataścitrapṭādipratibhāsābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | na ca 
vikalpaghaṭitaṃ taditi vācyam, uddhāṭitanetrasya jhaṭityeva yugapat 
tadavabhāsanāt tadānīṃ ca vikalpāsambhavādasaṃvedanācca | yaduktam-

na cemāḥ kalpanā asaṃviditā evodayante vyayante vā yena 
satyo'pyanulakṣitāḥ syuḥ | iti |

	vikalpajñānasyāpyekatvāt kathamanekātmakaścitrāvabhāsaḥ, 
tadyathā tatra nīlapītādyanekaprakāśyabhede'pi anubhūyamānasyaikasya 
jñānātmano na bhedaḥ, na ca tadabhedāt tasya 
nīlapītāderarthabhedasyāsiddhiḥ, 
ekasyānekaprakāśanaśaktisiddherbhavadbhirapīṣtatvāt tathā 
kramaprakāśe'pi anubhavasyaikasyaiva 
prakāśātmano'nekaprakāśanaśaktiyogāt 
kramavyavasthitānekaprakāśatvamanubhavasiddhameṣṭavyam | na ca 
tadabhedādarthabhedāsiddhidoṣaḥ, na hi dikkramāvabhāsāt 
kālakramāvabhāsasya prakāśātmani kaścit bhedo'nubhūyata iti | nanu 
dikkramaprakāśe prakāśātmano bhedāsaṃvedanāt 
pradīpādervartidāhatailakṣapaṇaprakāśasvajñānajananādivadekasyā-
neka kāryakartṛtvena virodhābhāvāt śaktīnāṃ samuccaye na virodhaḥ | 
kālakrame tu prakāśaśaktīnāṃ parasparābhāvarūpatvena 
ghaṭapaṭādiśaktīnāmiva bhedādvastubhedakatvam | yadāhuḥ-
	
śaktirhi bhāvābhāvābhyāṃ bhidyamānā vastvapi bhinatti, na 
punaḥ kāryabhedena | iti tadayuktamasiddhatvāt, nahi tatrāpi 
śakterbhāvābhāvabhedo'sti sarvadaikarūpasyaiva 
prakāśātmano'nāropitarūpasya saṃvedanādityuktam | na ca 
kāryābhāvācchakterabhāvaḥ nāvaśyaṃ kāraṇāni tadvanti bhavanti iti 
nyāyāt, tadatrāpi vastubhedāsiddheḥ tadvat śaktīnāṃ samuccaya eva 
yukto na virodhaḥ, api ca parokṣasya dharmiṇaḥ kāryabhedādindriyāderiva 
śaktibhedataḥ svarūpabhedo na pratyakṣasiddhābhedādasya, tasya hi 
pradīpāderivaikasyānekakāryakartṛtvena bhavadbhirapīṣṭatvāt 
pratyakṣasiddhābhedaścātmā, ityuktam | ato na tasyāpi śaktibhedād 
bhedaḥ kalpayituṃ yukta iti yugapatprakāśa

 iva na kramaprakāśe'pyarthabhedāsiddhiḥ | kathaṃ tarhi-

ghaṭajñānamiti jñānaṃ ghaṭajñānavilakṣaṇam |
ghaṭa ityapi yajjñānaṃ viṣayopanipāti tat || (vā0 pa0 3-105)

ityadilaukikajñānabhedaḥ na grāhakātmabhedāt tasya 
sarvadaikarūpābhedaprakāśātmatayā saṃvedanād api tu 
adhyavasāyabhedāt, adhyavasāya eva ca jñānaṃ 
buddhiguṇatvājjñānasya | yaduktam buddhiraṣṭaguṇā smṛtā | 
lakṣyate'dhyavasāyena | iti darśayiṣyāmaḥ | evaṃ buddhvā ityapi 
pratipadaṃ vyākhyātamiti |

	nanu yadyatsaṃvedyate tattatsaṃvidrūpameva yathā nīlādisaṃvittiḥ, 
saṃvedyate ca nīlādiḥ tasmāt tadapi saṃvidrūpamevetyasaṃvidātmano 
bāhyārthasyāsaṃvedanādarthaśūnyaṃ sarvameva vijñānamiti 
prayogāntareṇa śūnyavādinaḥ, yadāhuḥ �

saṃvedanaṃ kiṃ tādātmyāttadvadbhāsanameva tat |
idameva kimuktaṃ syātsa bāhyo'rtho'nubhūyate || iti |

ata āha-

pratyakṣā buddhireveti nirnibandhanamucyate || 6 ||
bodho bodhāntaragrāhyo neṣyate bhavatāpi yat |

	etadapyayuktaṃ sādhanadharmāsiddhatvād dṛṣṭāntasya nahi tavapi 
nīlādisaṃvidaḥ saṃvedyatvaṃ siddhaṃ kṣaṇikatvena 
saṃvidantarotpattikāle sambhavābhāvāt, na ca dṛṣṭānte 
svasādhyāsiddhasambandho hetuḥ sādhyaṃ sādhayituṃ 
śaknotyatiprasaṅgāt || 6 ||

athaitaddoṣabhayāt saṃvidaḥ saṃvidantarajananena vedyatvamucyate | 
yadāhuḥ-

bhinnakālaṃ kathaṃ grāhyamiti cet grāhyatāṃ viduḥ |
hetutvameva yuktijñā jñānākārārpaṇakṣamam || iti |

atrāpyāha-

grāhyatve'pi na vijñānaṃ sarvameva nirarthakam || 7 ||

	yadyevamanaikāntiko hetuḥ saṃvidaḥ svato'saṃvidarūpāyā api 
saṃvedyatvāt tadvadarthasyāpi saṃvedyatvaṃ bhaviṣyati yataḥ, atha 
saṃvidrūpatve sati saṃvedyatvāditi hetustarhi asiddhaḥ, nīlasya 
saṃvidrūpatvena saṃvedyatvāsiddheḥ � iti sarvameva 

 vijñānamarthaśūnyamiti naiṣa pratijñārtho bhavataḥ siddhyatīti || 7 ||

atra parābhiprāyaṃ darśayati-

athānubhavarūpatvād bodhaḥ pratyakṣa iṣyate |

nātra saṃvidantarasaṃvedyatvaṃ heturapi tu svasaṃvedyatvameva, 
tataśca na sādhanadharmāsiddhiranaikāntikatvaṃ vā doṣa iti ||

etadapi nirākaroti-

nābhāvaḥ sammukhīne'rthe tato'nubhavagocare || 8 ||

	yadyevamasiddho hetuḥ, nahi saṃvitsvabhāvatvaṃ nīlādeḥ 
saṃvedyasya siddhamapi tu saṃvedyataiveti, tato'siddhāddhetorarthe 
nīlādike saṃvedyatayedantānirdiśyamāne pratyakṣasiddhe dharmiṇi 
nābhāvo na bāhyatvanirāsaḥ sidhyatīti || 8 ||

	etadeva pratyakṣasiddhamityāha-

ayameva hi vijñeyo bhedo bodhārthayoḥ sphuṭam |
pūrvastvanubhavākāra uttarasyānubhūyate || 9 ||

	svaparaprakāśarūpatayā hi bodhātmānubhavasiddhastat 
saṃvedyatayārtho'pīti nātaḥ śūnyavādasiddhiḥ | 
nanvevamātmano'rthasya ca dvayoranubhūyamānatve ka ātmā iti na 
vidmaḥ, paśavo'pi hi yāvadātmaparabhāvaṃ pratyasammūḍhāḥ, 
so'yaṃ paśorapi paśuḥ kudarśanāparasmāragṛhītaḥ śocya eva, kathaṃ 
vā jñānajñeyayoḥ prakāśamānatve jñānameva bhāvānāṃ 
śuddho'rthaḥ | nanvartha evāhamarthajñānayorākārabhedānupalambhāt 
sādhuḥ samarthitaḥ sugatanayo yadamedhyākāro'pyevaṃ 
vineyavinetṛtayābhyupagataḥ |

nanūktam-

prabhāsuramidaṃ cittaṃ prakṛtyāgantavo malāḥ iti, tarhi 
ayamevātmaparabhāva iti nārthajñānayorākārabhedānupalambhaḥ 
siddho'pi tu bhinna evopalambhaḥ- iti | etena 
sattvadravyakārakatvādibhyo'cetanānumānamātmani pratikṣipta 
pratyakṣanirākṛtatvāt || 9 ||

punaḥ prayogāntareṇa śūnyavādinaḥ-

vastu vijñānato bhinnaṃ naiva vijñānajanmanaḥ |
pūrvatrāgṛhyamāṇatvāt

yasya yena sahopalambho niyatastasya tato nārthāntaratvaṃ yathā 
dvicandrajñānapratibhāsamānādekasmāccandramaso dvitīyasya, 
nīlajñānena sahopalambhaniyamaśca nilādeḥa, tena vinā tataḥ 
pūrvāparayoḥ saṃvedanābhāvāt, atastasyāpi tato nārthāntaratvamiti 
nīlādeḥ saṃvidrūpatvasiddhito'rthād bāhyatvanirāsaḥ sidhyati |
yadāhuḥ-

sahopalambhaniyamādabhedo nīlataddhiyoḥ | iti |

ācāryastu nīlābhāve'pi nīlaprakāśakasadbhāvasya 
pratipāditatvādasiddhametat sādhanam, atha 
bauddhādhyavasāyāpekṣayocyate tatrāpyasatye'rthe 
manorājyādyavasthāsu tasya bhāvādasiddhatayaiva iti matvā 
prameyabhedena tadbhedamabhyupagamyāha-

					ityanaikāntamucyate || 10 ||

ityevamabhyumagamya tatsādhanamanaikāntikamucyata ityarthaḥ |

kathamityāha-

cakṣurvyāpārataḥ pūrvaṃ prakāśācca na gṛhyate |
rūpaṃ loke na tattābhyāmabhinnaṃ sampratīyate || 11 ||

	cakṣuḥśabdenātropalambhaprastāvādgolakamevaitaducyate, tasya 
vyāpāro visphārāvasthā tenālokena ca sahopalambhaniyamo rūpasya, na ca 
cakṣurvyāpārarūpatvamālokarūpatvaṃ vā, ityanaikāntikatā, 
bāhyanirākaraṇāya ca bhavadbhireva prayogaḥ samuparacitaḥ-iti 
nālokāderapi vijñānarūpatvādanaikāntikaparihāro yuktaḥ, tasyāta eva 
sādhyatvenāsiddhatvāt | nanvanālokasyāpi rūpasya 
prāṇyantarairūlūkādibhirgrahaṇādālokena sahopalambho niyato nāsti, iti 
nānaikāntikatā, kathaṃ tarhi tamasā vṛtaṃ paṭeneva te'pi rūpaṃ 
paśyanti, na cāvastutvaṃ tamaso bhūcchāyātmakatvena 
bāhyārthavādibhirabhyupagamādasmadāderapi tato 
darśanapratighātasambhavācca | vastutve ca rūpāvaraṇasvabhāvaṃ yāvat 
tadanivṛttam, tāvat kuto rūpopalabdhiḥ, taccakṣuḥ 
 śaktyaiva teṣāṃ rūpaṃ prakāśata iti cet, tarhi saivāloko 
rūpaprakāśakatvāt | athāprakāśātmikā bhaviṣyatīti, cet, tarhi kathaṃ 
prakāśayati, nahi aprakāśātmanaḥ prakāśakatvaṃ tamasa ivopadyate-iti 
prakāśātmikaiva sā śaktirabhyupagantavyā, tataḥ prakāśena 
sahopalambho niyata eva rūpasya na ca prakāśarūpatetyanaikāntikataivāsya 
hetoriti | nanu cakṣurvyāpāramālokaṃ ca vinā rūpaṃ yogibhirupalabhyate-
ityatra sahopalambhaniyamābhāvāt nānaikāntikaviṣayatā | na loka ityatra 
viśeṣaṇāt, tataścāsmadādipratyakṣatve satyeṣāṃ 
sahopalambhaniyamo'styeva na cābhedaḥ- ityanaikāntika eva |

	kiṃ ca śūnyavādināṃ cirataraprarūḍho 
vihārārāmacaityādibhedo'nubhavasiddho nopapadyate, ityāha-

nīlavijñānasantānaḥ pāṇḍuraścetyanākṛtau |
ākāravati bodhe'pi niyamo nāviśeṣataḥ || 12 ||

	nīlaśuklādisantānabhedopalakṣitaḥ sarvo'yaṃ 
saritsāgaraparvatādibhedo bhinnārthakriyāsādhanatayā niścito nāhetuko 
deśakālaprakṛtiniyamayogāt | yadāhuḥ-

nityaṃ sattvamasattvaṃ vā hetoranyānapekṣaṇāt |
apekṣāto hi bhāvānāṃ kādācitkatvasambhavaḥ || iti |

	nāpi vijñānahetukastasyākāravattvenādyayāvat 
jagatyasminnasiddheḥ, ākāravati tasminnapyabhyupagamyamāne 
vijñānātmanaḥ sarvatra sarvadā cāviśeṣāt sarvaḥ sarvākāraḥ sarvatra 
sarvadā ca syāt-iti prāguktasya jagadvaicitryaniyamasyāsiddhiprasaṅgaḥ || 
12 ||

tameva prasaṅgārthamāha-

ekākāraiva vijñaptiḥ prāptāḥ vijñānavādinaḥ |

yadāhuḥ-

tadatadrūpiṇo bhāvāstadatadrūpahetujā | iti |

tato yadyenābhinnahetukaṃ tattenābhinnasvabhāvaṃ 
yathaikasmādadvayājjñānakṣaṇāddvitīyaṃ tadrūpam, yatra 
tvabhinnarūpatvaṃ nāsti tatrābhinnahetukatvamapi nāsti yathā sugatanaye 
jñānayoḥ, abhinnahetukaṃ ca vijñānavādināṃ 
parvatākārādvijñānāt saritsamudrādijñānamiti ekarūpameva syāditi ||

	nanvabhinnahetukamapi bāhyārthavādināṃ svapne parvatajñānāt 
saritsamudrādi jñānaṃ na cābhinnarūpamityanekāntaḥ, atrāha-

nīlādibhedo bodhasya vastvabhāve tu kiṃkṛtaḥ || 13 ||

	nānekānto, nahi bāhyārthavādibhisteṣāṃ vijñānamātrameva 
heturiṣyate'pi tu saṃskāradvāreṇa yathāyathaṃ bāhyānyapi vastūni, 
bhavatastu bāhyānāṃ vastūnāmabhāvādvijñānamātrasyaiva sarvatra 
hetutvamityeṣa prasaṅga uktaḥ | na, asmābhirapi vāsanābhyupagamāt 
tatkṛto'yaṃ bhedāvabhāso na jñānamātrahetuka ityaprasaṅgaḥ | 
taduktam-

jñānajo jñānahetuśca saṃskāro vāsanocyate | iti || 13 ||

atrāpyāha-

vāsanākṛta ityetat pavaneritabhāṣitam |

unmattapralapitatulyam ||

kuta ityāha-

tatkṣaṇajñānato nānyā vāsanā tava yujyate || 14 ||

	sāpi hi vāsanā na tāvadvijñānādanyā 
vijñānavyatirekeṇā'nyasya kasyacidindriyāderapi 
bhavadbhiranabhyupagamāt, api tu pūrvasmādvijñānakṣaṇāt jātaṃ 
jñānāntarakṣaṇahetubhūtaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ jñānameva vāsanā, 
ataścāviśiṣṭatvenoktameva, ato na kiñcidetat, asmākaṃ tu sthiratvād 
buddhervāsānādharmāntarāṇyanekānyapi sambhavanti, iti 
śarīrāvasthāviśeṣaṃ dhātusāmyavaiṣamyādi vā sukhaduḥkhaphalaṃ 
karma vā yathārthapradarśakam, devatāprasādaṃ vā 
viśiṣṭavāsanāparipākahetumarthāntaramapekṣya 
viśiṣṭākāratvamupapadyata eva | nanu kṣaṇikasyāpi 
jvālādestṛṇaparṇādihetujanitā pāṇḍutvalauhityādi vāsanā dṛśyata eva 
| na, tatsvarūpatvāt, svarūpaṃ hi tattṛṇajanyaṃ viśiṣṭamevāsyāḥ, 
viśiṣṭameva ca parṇādijanyaṃ na tvanavasthitāyāḥ 

kācit vāsaneti | yadyevam, nīlajñānakṣaṇasyāpi prāktanasya kasyacit 
tatsvarūpaṃ viśiṣṭamevāstu yena visadṛśaṃ parvatākāraṃ 
rasādyākāraṃ vā jñānamanantaṃ janayiṣyati- ityaprasaṅgaḥ | na, tasya 
svasadṛśakṣaṇajanakatvena dhārāvāhikaṃ jñāne'nabhyupagamāt | nanu 
kṣaṇabhedādanyadeva tannīlajñānamiti kāryabhedaḥ, tarhi 
kṣaṇabhedāviśeṣānna kaścit sadṛśaṃ kāryaṃ janayet-
ityekasantānānmano'sya śarīrādyākārasyāpi 
vijñānasyābhāvaprasaṅgāt sarvavyavahārābhāvaḥ, 
santānamūlatvādbhavatāṃ vyavahārasya | nanu 
kaścinnīlādijñānakṣaṇaḥ sadṛśaheturapi bhaviṣyati | na, tarhi 
kṣaṇabhedāt kāryabhedaḥ- iti pūrvaḥ prasaṅgaḥ | na nīlajñānātmanāpi 
bhedāt, anyadeva tajjñānaṃ sadṛśasya kṣaṇasya heturanyadeva 
visadṛśasya | kīdṛ:śaṃ kasyeti vācyam, yādṛśādyādṛśaṃ pratīyate 
tādṛśaṃ tasyeti pratītisamutpādavādinaḥ | tadime 
bhṛgupatanasthānaśvāna ivopasthitopasthitasya nimittaṃ kalpayantaḥ kutaḥ 
kiṃ bhavatīti heyopādeyasādhaneṣvanutpannabuddhayaḥ 
prekṣāvadbhirdūrataḥ parihāryāḥ, teṣāṃ hitāhitaprāptityāgāyaiva 
tatsādhanajijñāsāyāṃ pravṛtteḥ | nanu santānaviṣayāsau bhaviṣyati na, 
kṣaṇānāṃ jananāsambhavena tasyāsambhavāt kṣaṇapravāhātmakatvāt 
tasyeti sarvāniyamaparihārāyānubhūyamāno bāhyo'rthaḥ satyaḥ 
prakāśātmano bhinnaḥ prakāśyasvabhāvo'bhyupagantavyaḥ, tanna 
sahopalambhamātreṇāsyāsmādabhedaḥ, iti bodhyam ityapi pratijñāpadam, 
vyākhyātamiti || 14 ||

kiñca sahopalambhaniyamādabhede'pi nātmābhāvaḥ, ityāha-

ātmanyasattvaṃ no yuktamahaṃ pratyayagocare |

	ahaṃ pratyayasya sthiragrāhakaparāmarśarūpatvenātmaviṣayatvā-
dastyevādhyavaseyenātmanā sahopalambhaniyamaḥ, iti dvayorapi 
saṃvidrūpatvasiddheḥ satyatvamiti nairātmyābhāvaḥ | natvātmano 
vikalpātītatvenoktatvāt | mokṣakārikāsvācāryeṇa 
buddhibodhyatvanirāsācca ahaṃ pratyayagocaratvaṃ vādyasiddhameva | 
satyam, ātmādipratyayavattu kathañcid dūraviprakarṣeṇa so'pi 
vyavahārārthaṃ tadviṣayo'bhyupagamyate, iti nāsiddhiḥ | nanuṃ 
cādhyavasāyasyāvastuviṣayatvādasmākamadhyavaseyena sahābhāvo na 
siddhaḥ | yuktametat kadācit bāhyārthavādino vaktum, bhavatastu 
abodhātmano'saṃvedyatvādadhyavasāyasyāpi saṃvedyatvena 
bodharūpatayādhyavaseyasyeva sattvamiti nāsiddhistena sahabhavasyeti, tarhi 
vādyasiddho'yamahaṃpratyayābhāve'pi 
svasaṃvedanenātmopalambhābhyupagamāt | satyam, ahamiti 
tvadhyavasāyena 

sahabhāvo'dhyavaseyatātra hetuḥ sā ca tadānīṃ nāstīti tena 
sahopalambhaniyamo nāsiddhaḥ, anyā hi adhyavasāyaparāmṛśyatānyā ca 
saṃvidrūpateti buddhisaṃvitpravivekaprasaṅgena pradarśitaṃ 
svāyambhuvodyota eva | iha tvanupayogānnocyate, bhavadbhirapyuktam-

aśakyasamayo hyātmā cetanānāmananyabhāk |
teṣāmataḥ svasaṃvittiḥ nābhijalpānuṣaṅgiṇī || iti |

	ata evobhayabhedādasya nātra gṛhītagrāhitvamapi tu tāvanmātreṇa 
pramāṇatvamiti darśayiṣyāmaḥ |

	nanu gauro'haṃ kṛśo'ham ityanātmani śarīra evāhaṃ- pratyayasya 
dṛṣṭerviruddho heturityata āha-

na cānyasminnahaṃ buddhiḥ kvacidṛṣṭā tu nirguṇā || 15 ||

	na viśeṣitatvāt, gaurādibhiḥ padārthāntarairviśeṣito hi 
ahaṃpratyayo māṇavakādau siṃhādipratyayavad guṇavṛttyā śarīre 
vartate, tairaviśeṣitastu mukhyatayātmanyeva, sa eva cātra heturukto na 
saviśeṣaṇaḥ, ityaviruddha eva || 15 ||

	yadyevam, pratyayavyatirekeṇānyasya kasyācidātmanaḥ 
pratyeyasyānupalabdhervanādyekatvapratyayavadahaṃ pratyayo'pi nirviṣaya 
evetyasiddho hetuḥ, atrāpyāha-

kartṛkarmāvabhāsācca spaṣṭā dṛṣṭā kvacinmatiḥ |
ahametatprapaśyāmityataḥ siddhaṃ sphuṭaṃ dvayam || 16 ||

	satyam, syāt nirviṣayo yadyātmā sarvārthaprakāśakatayā svato 
nāvabhāset, sa tu svasaṃvedananena vikalpātīta eva sarvadā bhāsata 
ityuktam | tadviṣayaścāyaṃ proktanayenāhaṃ pratyayaḥ kvacit ahametat 
prapaśyāmi ityādau parāmarśaviśeṣe susphuṭānubhava eva dṛṣṭaḥ-iti na 
vanādyekatvādapratyayavannirviṣayaḥ, tannāsiddhiḥ prāguktasya 
sahopalambhasyeti | yadyevaṃ 
sahopalambhaniyamādātmādvaitasiddhiritīṣṭavighātaḥ | na 
tvatpakṣe'pyapratikṣepyamevātmasaṃvedanamityevaṃparatvādasya, 
paramārthatastu ata iti ahametat prapaśyāmi iti parāmarśabuddherapyasyāḥ 
sakāśādātmā sarvārtha prakāśakatvena sarvadā parāmarśanīyaḥ 
arthastu tatprakāśyatayā krameṇa 

pratīyate, ityatyantabhinnamadhyavasīyamānatvāt sphuṭametadvayaṃ 
siddhimiti na bhedo'pyayuktaḥ-iti neṣṭavighātaḥ | taduktam-

ayameva hi vijñeyo bhedo bodhārthayoḥ sphuṭam |
pūrvastvanubhavākāra uttarāścānubhūyate || iti || 16 ||

	nanvayaṃ ityāderadvayaviṣayasyāpi vimarśasya 
darśanādatrānaikāntikatā, tadayuktamityāha-

sarvameva hi vijñānaṃ kartṛkarmāvabhāsakam |

	ayaṃ ghaṭa ityādyapi vimarśavijñānaṃ na 
vimṛśyamātraviṣayameva yuktaṃ svātmano vimarśābhāvena 
paravimṛśyasyeva tadrūpatvāsiddheḥ | svātmanāpyasya 
tadānīmasaṃvedanāt na siddhiriti cet, na, saṃvedanasyāpyavimṛṣṭasya 
gacchatāṃ tṛṇādiviṣayasyeva siddheḥ | yadāhuḥ-

saṃvittiraparāmarśādvidyamānāpi vastutaḥ |
tṛṇādivittivadyātuḥ siddhaivāvidyamānavat || iti |

	tasmādakartṛka eva tenāsau vimarśanīyaḥ, ātmakartṛke ca viṣaye 
vimṛṣṭe svātmāpi tadupasarjanībhūto vimṛṣṭa eva sāmarthyādbhavati-iti 
sarvameva vimarśajñānaṃ kartṛkarmadvayaviṣayameveti nānaikāntikatā ||

	anumānenāpyetat siddhamityāha-

trayaṃ saṃsmaryate yasmāt tadahaṃ dṛṣṭavāniti || 17 ||

	ayaṃ ghaṭa ityādivimarśaviṣayā smṛtirbhavantī 
kartṛkarmakaraṇaviṣayā dṛṣṭeti karmakaraṇayoriva pūrvaṃ karturapi 
aśabdaṃ vimarśaṃ gamayatyeveti sarvo vimarśo dvayaviṣaya eveti | ata eva 
nāvastu viṣayo vimarśaḥ pratyakṣavadvastvanvayavyatirekānuvidhānāt | 
anyairapyuktam-

astihyālocanaṃ jñānaṃ prathamaṃ nirvikalpakam |
bālamūkādivijñānasadṛśaṃ śuddhavastujam ||
tataḥ paraṃ punarvastudharmairjātyādibhiryathā |
buddhyāvasīyate sāpi pratyakṣatvena sammatā || iti |

nanvasatyapi vastuni ghaṭādivimarśo manorājyādau dṛṣṭaḥ | satyam, ata eva 
tayorvastvavastuviṣayatvāt pratyakṣatadābhāsayoriva bhedaḥ | nanu 
vastuviṣayatvenābhimatasyāpi sa evāyaṃ ghaṭaḥ itvevamādeḥ 
pūrvadṛṣṭatayā vimarśasya pravṛtteḥ, adhunā ca pūrvadarśanābhāvena 
tadasambhavādavastuviṣayatvaṃ na, prāgdarśanābhāvāsiddheḥ, 
ātmaprakāśa evārthadarśanaṃ tacca sarvadāsti, ityuktam, ataḥ 
prāgdṛṣṭatāpi idānīṃ tatra vastveveti nāvastuviṣayaḥ, ghaṭādau 
kṣaṇikatvasyāsiddheriti vakṣyāmaḥ | evaṃ tarhi santamase spṛśato'pi 
ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ ityadṛṣṭānāṃ rūpādīnāmavimarśādavastuviṣayo 
mānasa eṣa smārto vikalpaḥ-iti | na, teṣāmekasāmagrīpratibaddhatvena 
viśiṣṭāt sparśalakṣaṇālliṅgādapratyakṣāṇāmapi tadānīmanumānāt | 
yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

ekasāmagryadhīnasya rūpāderasato gatiḥ |
hetudharmānumānena dhūmendhanavikāravat || iti |

	na cānumānavimarśo'pi avastuviṣayo 
vastvanvayavyatirekānuvidhānena pramāṇatvāt pratyakṣatadvimarśavat | 
taduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

arthasyāsambhave'bhāvātpratyakṣe'pi pramāṇatā |
pratibaddhasvabhāvasya taddhetutve samaṃ dvayam || iti |

	nāpi asphuṭāvabhāsatvenāsyāvastuviṣayatvaṃ 
pratyakṣasyāpyarthasya dūravartinastatsambhavato'naikāntikatvāt, 
āgamokto'pi vimarśastadāgamapraṇetṛjñānadvāreṇa vastuni 
pratibaddhatvādavañcakapitrādivākyaśrutijanitavimarśavadyathārtha eva | 
taduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

iṣṭo'yamarthaḥ śakyete jñātuṃ sātiśayo yadi | iti |

	pratyakṣānumānāgamabahiṣkṛtastu vimarśo 
manorājyādivimarśavadvastupratibandhābhāvādavastuviṣayo'stu ahametat 
prapaśyāmi iti tu vimarśaḥ saṃviditātmārthaviṣayatvāt nāvastuviṣayaḥ 
kintu proktanayena pratyakṣaviśeṣatvāt pramāṇamevetyato'pyātmasiddhiḥ 
| tataśca bodhena ityetadapi pratijñāpadaṃ vyākhyātamiti || 17 ||

	tadevamātmani sādhakabādhakapramāṇasadasattābhyāṃ 
sadvyavahārābhāve niraste'pi laukāyatikāḥ prāhuḥ-atastvevaṃ 
śarīrādivyatiriktaḥ sarvārthasākṣitayā 
svasaṃvedyo'haṃpratyayapratyeyatvena 

cābhinnarūpa evātmā, sa tu prayāṇakāle vinaśyati utpattimatvād 
ghaṭādivadasthira eva, na cāyamasiddho hetuḥ 
śukraśoṇitādipariṇāmaviśeṣābhāve 
caitanyātmanastasyotpattidṛṣṭestadabhāve cādṛṣṭervahnāviva dhūmasya 
kāryatvaṃ siddhaṃ yataḥ, tadidamuktam caitanyaparipūrṇo hi kāyo jāyate 
iti etat pratikṣipati-

sthiratvaṃ cāpi puṃso'tra smaraṇādeva gamyate |

	sthiratvaṃ janmanaḥ prāgapi sattā, 
śukraśoṇitādipariṇāmaviśeṣādutpattyabhāvaḥ | puṃso 
janmasamanantaraṃ smaraṇāddhetoḥ pratīyate |

	tatra tāvaddhetorasya vyāptiṃ sādhayati-

yato'nyenānubhūte'rthe tadanyasya na jāyate || 18 ||

smaraṇaṃ hi viśiṣṭaikādhikaraṇānubhavapūrvakaṃ svātmani 
bālyāvasthāta uttarakālaṃ niścitameva, yataḥ 
paṭīyaso'nubhavāddevadattādyāśrayāntaragatādāśrayāntare 
yajñadattādau samaraṇaṃ na dṛśyate, iti smaraṇasya 
viśiṣṭaikādhikaraṇānubhavapūrvakatvena vyāptisiddhiḥ || 18 ||

	pakṣadharmatvamapyāha-

harṣādilakṣyate yena tajjāte yauvanasthavat |
arthānubhavasaṃskāratadvyaktismṛtipūrvakam || 19 ||

	jāte labdhaśarīrajanmini puruṣe janmano'nantaraṃ 
mukhavikāsādyanyathānupapattyā yena kāraṇena harṣāditāvallakṣyate, 
ādigrahaṇāt sthānakaraṇābhighātādi | yadāhuḥ-

ādyaḥ karaṇavinyāsaḥ prāṇasyordhvaṃ samīraṇam |
sthānānāmavighātaśca na vinā pūrvacetanām || iti |

	tacca nonmatta iva vātādikṣobhajanitaṃ tatra tadanāvṛte'pi 
tadānīṃ sarvasminnanubhavāt, api tu yauvanādisthe puruṣa iva 
tadarthasyārthyamānasya sukhādestatsādhanasya vā yo 
yo'nubhavastenāhito yo buddhau saṃskārastasya 

prabodhādyat smaraṇaṃ tat tatkāraṇakaṃ loke lakṣyate, iti yatra harṣādi 
tatrāvaśyaṃ tathābhūtasmaraṇamityato'numitānumānenāsya 
pakṣadharmasiddhirityuktaṃ bhavati || 19 || 

	nanu sukhādyananubhavapūrvakamapi yauvanādyavasthāvati puruṣe 
harṣādi dṛśyate, iti tatsmaraṇasiddhāvanaikāntikamityāha-

	na cāpyanubhavastasya lakṣyate'nubhave yataḥ |

	yasmādanubhavaviṣaye tasya puṃsaḥ sukhādyanubhavastadānīṃ 
sukhasādhanakṣīrapānādyabhāvadvāreṇa na lakṣyate nāstīti niścīyate | 
tataḥ sukhādyanubhavābhāve satīti viśeṣitaṃ harṣādihetutvena 
prayujyamānamavyabhicārāviśiṣṭaṃ sukhādismaraṇaṃ gamayati, iti 
nānaikāntikatvam |

	tataḥ kimityāha-

	bhavāntaramatikrāntamataḥ puṃso'numīyate || 20 ||

	ato harṣādiliṅgaviśiṣṭātsmaraṇādviśiṣṭaikādikaraṇānubhava-
pūrvakatvena siddhavyāptikājjātasya puṃso 
bhavāntaramatikrāntamanumīyate | na tu śarīreṇa saha janma iti, yāvapi 
śukraśoṇitakāryatve'sya tadbhāvabhāvatadabhāvābhāvau tāvapi svataḥ 
prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsābhāvāsaṃvedanāt paracaitanyasya ca pareṇa 
pratyakṣīkartumaśakyatvānna sambhavata iti | yathā ca 
sthānakaraṇādīnāmapyatra gamakatvaṃ tathā darśitamācāryeṇa 
rauravavṛttau paramokṣanirāsakārikābhiḥ-

janmāntarānubhūte'rthe smṛtirjāte'pi dṛśyate |
aśanāya bhavetsā ca stanyaṃ pratyupasarpaṇāt || iti |

tata evāvadhāryam || 20 ||

evaṃ cānādikā tasya yoktavyā janmamālikā |

evamanena krameṇānādirjanmaprabandhaḥ puṃsaḥ sādhyaḥ ||

tataśca-

anādidharmayogitvādavināśyādisambhavaḥ || 21 ||

	utpattimatvāddhi ghaṭādivadanityatvamasyoktaṃ bhavadbhiḥ, 
taccānāditvānna sambhavati- ityavinaśvaro'pyātmā,- iti siddham || 21 ||

nanu cānāderapyasya mokṣe paramātmalayādiva 
pradīpanirvāṇavādināmiva vā jīvātmavādināṃ vināśo bhaviṣyati, 
ityāha-

śuddhe pramuktavijñāne sarvopaplavahānitaḥ |

	nāsmākamātmanāśe mokṣo'pi tu sarvabandhanivṛttiḥ 
sarvajñatvābhivyaktiśceti vakṣyāmaḥ |

	tadevaṃ pravṛttiphalabhoktā ca, iti 
pratijñāpadamāmutrikaphalaviṣayatayā vyākhyātam, 
adhunaihikaphalaviṣayatayā darśayituṃ kṣaṇiko grāhakātmā iti 
śākyapakṣo na kevalaṃ 
prākpradarśitabādhakapramāṇasadbhāvādayukto, yāvat sādhaka 
pramāṇābhāvādapītyāha-

kṣaṇikatvasya no mānaṃ bhedādarśanato'sti hi || 22 ||

	tathā hi aśrāvaṇaḥ śabdaḥ itivadayaṃ pakṣaḥ pratyakṣanirākṛto 
bhedādarśanāt svasaṃvedanapratyakṣeṇānāropitasya tadabhedasya 
proktanayena prakāśanādityarthaḥ | kiṃ ca na santi pramāṇāni 
prameyārthāni itivat svavacananirākṛtaḥ pramāṇābhāva ivaitasya 
kṣaṇikatve'sya pratijñāvacanasyānupapatteḥ, paurvāparyarahito hi kālaḥ 
kṣaṇa iti bhavatāmabhyupagamaḥ, anekakṣaṇanirvartya caitadvacanaṃ 
kathaṃ kṣaṇo nirvartayediti virodhaḥ | satyam, ata evāsmābhiḥ 
pakṣaprayogo neṣyate trirūpādeva liṅgāt sādhyasiddheḥ, tadapi tarhi atra 
liṅgamucyatām | nanu yat sat tat sarvaṃ kṣaṇikam, kṣaṇikatvābhāve 
sattvābhāvaḥ, san ca grāhakātmā- iti sattvākhyameva hetuḥ | yadyevaṃ 
viruddho'yaṃ hetuḥ sattvasyākṣaṇikatvena sarvatra ghaṭādau dṛṣṭānte 
vyāptisiddheḥ | nanu vidyujvālādiratra dṛṣṭānto na, 
tasyāpyasmadādigocaratvenānekakṣaṇarūpatvāt | asarvadṛśāṃ 
kṣaṇaviṣayavyavahārasya bhavadbhirapyanabhyupagamāt kṣaṇarūpatve 
vānaikāntika eva | nanu śuddhaṃ sattvamatra hetuḥ, yadyevamasiddhaḥ nahi 
sattvamātramakṣaṇikatvarahitaṃ kvaciddharmyantare'pi siddham | nanu 
vijātīya vyāvṛttidvāreṇa pratiniyatabhāgābhiniveśitvādvikalpānāṃ 
ghaṭādāvapi lohito

mṛṇmayaḥ parivartulaḥ ityādi vikalpāntaraviṣayalauhityaparihāreṇa 
sattvavikalpaḥ pravartamānaḥ sattvaṃ śuddhameva viṣayīkaroti- iti 
kathamasiddhaḥ |

yaduktam-

yāvantoṃ'śasamāropāstannirāsena niścayā |
tāvanta eva śabdāśca tena te bhinnagocarā || iti |

	yuktametaddharmāntaraviṣayaṃ na tu akṣaṇikaviṣayaṃ tena vinā 
vikalpatvānupapatteḥ, sarvo hi vikalpaḥ prāgdṛṣṭatayā sa evāyam iti 
pravartamāno vikalpatāmāsādayati | yojanā kalpanetyayojakatvena 
nirvikalpatvaprasaṅgāt, yojanayaiva cārtha 
ivātmanyapyakṣaṇikatāniścayaḥ | nahi ātmanyapi kṣaṇikatve sati viśiṣṭa 
śabdavācyatayā ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ ityapi yojanopapadyate | yaduktam-

taddṛṣṭāveva dṛṣṭeṣu saṃvitsāmarthyabhāvinaḥ |
smaraṇādabhilāṣeṇa vyavahāraḥ pravartate || iti |

	tad yojanātmakatvād yojayitavyārthavat ātmāpi yojakatvena 
tadupasarjanībhūto'kṣaṇikatayā niścita eva sarveṇa vikalpena bhavati,- iti 
na dharmāntaraṃ lauhityādikamiva sattvavikalpenākṣaṇikatvamapi 
parihartuṃ śakyamiti śuddhaḥ sattvākhyo heturasiddha eveti | yadi 
śuddho'yaṃ hetustato nityānityayorekenānvitatvādvastudharmatvenobhaya-
bahirbhāvāyogād śabdānityatvādau 
śrāvaṇatvādivadasādhāraṇānaikāntikaḥ | yathādṛṣṭānvitastu na 
śuddho'taśca viruddhaḥ, ityuktam | sarvasya ca sataḥ kṣaṇikatvena 
vyāptipradarśanato'kṣaṇikasya paramārthasato 
vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntasyānupapattiḥ, vipakṣavyāvṛttyasambhavena 
kevalānvayī, ayaṃ hetuḥ syādagamakaḥ, trirūpakasyaiva gamakatvena 
hetoruktatvāt | na vipakṣādvyāvṛttirhetostṛtīyarūpam, yanaiṣa doṣaḥ syāt, 
api tu sādhyābhāve sādhanābhāvātmako vyatireko'ta eva 
tato'nyastadviruddhastadabhāvo vipakṣo'syābhiruktaḥ sa cāsyāsti, iti na 
kevalānvayitvam | taduktam-

tasmādvaidharmyadṛṣṭānto neṣṭo'vaśyamihāśrayaḥ |
tadabhāve'pi tanneti vacanādapi tadgateḥ || iti |

yadyevaṃ sandigdhavyatireko'yaṃ heturvacanagamyasyārthasya 
sandigdharūpatvāt nidhimānayaṃ bhūbhāgaḥ itivat | niścitarūpatve hi 
pratijñāmātrādeva sādhyasiddheḥ kimanenāsaddhetūpanyāsāyāsena | 
nanu vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntābhāve'pi, asya vipakṣe 
bādhakapramāṇabalādvyatirekaniścayo'sti, iti na sandigdhavyatirekitā | 
yadyevaṃ tadeva bādhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ prathamamupanyasanīyaṃ tena 
vinā hetvabhidhānasyāsyānabhidhānasamānatvāt | nanvakṣaṇikasya sato 
vināśahetvayogāditi, nāsya viruddhatvāt, vināśahetvayogo hyavināśaṃ 
sādhayati na tu kṣaṇikatvam | evaṃ tarhi dṛṣṭatvena viśeṣyate, 
ghaṭādīnāṃ hi avinaśvarasvabhāvatve dṛṣṭasya kṛtakatvādinānumitasya 
vā vināśasya heturasamartha eva vinaśvarasvabhāvatve vā vyarthaḥ, 
svabhāvādeva vināśasiddheḥ | svabhāvanasya cāviśeṣāt pratikṣaṇaṃ 
sato vināśaḥ sidhyati, iti | yadyevamasiddho grāhakātmano 
vināśahetvadarśanasya pratipāditatvād dṛṣṭavināśādeva ca, 
vipakṣādanenāsya hetorvyatirekaḥ sidhyatīti, na adṛṣṭavināśāt 
paramāṇvādeḥ sambhāvyamānāditi punarapi 
sandigdhavipakṣavyāvṛttirayamanaikāntika eva, na ca 
vināśahetoratrāsāmarthyameva vaiyarthyaṃ vā- iti vakṣyāmaḥ | tarhi 
nityasya kramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyāvirodhāt, ityetadvipakṣe 
sarvatrāstu bādhakaṃ pramāṇam, na, 
āśrayāsiddhatvādidamapyapramāṇameva, nahi nityaḥ kaścidartho 
bhavadbhirabhyupagamyate yatrāyaṃ hetuḥ siddhaḥ syāt | prativādinaḥ 
siddhatvāddhetureveti cet, kathaṃ tasya siddhiḥ, dharmiṇaḥ siddhatvāditi cet, 
sa kuto'sya siddhaḥ, pramāṇena yadi, vādino'pi siddha eva 
vastvadhiṣṭhānatvāt pramāṇasyeti kathaṃ nityapratikṣepaḥ, 
athāpramāṇādabhyupagamamātrāt tasya siddha ityucyate, 
yadyevamasadevāsau kathaṃ vastu sādhayet, vastuno hi vastusiddhirityuktam | 
nanu yatra kramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyā tatra kṣaṇikatvaṃ 
kṣaṇikatvābhāve tu kramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyābhāvaḥ, iti 
dharmayorvyāptimātrapradarśanena dharmiṇaḥ kasyacidāśrayāt 
nāśrayāsiddhatā, atra doṣaḥ | yadāhālaṅkārakāraḥ-

prasaṅgasādhanamidaṃ nāśrayāsiddhatā tataḥ |
yadyevaṃ syādidamapi na cobhayamitīṣṭitā || iti |

	yadyevamapakṣadharmatvena nyūnatvādayamahetureva athamasya 
vipakṣavyāvṛttiṃ sādhayet, nahi anvayavyatirekāveva hetorlakṣaṇaṃ 
trirūpasyaiva hetutvenoktatvāt, prāgvacca vyāptirapi atrāsiddhā- iti 
darśayiṣyāmaḥ | dharmottarāstvāhuḥ- sattvaṃ hi

kramayaugapadyābhyāṃ vyāptam, ataḥ kramayaugapadyābhāvena 
sattvābhāvo'tra vyāpakānupalabdhvā sādhyastat 
nāśrayāsiddhatvamabhāvasya śaśaviṣāṇādāviva nitye dharmiṇi 
siddhatvāt | bhāvātmano hi hetostatraiṣa doṣo'ta eva 
prasaṅgaviparyayaniṣṭhaḥ prasaṅgahetuḥ iti | etadayuktaṃ bhavadbhirapi 
svasādhyapratibaddha eva heturucyate | yaduktam-

kāryakāraṇabhāvādvā svabhāvādvā niyāmakāt |
avinābhāvaniyamaḥ �������- || iti |

	na cāvābhāvarūpahetoratyantāvastunaḥ kvacit pratibandhaḥ 
sambhavati- ityahetutā | yadāhālaṅkāro'pi-

abhāvamātrake hetau kva kiṃ kena prasādhyatām | iti |

	pratibandho hi nābhāvarūpatā, ityasiddhatvamuktam, 
śaśaviṣāṇādīnāṃ tu 
vikalpakalpitatvenābhāvavyavahārādimātraviṣayatvaṃ kālpanikameva 
siddham, na tu kasyacit sādhanatayetyavirodhaḥ, ata evānupalabdheḥ 
pratipattyavasāyavaśādabhāvasādhanatvam, paramārthatastu vastuno 
viśiṣṭādviśiṣṭasya vastunaḥ siddheḥ svabhāvahetutaivetyuktaṃ 
bhavadbhireva-

prayogaḥ kevalaṃ bhinnaḥ sarvatrārtho na bhidyate | iti |

	na kiñcidetat | anye punarāhuḥ- yadeva sapakṣatvena ghaṭādīṣṭaṃ 
tadeva tāvadvipakṣatvenāpi, tanniṣṭhaṃ ca bādhakaṃ pramāṇamiti 
kuto'syā śrayāsiddhatā | yadyevaṃ puruṣecchākṛtāsya 
sapakṣavipakṣādivyavasthā na vastutaḥ, iti 
pakṣasapakṣānyataratvāderivānumānābhāsatā, na ca sapakṣa eva 
vipakṣaḥ sambhavati sādhyabhāvābhāvayoratyantavirodhāt na vastuto'sya 
sapakṣatvādvipakṣatvaṃ tu ghaṭāderāropya bādhakena pramāṇena 
niṣidhyate, yadi ghaṭāderakṣaṇikatvaṃ syāt tato'kṣaṇikasya 
kramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyāvirodhaḥ syāt, karoti cārthakriyāmataḥ 
kṣaṇika iti | yathā yadyatra vahnirna bhavet dhūmo'pi na bhavet, asti ca 
dhūmaḥ tasmādatrāgnirityavirodhāt nāśrayāsiddhatā, iti | tadapyayuktaṃ 
virodhāsiddheḥ, akṣaṇikasyaiva ghaṭādeḥ 
kṣīraghṛtadhāraṇādyarthakriyā krameṇa, pradīpādeśca 
vartidāhatailakṣapaṇādikā yugapaddṛśyate- iti

kutastadvirodhasiddhiḥ | tasyaikasvabhāvatvāt krameṇārthakriyāyā 
anupapatteḥ sarvāḥ kālāntarakāryā api yugapat kuryāt, na ca tā yugapat 
kurvan dṛśyata ityato virodhasiddhiḥ | na, asyāpi 
hetorvyadhikaraṇasiddhatvāt yasya hi yugapadanekārthakriyājanakaḥ 
svabhāvaḥ sa pradīpādiryugapattāḥ kurvan dṛśyata eva | yastu krameṇa 
kāryaṃ kurvan dṛśyate sa krameṇaivānekārthakriyājananaikasvabhāvaḥ 
siddho na yugapadityavirodhaḥ | atha kimarthaṃ krameṇa karoti, 
tathāsvabhāvatvādyugapadiveti vakṣyāmaḥ | tadevaṃ vipakṣe bādhakasya 
pramāṇasyāsambhavādanaikāntikametat na kṣaṇikatvasya sādhakaṃ 
pramāṇamiti | abhinavaśākyāstvāhuḥ- nāsya vipakṣe 
bādhakapramāṇavaśādasattāniścayo'pi tu 
vastupratibandhādhiṣṭhānādvicārādeva, sattvaṃ hi vicāryamāṇaṃ 
tejorūpaṃ dāhakatva iva svabhāvapratibandhādevākṣaṇikāt 
nivartamānaṃ kṣaṇikatve viśrāmyati- iti 
vastusvarūpaparyālocanāsiddhametat ato 
nāśrayāsiddhatādipūrvoktadoṣaprasaṅga iti | 
yadyevamarthāpattyātmako'yaṃ vicāraḥ proktaḥ pratibhāvātmako vā | 
yathāhurvaiyākaraṇavṛddhāḥ nāpūrvaṃ kiñcit iti | 
pramāṇāntaratvādasya pratyakṣamanumā ca 
pramāṇamevetyabhyupagamavirodhaḥ, apramāṇatve tu kathaṃ tato 
vipakṣābhāvaniścayaḥ sadasanniścayahetoreva pramāṇatvāt | nanu 
vastusvarūpaparyālocanayāpi nayavidāmasannihitārthaviṣayaḥ ūhātmako 
niścayo niścayo dṛśyata eva | satyam, yogyatānumānādevāsau nānyataḥ | 
yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

hetunā yaḥ samagreṇa kāryotpādo'numīyate |
arthāntarānapekṣitvātmasvabhāvo'nuvarṇitaḥ || iti |

	api ca pratibandhādhiṣṭhāno vicāra iti ko'rthaḥ | kiṃ vicārāt 
pratibandhasiddhirāhosvit siddhe pratibandhe vicāra iti | pūrvasmin 
kalpe'tyantāsambandhayorghaṭapaṭayorapi vicārāt pratibandhaḥ syāt- 
ityanavasthā | atha dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tarhi pratyakṣasiddhatvāt pratibandhasya 
tejodāhakatvayorivottarakālabhāvī ayaṃ vicāraḥ smṛtipūrvo vyavahāra 
evoktaḥ syāditi na tato'pūrvārthapratipattiḥ | yaduktam-

yaddṛṣṭāveva dṛṣṭeṣu saṃvitsāmarthyabhāvinaḥ |
smaraṇādabhilāṣeṇa vyavahāraḥ pravartate || iti |

na ca sattvakṣaṇikatvayoḥ kathañcidapi pratibandhaḥ siddhaḥ iti tadviṣayasya 
vicārasyānupapatteḥ kathaṃ tato'sya hetorvipakṣād vyāvṛttiryena 
kṣaṇikatvasiddhau mānatā syāditi || 22 ||

abhyupagamyāpi kṣaṇikapakṣamanirmokṣaprasaṅga ityāha-

punarbhavaprasaṅgaḥ syātrirnimitte hyupaplave |

	tairhi duḥkhātmano'sya saṃsārasya samudaye nimittamavidyātṛṣṇe 
gīyete | yaduktam-

duḥkhe viparyāsamatitṛṣṇāśā bandhakāraṇāt |
janmino yasya te na sto na sa janmādhigacchati || iti |

	na ca tayornimittatvamupapadyate pratikṣaṇaṃ pūrvasyaiva 
nīlapītādijñānakṣaṇātmanaḥ samanantarapratyayasyātra 
nimittatvābhyupagamato'nyasyāsambhavāt | taduktam-

yasmin sati bhavatyeva yattato'nyasya kalpane |
taddhetutvena sarvatra hetūnāmanavasthitiḥ || iti |

	tato nimittāntarābhāve naitāvanmātranimittatvādasya 
saṃsārātmano viplavasya prāgvat punaḥ prasaṅgāt mokṣalakṣaṇasya 
nirodhasya tadupāyasya ca samudayaviruddhabhāvanārūpasya 
mārgasyānupapatteḥ | duḥkhasamudayanirodhamārgāṇāṃ caturṇāmapi 
āryasatyānāmasatyataiva, iti |

	astu vā kadācit pāramparyeṇāpi etat, rāgādiviplavasyaiva nimittaṃ 
na tu grāhyagrāhakātmanaḥ, ityāha-

nimittaṃ sādhu vaktavyaṃ meyamānavyavasthiteḥ || 23 ||

	na hi yatra grāhyagrāhakavyavasthā tatrāvidyātṛṣṇe bhavatāmapi 
siddhe, vītarāgapuruṣāṇāṃ sarvajñānāṃ ca 
tayorasambhavādayuktamatra tayornimittatvam || 23 ||

	nanu grāhyagrāhakavyavasthānimittatvametayoḥ svapne 
siddhamityāha-

svapnādipratyayā ye'nye dṛṣṭāntatvena te matāḥ |
vāsanādikṛtāste'pi susthirāṇorbhavanti hi || 24 ||

	na hi tatrāpi tayornimittatvaṃ kenāpi pramāṇena siddhamapi tu 
karmopasaṃskṛtāyā

anubhavavāsanāyā eva yathānubhavaṃ rūpādyālokanena 
duḥkhādivedanāt jātyandhānāṃ svapne rūpādyanavabhāsācca, 
vāsanā ca sthirātmavādināmevopapadyate na kṣaṇikapakṣe, ityuktaṃ 
prāk | kathaṃ tarhi bhrāntipratibhāsā nāviṣayā 
dvicandrādyākārāvabhāsanāt nāsatyaviṣayā asato bhāsanāyogāt, na 
viparītārthāstena tena rūpeṇa tasyāsatyatvataḥ proktadoṣāt, 
nātmākārāstasyāvikārāt, kiṃ tu 
adhyavasāyavāsanopaskṛtabuddhyākāraviṣayā evetyuktamanyatra 
antaḥkaraṇatattvasya bhāgā bahiriva sthitāḥ | iti na teṣāmapi 
viplavanimittatā || 24 ||

	etadeva darśayati-

samastameyamānādi gītaṃ dhīvādināpi hi |
antarbhāvagrahātsattvāt mithyātvamapi tasya na || 25 ||

	nahi grāhyagrāhakagrahaṇātmano bhedasyāsya 
vijñānavādibhirabhāvo'bhidhātuṃ śakyo'nubhavasiddhatvāt, api tu 
vijñānasvabhāvatvameva | tato'ntarbhāvagrahādvijñānarūpatvenāpi 
asya satvāt mithyātvamupaplavarūpatvaṃ nopapadyate'pi tu 
bodharūpataivetyanivartyatvād dvayāsiddheranirmokṣa eveti || 25 ||

	nanu grāhyagrāhakaprakāśasya pramāṇabādhitatvādanāt-
manyātmabuddherivopaplavarūpatvameva na vijñānasvabhāvatā, tathā hi 
pratyakṣaṃ sarvamidaṃ ghaṭādi grāhyaṃ grāhakānna bhidyate 
grāhyatvāt, bhede hi devadattayajñadattajñānayoriva parasparamagrāhyatā 
syāt | asti ca sā, tato dvayābhāva eveti | yadāhuḥ-

paramārthena na grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ vā na kiñcana | 
avidyānirmitaḥ sargo viplavo'yaṃ janasya tu || iti |

ata āha-

pratyakṣaṃ jñānameveti mithyāvākyaṃ tadeva hi | 
sarvaṃ pratīyate yasmāt na ca bādhaḥ kathañcana || 26 ||

	etadapi parasparāviruddhatvānna bādhakaṃ bādhakasyāpyasya 
jñānasya bādhyamānārthaviṣayatvāt tadaviṣayatve 
tanniṣedhānupapatterbādhakatvāyogaḥ, iti grāhyasya sarvasyābādhyatvena 
pratīyamānatvāt na, anātmanyātmabuddherivopaplavarūpateti, ata eva 
pratyakṣā buddhireva ityādinātrāpaunaruktyaṃ sādhyabhedāt || 26 ||

	kiñca dharmisvarūpaviparītasādhano'yaṃ heturbhavatāmityāha-

grāhyatvādarthavadbuddheranavasthā prasajyate |

grāhyatvād buddhisaṃvedyatve'rthasya jñānasyāpi 
jñānāntaragrāhyatvenaiva saṃvedyatvādasvasaṃvedyatāprasaṅgaḥ, 
tasyāpi cāsaṃvedyatve tadgrāhakatvānupapattestasya tasyāpītyanavasthā ||

	nanvajñātamapi jñānamindriyamarthavāt jñānāntaraṃ grahīṣyati 
tato nānavastheti pūrvānuvādenāha-

buddhyantaragṛhītā dhīryadyarthe sampravartate || 27 ||

syādasaṃsthā tayoryāvannaivaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ kadācana |

	yuktametat cakṣurādīnāmarthasākṣātkāraṃ prati kārakatvāt | 
jñāpakānāṃ liṅgaśabdādīnāṃ svaprākāśaṃ vinā 
grāhyaprakāśakatvaṃ na kadācid dṛṣṭamiti jñānasyāpi jñāpakatvāt 
svaprakāśaṃ vinā jñānāntaragrāhakatvavyavasthā nopapadyate, iti 
sarvāpratyakṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ | taduktam-

apratyakṣopalambhasya nārthadṛṣṭiḥ prasidhyati | iti ||

	kiñca vādyasiddho'yaṃ heturityāha-

svāṃśapratyavasānaṃ ca vijñānaṃ tena kalpyate || 28 ||

	vijñānavādināpi ekasya vijñānasya ghaṭādyātmano'nekatvaṃ 
parikalpyam | yaduktam-

ekaṃ ceccitravijñānamidaṃ citrataraṃ tataḥ |

iti jñānāṃśaparāmāṇuparyanto bhedaḥ kalpanīya iti 
ghaṭāderarthābhimatasya pratyakṣatvāsiddhirityanubhavavirodhaśca || 28 ||

	api ca vijñānasantānāntaramapi na śakyameva kalpayitumiti 
kimarthaṃ śāstrapraṇayanaṃ dānādi veti 
sarvavyavahārapralopo'syetyāha-

na cāpyālambanaṃ bhinnaṃ santānāntaramasti hi |
svākāraṃ cetyamityasmātsarvābhāvaḥ prasajyate || 29 ||

	kāyavāgvyavahāro hi svaśarīre viśiṣṭavijñānapūrvako dṛṣṭaḥ, 
iti paraśarīre'pi taddarśanādvijñānamanumīyate sarvaireva | 
vijñānavādināṃ tu svākārasyaiva sarvasya cetyatvāt svapnavat 
paraśarīrāsiddhermithyā eṣa pratibhāsaḥ, iti kutastato 
jñānasantānāntarasiddhiḥ, siddhāvapi tanna bhinnamālambanaṃ sidhyati 
arthavattasyāpi bhinnasya sākāreṇa nirākāreṇa vā jñānena 
viṣayīkartumaśakyatvāditi tadapi

svākārātmakameva vedyaṃ sidhyatīti | yaduktam-

buddhipūrvāṃ kriyāṃ dṛṣṭvā svadehe'nyatra tadgrahāt |
jñāyate yadi dhīścittamātre'pyeṣa nayaḥ samaḥ || iti |

	tadapyayuktamityataḥ kaḥ kasya kiṃ kuryāditi 
sarvavyavahārābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ, tataḥ satyabhūta eva 
grāhyagrāhakabhedo'bhyupagantavyaḥ, ityanivartyatvādadvayāsiddhe- 
ranirmokṣa eva bhavatām || 29 ||

	adhunā yairbāhyamabhyupagamya sākārameva tasya grāhakamiṣyate 
arthajñānayorākārabhedaṃ nopalabhāmahe iti vadadbhistān pratyucyate-

saṅguptakaraṇasyeha yogino buddhipaddhatiḥ |
apāstaviṣayākārā dṛṣṭā tasmātpratīyate || 30 ||

śabdādiviṣayo yo'pi bodhaḥ so'pyatadākṛtiḥ |
tatparicchedarūpo'sau tadākāra iti bhramaḥ || 31 ||

	bhavedetat, yadyarthajñānayorākārabhedo nopalabdho bhavet | sa 
tūpalabhyata eva | tathā hi samupahṛtasamastakaraṇavṛtterata eva 
vinivṛttākhilaviṣayākāravedanasya saṃvinmātrayoginaḥ śuddhameva 
vijñānātmarūpaṃ prabandhena prakāśate, iti bhavadāgame'pi 
dṛṣṭamadvayasyārthānārūpitasyānyathāprāptyasambhavāt | proktaṃ 
cādhyātmakuśalairapi-

tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpe'vasthānam || iti |

	arthakārādbhinna eva grāhakaḥ siddhastato bhedādeva 
śabdādiparicchedakāle tatparicchekatvameva 
grāhakātmano'nubhavasiddhatvāt pratyetuṃ śakyam, na tu 
śabdādyākārarūpatānubhavavirodhāditi sākārapakṣo'pi bhrama eva || 
31 ||

	etadeva ghaṭayannāha-

artha eva hi sākāro yatastadvān sa gṛhyate |

	artho hi anekanīlapītādyākārabhedabhinno grāhakātmanaḥ 
pratyagrūpeṇādhyātmamanubhūyamānāt purorūpatvena 
bahirdeśasambaddhaḥ sākāra eva vedyate, iti tato'nākārājjñānādbhinna 
eva yuktaḥ |

	evaṃ grāhakātmāpekṣayoktvādhyavasāyāpekṣayāpi āha-

pūrvaṃ ca gṛhyate vastu bodhaḥ paścāditi sthitiḥ || 32 ||

	jhaṭiti indriyārthasaṃyoge hi prathamamadhyavasāyarahitaṃ 
śuddhameva vastu, paścāttu ghaṭo'yam ityādyadhyavasāyo'pi saṃvedyata 
iti tadabhedo'pyayuktaḥ | nanu buddhyavasitamarthaṃ puruṣaścetayate, iti 
sarveṣāṃ buddhipuruṣavivekavādināmabhyupagamaḥ, tataḥ 
pūrvamadhyavasāyaḥ paścātsaṃvedanamiti bhavatāṃ sthitiryuktā na 
tviyaṃ śākyadarśanaparidṛṣṭeti | satyam, smṛtipratibhāsādāvevāsau, na 
sarvatrānubhavavirodhāt, nahi pratidarśanamanubhavabhedo, yuktastasya 
vastusiddhatvāt nahi nīlaṃ śākyānāmeva nīlamārhatānāṃ tu pītamiti 
śakyamatinipuṇatarairapi vaktum | anubhavo hyayaṃ vastusāmarthyasiddhaḥ 
prathamamanadhyavasāyātmaka eva saṃvedyamānaḥ kathamanyathā 
vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyaḥ | yadāhuḥ-

asti hyālocanaṃ jñānaṃ prathamaṃ nirvikalpakam |
bālamūkādivijñānasadṛśaṃ śuddhavastujam ||

tataḥ praṃ punarvastu dharmairjātyādibhiryayā |
buddhyāvasīyate sāpi pratyakṣatvena sammatā ||

iti yuktamuktam iti sthitiḥ iti || 32 ||

	nanu nākāraṇaṃ viṣayo jñānasyānavasthāprasaṅgāt kāraṇatve 
cendriyāderapi prasaṅgāt tadākārataiva yuktā, ityāha-

vastuno janakatvaṃ hi biddhiṃ pratyupakāritā |
karmatendriyasambandhādādānaṃ nākṛterataḥ || 33 ||

	buddhiṃ prati vastuno janakatvamupakāritvaṃ karmatā 
grāhyatvamevocyate yataḥ, tato na tadākārādānamupapadyate, 
nāpyanavasthetyāha- indriyasambandhāt | grāhyatvāviśeṣe'pi 
yadevendriyasambaddhaṃ vastu tadeva gṛhyate nānyat yataḥ | taduktam-

satsamprayoge puruṣasyendriyāṇāṃ buddhijanma tat pratyakṣam |

iti, ato nirākārapakṣasyaivānubhavasiddhatvādabādhitatvācca satyatvamiti 
sākārapakṣo bhrānta eveti, ataśca muktāvadvayāsiddhirityupasaṃhāraḥ || 
33 ||

	nanvevamapi kṣaṇikatva ivākṣaṇikatve'pi grāhyasya 
pramāṇābhāvāt

kṣaṇikatvākṣaṇikatvasandehādapi upaplavarūpamityakṣaṇikatve 
pramāṇamāha-

ato'pi pratyabhijñānād vyabhicāre tu satyapi |
sthairyaṃ kvacid gṛhītaṃ yat svabhāvatvena vastunaḥ || 34 ||

	na kevalaṃ prakpradarśitanayena grāhakātmanyeva sthairyaṃ 
yāvattadgrāhye'pi arthe pratyabhijñānāditi ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ iti 
sthiratvenaivādhyavasāyāt, nahi bhavadbhirapyadhyavasīyamānaṃ 
sthairyamartheṣvapahnotuṃ śakyamiti darśitaṃ prāgeva | nanu 
lūnapunarjāteṣviva keśanakhādiṣu sādṛśyanibandhanā 
sthairyapratipattirbhaviṣyati- ityāha kvacid bādhakabalāt vyabhicāre tu 
satyapi anyatrābādhite viṣaye satyarajate rajatādhyavasāyasyaiva 
bādhakābhāvādavyabhicāraḥ, atrāpi bādhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ bhaviṣyatīti 
cet, na, asaṃbhavādityāha gṛhītaṃ yat iti | yasmādidaṃ sthairyaṃ 
vastugrahiṇā pratyakṣeṇa nīlādivat prāggṛhītameva, agṛhīte hi 
vasturūpe'dhyavasite śuktikārajatāderiva 
keśanakhasthairyādhyavasāyasya bādho bhavatyeva, na tu gṛhīte 
nīlādhyavasāyasyevānavasthāprasaṅgāt | nanu 
sthairyasyānekakālavyāpārātmakatvadvartamānamātragrāhiṇā 
pratyakṣeṇa grahaṇaṃ na sambhavati, ityāha svabhāvatvena vastunaḥ iti | 
sthairyamapi vastusvabhāvātmakameva tadasvabhāvatvena 
vastunastadanupatteḥ praṇālikāgraniryattvaritajalapravāhavat 
pratikṣaṇavinaśyadrūpameva sarvaṃ vastu dṛṣṭaṃ syāt- iti 
pratyakṣavirodhaḥ sthirātmatayaiva vastuno grahaṇāt | taduktam-

rajataṃ gṛhyamāṇaṃ hi cirasthāyīti gṛhyate | iti |

	na ca sadṛśaparāparotpatteḥ sthairyapratipattirbhrānteti yujyata iti 
vaktuṃ tasyāpi asiddheḥ siddhe hi kṣaṇikatve kadācidetadupapadyate | 
taccādyayāvat kimātmakaṃ kena kena pramāṇena siddhamiti na kasyacit 
siddham | na ca vastusvabhāvo'pi vastuni gṛhīte na gṛhīta iti śakyaṃ 
vaktumabhedāt | yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

ekasyārthasvabhāvasya pratyakṣasya sataḥsvayam |
ko'nyo na dṛṣṭo bhāgaḥ syādyaḥ pramāṇaiḥ parīkṣyate ||

iti nāpi ca pratyakṣaṃ vartamānamātragrāhitasyāpi sthirātmakatvāt, 
indriyārthasannikarṣastu vartamāno na pratyakṣo'rthastatsaṃvidvā 
ātmasvabhāvatvāt tasyāḥ- iti pratipāditam || 34 ||

etadeva darśayati-

bhūyo bhūyo hi yo'rthātmā gṛhyate tvekayā dhiyā |
yadā tadā ca tatsthairyaṃ brūta kena nivāryate || 35 ||

	evaṃ hi bodhisattvānāmapi punaḥ punardṛśyamāneṣu 
jāyājananyādiṣvartheṣu gurudāsādiṣu cānurūpo vyavahāra 
upapadyate'nyathā viparyaya eva syāt | nanu santānasya jananītvaṃ 
jāyārūpatā veti cet, na, tasyāpi santānivyatirekeṇa vastuto'sambhavāt 
santāninaḥ kṣaṇasyaiva jananītvaṃ yato jātaḥ tasyaivābhyupagantavyam | 
tataḥ pañcamasya tu kṣaṇasya pañcamīṃ mātṛbandhubhyaḥ iti 
samācārājjāyātvaṃ bhavadaviruddhameva syāditi jitaṃ 
paśubhirbhavaddarśanaceṣṭāniṣṭhaireva | kathaṃ vā parvatādiṣu 
sarvadaikarūpābhāseṣu na sthairyam || 35 ||

	nanu parvatādiṣvapi 
hemantagrīṣmādijanitaśaityoṣṇatādiviruddhadharmayogādavaśyaṃ 
tadbhedena bhedastataḥ sthairyānupapattirityāha-

śītādibhedato bhedo nahi bhedadhiyaṃ vinā |

	nahi grīṣmahemantādyātmakaśītātapādīnāmiva 
tadabhivyaṅgyasya tajjananyasya vā śaityoṣṇatādeḥ parvatātmanaḥ 
svabhāvasya bhedo yugapat sākārajñāna ivaikasāmagrīpratibaddhe 
rūpādāviva vā krameṇa vicitraikasvabhāvayogāt | sākāraṃ hi 
ghaṭādivijñānamekamanekarūpamekasāmagrīpratibaddhaṃ ca 
rūpādyanekopakārasvabhāvamabhyupagatameva bhavadbhiḥ, anyathā 
pratiparamāṇubhedakalpanayā 
sthūlākārāvabhāsasyānekopakārāyogācca sāmagryasambhavena 
viśiṣṭakāryotpādasyābhāvaprasaṅga ityuktam, tathehāpi 
krameṇānekarūpānekopakārasvabhāvasya cābhyupagamādavirodhaḥ | 
nanu yaugapadyenā'nekasya rūpasyānekopakāraśaktīnāṃ ca tatra 
samuccayādyuktamaikyam, krame tu 
parasparaparihārasyāvaśyaṃbhāvādatra bheda eveti | na tatrāpi 
pṛthubudhnodarāṇāmanekopakāraśaktīnāṃ ca parasparaparihāreṇaiva 
sthiteḥ, anyathā hi aṇumātrameva piṇḍaḥ pratibhāseta, rūpādisāmagryapi 
ekameva kāryaṃ kuryāditi sarvamasamañjasam | tadyathā 
tatraikamanekarūpamanekopakāraśaktiyuktaṃ caikaṃ 
yugapattathāsvabhāvatvādupapadyate, tadvadiha parvatādyapi 
krameṇānekarūpamanekopakāraśaktiyuktaṃ caikameva tathāsvabhāvaṃ 
sthirarūpamanubhavataḥ siddhamiti viruddhadharmāyogāt na śītādibhedato 
bhedo'pi tu yatra bhinnā buddhistatraiva ghaṭapaṭayoriva bhedaḥ, yatra 
tvabhinnā tatra ghaṭākāre vijñāna ivābhedaḥ, ityavirodhaḥ | yaduktaṃ 
bhavadbhirapi-

abhinnavedanasyaikaṃ yannaivaṃ taddhi bhedavat |
sidhyedasādhanatve'sya na siddhaṃ bhedasādhanam || iti |

abhyupagamyādi śītādibhedena bhedamāha-

tadabhedādabhedaśca yāvattāvatprasidhyati || 36 ||

	śītātapādibhedavattajjanitaśaityoṣṇatādidharmabhedena 
parvatāderbhede'pi yātkālamabhedaḥ śītasya hemantātmano 
grīṣmarūpasya cātapasya tāvatkālamabhedaḥ, parvatādāvapi sthairyaṃ 
kena nivāryate viruddhadharmayogāddhi bhedaḥ pratipādito na ca tāvantaṃ 
kālamasāvasti, iti tāvantaṃ kālaṃ sthairyaṃ siddhameva || 36 ||

	nanu parataḥ svabhāvādeva tena naṣṭavyam, ityaviśeṣāt pūrvamapi 
vinaśyediti kṣaṇikatvasiddhiḥ,- ityāha-

vilakṣaṇasya tūtpāde kāraṇāttannivartate |
cidātmanaḥ samucchedo janma tasyācito'pi ca || 37 ||

	śaityasvabhāvāttasmāt 
parvatāderātapādisahakāriṇastasyauṣṇyadharmakasyānyasyaiva 
pavartatāderutpāde sati tata evātapādeḥ kāraṇāt tat pūrvaṃ 
śaityasvabhāvaṃ parvatādi nivartate'nvayavyatirekābhyāṃ 
tathāvagaterna tu svabhāvamātrāditi kutaḥ kṣaṇikatvaprasaṅgaḥ | yathā ca 
vināśahetorasāmarthyavaiyarthyādayo na doṣāstathā vakṣyāmaḥ | tata 
eva ca kāraṇāttadānīṃ tatra pratyabhijñānapratyayasyāpi nivṛttiḥ, 
tasyācetanasya vilakṣaṇasyauṣṇyadharmakasya parvatāderjanma yataḥ | 
idameva ca dharmapariṇāmaṃ sthairyavādinaḥ samācakṣate || 37 ||

	atha kathameko'sau vilakṣaṇametat kāryatrayaṃ karoti- ityāha-

svabhāvena ca sarvatra vaicitryaṃ na bhave bhavet |

	yadyevaṃ nābhyupagamyeta tadā sarvatrāsmin saṃsāre svabhāvena 
vaicitryamanekakāryakartṛtvaṃ na kasyacidapi bhavediti pradīpādirapi 
vartidāhatailakṣapaṇādikāryamanekaṃ na kuryāt- iti pratyakṣavirodhaḥ, 
tata etadavaśyameṣṭavyam |

	kiṃ ca kṣaṇikapakṣe kāryakāraṇabhāvo na jñātuṃ śakyaḥ- 
ityāha-

pūrvottarakṣaṇājñānātkāryakāraṇatā kutaḥ || 38 ||

	kriyāpalāpanibandhanaḥ kṣaṇikavādaḥ- iti na kriyāviśeṣayogāt 
kāraṇatvaṃ

vahnyāderapi tu prāgbhāva eva, paścādbhāvastu dhūmādeḥ kāryatā | 
tau ca pūrvāparādivat pūrvāparabhāvau parasparasambandhirūpatvāt 
naikaikakṣaṇagrahaṇakāle grahītuṃ śakyau, na cobhayakṣaṇagrāhakaṃ 
jñānāntaraṃ bhavatāṃ sambhavati kṣaṇadvayasthitivikalatvena 
bhāvānāmabhyupagamāt | tataḥ pratyakṣānupalambhasādhanaḥ 
kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ ityetadapi ayuktamiti || 38 ||

	nanu dhūmahetusvabhāvatvādvahnikṣaṇasya tadgrahaṇe gṛhītameva 
tat, yadyevaṃ sarvajñajñeyatvamapi tasyāsti- iti tasyāpi tadgrahaṇe 
grahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ | na, grahaṇe'pi aniścayāditi cet, na, kṣaṇikatve 
niścayasyāpi asambhavāditi na kiñcidetat | akṣaṇikapakṣe tu naiṣa doṣaḥ, 
ityāha-

sāmarthyavyañjikā siddhā sāmagrī susthirātmanaḥ |

	vahnyādikāraṇasāmagrī susthirā satī svātmanaḥ sambandhino 
dhūmādikāryajananasāmarthyasya niścāyikā siddhā, sthirāyāṃ 
satyāṃ kāryakāraṇatāparicchittirupapadyate, na kṣaṇikāyātmityarthaḥ |

etadeva darśayati-

vyaktasāmarthyakaṃ vastu yogyaṃ kṛtyamapekṣya tu || 39 ||

	yogyamakṣaṇikameva kāryamapekṣya kāraṇavastu 
abhivyaktakāraṇasāmarthyaṃ bhavati || 39 ||

sthairye sanmānasaṃsiddhe yugapatkramaśo yathā |
dṛṣṭistathābhyanujñānaṃ sāmagrīvaśataḥ kriyā || 40 ||

	sthiratareṇa pramāṇena sthairye siddhe sati kvacidyathā 
naṭamallaprekṣādau yugapaddarśanam, gacchato vicitrādhvaviṣayaṃ ca 
krameṇopapadyate tathā krameṇedamidaṃ tu yugapatkāryam 
ityabhyanujñānam | kāraṇasāmagrīviśeṣalābhācca kvacidyugapadanyatra 
krameṇārthakriyāpyupapadyate, na tu kṣaṇikasya kṣaṇikatvādeva 
kramābhāvāt | yugapadapyutpatteḥ prāgasattvādutpattikālasamakālaṃ 
cābhinnakālatvena kāryāsambhavādutpannasya ca 
nāśānantaritatvādarthakriyā na sambhavatyeva | nanu dvitīyasmin kṣaṇe 
nāśavat kāryaṃ tato bhaviṣyati- iti | yadyevaṃ nāśānantaritvāt tadeva 
taditi kṣaṇikatvāsiddheḥ, tadantaritatve tvabhāvādutpattirdvitīyasya 
ghaṭādikṣaṇasyetyahetukatvāt nityaṃ sattvamasattvaṃ vā syāt- ityuktam, 
ataḥ kṣaṇikasya 
kramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyāyogādavastutvaprasaṅgaḥ || 40 ||

kiṃ ca pramāṇaprameyabhāvāścātra- ityāha-

na ca mānaṃ parasyāsti svakḷptārthaprasiddhaye |

	kṣaṇikatve hi sarvasyotpattisamanantarameva dhvaṃsāt kasya kiṃ kutra 
pramaṇamiti sarvāsiddhiḥ |

	nanu ca taduttarakālabhāvivikalpakalpitā ānumānikyeveyaṃ 
pramāṇādivyavasthā | na, kṣaṇikatvena 
tayorapyayogādityuktamabhyupagamyāpi āha-

vikalpādi vinā nārthaṃ liṅgaṃ nāviṣayatvataḥ || 41 ||

	arthaṃ vinā nirarthakatvena bhavadbhirabhyupagamāt nātra vikalpādi, 
ādigrahaṇādātmādhyāroparūpaṃ kliṣṭamapi ca manaḥ pramāṇam | 
nāpyanumānaṃ nirviṣayatvenaiva tasyāpi paramārthato'bhyupagamāt, na 
cāviṣayaṃ mithyājñānavadvyavasthāpakamupapadyate || 41 ||

	nanu-

sāmānyaviṣayaṃ proktaṃ liṅgaṃ bhedāpratiṣṭhiteḥ |

ityuktatvāt nāsmākaṃ nirviṣayamanumānamityāha-

sāmānyavati sambodhastvaduktādakṣato'pi hi |
smṛterniścayataścaitadanyathā nobhayaṃ tu tat || 42 ||

	evaṃ tarhi sāmānyasya pramāṇaviṣayatvena svalakṣaṇavat 
sattvaprasaṅgāt tvaduktādakṣato'pi indriyavijñānenāpi grahaṇaṃ syāt 
tathaiva tadarthasya smaraṇāt ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ iti sāmānyaviśeṣātmana eva 
niścayāt, anyathā svalakṣaṇasyāpi sattvāsiddheḥ, tadubhayamapi 
sāmānyaviśeṣātmakaṃ vastu neti sarvāsiddhiḥ | tataḥ sāmānyamapi 
sadabhyupagantavyam || 42 ||

kimata ityāha-

vyaktyantare grahāttasya vyatireko vivakṣitaḥ |
tena vyaktivināśe'pi tattāvadavatiṣṭhate || 43 ||

	tena sāmānyatvena vyaktivināśe'pi tasya vyaktyantare sato grahaṇāt 
tāvatsthairyaṃ siddhaṃ na yāvat maṣakādisāmānyasyeva 
sarvavyaktivināśo jātaḥ- iti kṣaṇikāḥ sarvasaṃskārāḥ ityetad 
bādhitameva || 43 ||

nanu paramārthataḥ kalpitameva sāmānyamityāha-

virahayya na sāmānyaṃ vyaktiḥ puṃbhogasādhikā |

	dvividhaṃ sukhādi vaiṣayikamābhimānikaṃ ca saṃvedyate | tatra 
pūrvaṃ śītādisparśajanyaṃ svalakṣaṇahetukameva siddham, uttaraṃ 
tvabhimānasyādhyavasāyarūpatvena 
pūrvāparaparāmarśayogyārtharājyādiviṣayaṃ siddham | na ca 
svalakṣaṇaṃ tathābhūtaṃ bhavadbhiriṣyate- iti sāmānyahetukameva, tataḥ 
sukhādisādhakatvātsvalakṣaṇavat sāmānyasyāpi nāvastutvam | nanu 
tajjñānasya tatra hetutvaṃ na tasyeti cet, na, svalakṣaṇaviṣayasyeva 
sāmānyaviṣayasyāpi jñānasyaiva sukhādyavabhāsātmakatvāt svātmani 
kriyāvirodhācca viṣayasyaiva hetutvamiti ||

	adhunā yairvināśahetvasāmarthyavaiyarthyābhyāṃ kṣaṇikatvaṃ 
sādhyate tān pratyāha-

yataḥ kāraṇanāśāderbhaṅgo'rthe na svabhāvataḥ || 44 ||

yataḥ pūrvoktāt sthairyāt pramāṇasiddhāddhetoḥ kāraṇasya 
nāśena nāśo'rthasya yathā śleṣmādernāśena ślaiṣmikasya vyādheḥ | 
ādigrahaṇānnāśahetoḥ saḥ | kvacicca kāraṇānnāśastena nāśahetoḥ 
sadbhāvāt yathā ghaṭādermudgarādibhyo na tu svabhāvādeva yena 
svabhāvasyāviśeṣāt prāgapi nāśasiddheḥ kṣaṇikatvaṃ siddhyet || 44 ||

	nanu nāśahetavaḥ kimavyatiriktaṃ vyatiriktaṃ vā tasya nāśaṃ 
kurvanti | avyatireke bhāva eva vināśaḥ, sa ca svahetunaiva kṛta iti 
vaiyarthyaṃ teṣām | vyatireke'pi avastu vastu vā nāśaḥ- iti vācyam | 
tatrāvastuno'tyantāsattvataḥ 
kartumaśakyatvādakiñcitkaratvenāsāmarthyaṃ teṣām | vastutve tu 
ghaṭasyāsau paṭāderivārthāntaratvāt na nāśaḥ, iti 
prāgvadupalambhaprasaṅgaḥ | nanu ghaṭanāśātmanastasya kṛtatvād naiṣa 
prasaṅgaḥ | na, ghaṭasya na kṛto yataḥ | nanu tato'sya vināśo bhaviṣyati | na, 
tatrāpi sa eva prasaṅgaḥ, ityanavasthā | tasmādahetuka eva 
vināśo'bhyugantavyaḥ, ahetośca deśakālaniyamāyogāt pratikṣaṇaṃ 
vināśasiddhiriti | ata āha-

na cāpyahetuko nāśaḥ kadācidbhāvato'rthavat |

	na tāvadahetuko bhāvānāṃ vināśo 
mudgarādihetvanvayavyatirekānuvidhānājjanmavat | ata eva nāvastvasau, api 
tu karparādyātmako vastveva | ghaṭāderhi karparādyātmatānāśaḥ 
pratyakṣasiddho nānyastasyāvastutvena pramāṇāgocaratvāt | taduktam-

yathāheḥ kuṇḍalībhāvo vyagrāṇāṃ vā samagratā |
tathaiva janmanāśau tu satāmeke pracakṣate || iti |

	yadyevaṃ kāryameva vināśa uktaḥ syāt | na, vikāritvāt, kāryaṃ hi 
udakadhāraṇādyavikṛtarūpasyaiva ghaṭādeḥ siddham, nāśastu 
rūpāntarāpattilakṣaṇo vikāraḥ | idameva prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaṃ 
mahāmatayo manyante | loko'pi naṣṭaṃ kṣīramamblībhūtam ityāha | nanu 
dadhyutpāde kṣīrasya kiṃ jātaṃ yena tannopalabhyate | bālabhāṣitametat 
nāśa eva, naṣṭatvānnopalabhyate, tannāśātmakaṃ hi dadhi- ityuktam | 
nanu bhāvanivṛttyātmaka evābhāvaḥ nāstīha ghaṭaḥ ityādāvapi 
tathādarśanāt | satyam, nāśastu na tadātmako'pi tu vastvantararūpaḥ 
pratyakṣasiddha ityuktam, bhāvanivṛttimātraṃ tvabhāvo'tyantāvastutvāt,
