na pramāṇaviṣayo'pi tu 
deśādiniyatapadārthāntarasaṃsargabhrāntivivekarūpo nāstīha ghaṭaḥ 
ityādau vyavahāramātraphalo vikalpakalpita eva | taduktaṃ bhavadbhireva-

rūpābhāvādabhāvasya śabdarūpābhidhāyinaḥ |
nāśaṅkyā eva siddhāste vyavacchedasya vācakāḥ || iti |

	na tasya kāryatā, api tu anartharūpasyaivābhāvasyeti na 
vināśahetorasāmarthyaṃ vaiyarthyaṃ vā doṣa iti ||

	ye tu vināśaḥ svabhāva eva bhāvasya tato vināśasvabhāvādeva 
bhāvo naśyati na tu hetvantarāt tenāpyatatsvabhāvasya 
nāśayitumaśakyatvāt, ata eva ca 
kṛtakatvānityatvayostādātmyādavyabhicāraḥ sidhyati nānyathā | 
taduktam-

ahetutvādvināśasya svabhāvādanubandhitā |
sāpekṣāṇāṃ hi dharmāṇāṃ nāvaśyaṃ bhāviteṣyate ||

ityāhustān pratyāha-

nāpyarthādapṛthagyasmādarthavannopalabhyate || 45 ||

	yo hi arthasvabhāvaḥ sa tasmin niṣpanne niṣpanna eva śiṃśapātva 
iva vṛkṣatvam | yastu yānniṣpattāvaniṣpanno na sa tatsvabhāvo gotva iva 
vṛkṣatvam | na ca arthaniṣpattau tannāśo vastu avastu vā 
tadātmanyupalabhyate'tyantaviruddhatvena 
parasparaparihāreṇāvasthānatastādātmyāsambhavāt | yaduktamācaryeṇa 
paramokṣanirāsakārikāsu-

viruddhāvekakālasthau dharmāvekāśrayaṃ gatau |
itaretaranāśāttau kuruto lopamātmanaḥ || iti |

	tanniṣpattāvaniṣpatternārthasvabhāvo nāśaḥ | nanu dvitīyasmin 
kṣaṇe sa tasya bhavati- iti kathamasvabhāvaḥ, ata eva kāryatvāt | nanu-

hetvantarānapekṣitvātsa svabhāvo'nuvarṇitaḥ | iti |

	tarhi sāmayika eṣa svabhāva iti na tato vastusiddhiranyathāpi 
sambhavato'navasthāprasaṅgāt- iti kṛtakatvena nāśasya 
tādātmyapratibandhābhāvādatādātmye cāvaśyaṃ 
bhavānabhyupagamādananumānaṃ tadvināśayogyatā kṛtakasya vastunaḥ 
svabhāvo na nāśastato naiṣa doṣa iti | yadyevamasmatpakṣa eva || 45 ||

	na ca vyabhicāro'numānasyetyāha-

abhedakārakaṃ yadvadarthasiddhyai pravartate |
tannāśāya tathaiveti nāśo jātasya jāyate || 46 ||

	yathaiva sahakārikāraṇaṃ daṇḍacakrasūtrādi ghaṭāderarthasya 
sādhanāya pravartate tathaiva tasya karparādyātmakanāśakaraṇāya 
mudgarādi sahakārikāraṇaṃ pravartate- iti yat kṛtakaṃ tat 
sahakārisannidhāvavaśyaṃ naśyati, yastu na naśyati nāsau kṛtako 
yathātmādiḥ, ityanvayavyatirekasadbhāvāt kṛtakatvenānityatvaṃ 
śabdādāvanumātuṃ yuktam, na bhavatāṃ vyatirekāsambhavataḥ 
kevalānvayino'sya gamakatvāsiddheḥ | nanu naśvarasvabhāvasya kiṃ 
mudgarādyapekṣā iti yaduktaṃ tadayuktaṃ mudgarādisannidhāveva tasya 
rūpāntarāpattisvabhāvatvāt | 
nanvanupakāriṇo'pekṣāyogānmudgarādyupakāre tu sa pūrvaḥ 
svabhāvata eva ghaṭo naṣṭo'yamanyastadupakṛtaḥ samutpannaḥ- iti kṣaṇika 
eva | na, tathāsvabhāvatvādityuktam | svahetorhi sa tathābhūtasvabhāvo 
bhāvo bhavati yaḥ sarvadānupakārakamudgarādisannidhau vikāramupayāti 
nānyathā, ityatra tathādarśanameva pramāṇam | na ca svabhāvā 
bhāvanāṃ paryanuyogamarhanti | etena 
tathābhūtasahakārisannidhāvarthakriyākaraṇasvabhāvatvāt sthirasya 
krameṇārthakriyāvirodhaḥ parihāryaḥ | nanu sahakāryasannidhau 
janakādbhinnastasya svabhāvo jananābhāvādeva | na, tadāpi 
sahakārisannidhau kāryakaraṇasvabhāvatvāt, tatsannidhau tu sa eveti na 
bhedaḥ, na hi asmāt svabhāvāt kālatraye'pi tasyānyathābhāvaḥ, 
sahakāryasannidhānāttu na prāk

kāryodbhavaḥ- iti tatsannidhyasannidhī paraṃ bhidyete na vastubhedaḥ | nanu 
sannidhyasannidhī tarhi sahakāriṇo bhedaṃ vidadhāte 
vidadhatorviruddhasvabhāvatvāt | na tatrāpi virodhastasyāpi 
sannidhāpakasvasahakārisannidhau tatsannidhisvabhāvatvāt tasyāpi 
svasahakārisannidhau tasya tasyāpi, iti nāniyamaḥ 
svabhāvaniyamitatvādbhāvānāṃ vaiśvarūpyasyeti sahakāriṇāmapi 
kṣityādīnāṃ bījādikāraṇasannidhau tatkāryakāraṇastu asau tatsadṛśa 
eva svabhāvato yena tatsannidhāna eva tadaṅkurādikāryaṃ kurvanti 
nānyadā- iti sarveṣāṃ tatkārakatvaṃ svabhāvaḥ 
parasparasannidhānāpekṣaḥ- iti sarve kārakāḥ sahakāripadavācyā 
naikakāḥ | na ca teṣāṃ svabhāvabhedaḥ, sarveṣāṃ ca sāmarthye'pi na 
kāryānekatvaprasaṅgaḥ parasparasannidhau 
tadekakāryanirvartakasvabhāvatvāt, evaṃ nānābhūtārthakriyākaraṇe 
nānābhūtatattatsahakārisannidhau 
nānābhūtānekakāryakartrekasvabhāvatvāt, krameṇa 
nānābhūtārthakriyākaraṇe'pi na svabhāvabhedo yathaikasyaiva caitrasya 
lavanapacanabhojanādyanekakāryakartustattachātrādisahakārisannidhau 
tattallavanapacanādikāryakartṛsvabhāvatvāt krameṇa 
lavanapacanādibhāve'pi na svabhāvabhedastathābhūtasvabhāvasya 
tatrākrameṇaiva bhāvāt pradīpa iva | pradīpasya hi 
vartidāhatailakṣapaṇaprakāśasvajñānādyanekakāryanirvartakaḥ 
svabhāvo'bhyupagato bhavadbhirna tu kāryabhedena svabhāvabhedaḥ- iti 
darśitaḥ prāgeva, tadvadatrāpi sahakārisannidhau 
tatkāryakartṛsvabhāvāviyogāt sarvadaiva sthirasya bhāvasya | 
sahakārisannidhānasya tu kramikatvāt krameṇānekakāryanirvartane'pi na 
bhedastatsvabhāvābhedāt, svarūpasya tu bhedasiddhau hi 
nañarthānupraveśaparamāstrā bauddhāḥ, na ca 
sarvadaikasvabhāvadharmāchāditānāṃ sa bhavati- iti na sthirasya 
kramayaugapadyābhyāmarthakriyānupapattiriti || 46 ||

	nanvakāraṇa eva kārakāntarādvātmāderapi vināśo bhaviṣyati, iti 
vyatirekāsiddheryuṣmākamapyanaikāntiko'yaṃ heturityāha-

vyatireke'pi viṣayo yastasyāsau na jāyate |
virodhitvena kiṃ kurmo dṛṣṭe bādhā na vidyate || 47 ||

	vyatirekanimittaṃ yastasya vyatirekasya viṣaya ātmādistasya nāśo 
nopapadyate virodhitvāditi | ayamarthaḥ- yasyaiva yathā nāśena saha 
virodhaḥ pramāṇena siddhastasyaivāsau viruddhatvāt śītasyeva 
kāraṇāntarādbhavadauṣṇyamauṣṇyasya vā śītaṃ nivartakamanumīyate, 
yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

avikalakāraṇasya bhavato'nyabhāve'bhāvādvirodhagatiḥ iti 
nāviruddhasya | kāraṇācca ghaṭāderutpattimataśca hetumatā saha virodhaḥ 
pratyakṣeṇa siddhaḥ, iti tathā bhūtasyaivāsāvanumānena sambhāvayituṃ 
yukto nānutpattimato'hetukaścātmādeḥ | nahi pratyakṣadṛṣṭasya 
kvacidanyathātvaṃ yuktaṃ dhūmādīnāmapi deśāntare'nagnito'pi 
bhāvasambhāvanayā sarvānumānābhāvaprasaṅgāt | atha 
nātadbhāvinaḥ sakṛdapi tato bhāvāt, iti vastuvyavasthitamityucyate, tarhi 
akṛtakasyātmāderahetukanāśakalpanā kalpanāmātrameva ato 
vyatireko'sya siddhaḥ, iti nānaikāntikatā || 47 ||

	etadeva darśayati-

vināśahetusadbhāvavyāpte sarvatra vastuni |
kṛtakatvamanityatvaṃ nāto vyabhicariṣyati || 48 ||

	sato bhāva utpādastena vyāpte sarvasminnutpattimati vastuni 
vināśahetuḥ śītasyevauṣṇyaṃ yato vāstavena pratibandhena sarvatra 
sarvadā vyāpakatvena sidhyati tataḥ kṛtakatvākhyo hetuḥ śabdādau 
dharmiṇyanityatvaṃ sādhayatyeva na tvanaikāntikaḥ | yadāhuḥ-

avaśyaṃbhāvaniyamaḥ kaḥ parasyānyathā paraiḥ |
arthāntaranimitte vā dharme vāsasi rāgavat || iti |

	yato na vāsasyapi rāgo'numīyate'pi tu rāgayogyatā- ityavyabhicāra 
eva | yadyevaṃ kṛtakaḥ kaścinna vinaśyediti | na ata eva 
mahāpralayasiddheḥ | api ca nāśayogyatāsya sādhyā na nāśaḥ, ityuktaṃ 
tasyāścāvyabhicāra eva | yathā hi bījasyāṅkurajananayogyatānumāne'pi 
kadācit kākādibhakṣaṇādaṅkurajananābhāve'pi anumānamaduṣṭameva 
ghaṭādivadatyantāyogyatvanirākaraṇaphalatvāt tasya, 
tathehātmādivadatyantanāśāyogyatānirākaraṇāya ghaṭāderarthasya 
nāśayogyatānumīyate, ata eva cārtharakṣādau nītiśāstrāṇāṃ 
pravṛttirnayavidaśca nipuṇā dṛśyante nānyathetyadoṣa || 48 ||

	tadevaṃ grāhakātmano'pi 
kṣaṇikatvāsiddheraihikaphalaviṣayatayāpi pravṛttiphalabhoktṛtvaṃ 
pratijñāsūtrapadopātaṃ vyākhyātam | adhunā yaḥ pumān iti 
padadvayaṃ vyākhyātuṃ yaiḥ indriyasaṅgha eva cetano nānyaḥ kaścit 
puruṣo nāma tatraivāhaṃ pratyayasya dṛṣṭeḥ ityucyate tān pratyāha-

kāntāyāmiva boddhavyo bodho'hamiti vai tanau |
jānātyeva pumānyasmādiyaṃ me bhogasādhikā || 49 ||

	caitanyaṃ tāvat svasākṣikamanubhavasiddhaṃ ityuktam, yadi ca 
saivendriyasaṃhatirūpā tanuḥ, ityucyate | bhavatu nāmāntarameva tat tato 
nārthāntaram, na ca nāmni vivādo viduṣām | atha 
rūpādisaṃvittyanyathānupapattyā cetanena svātmanaḥ karaṇatayā 
yaścakṣurādisamūho'numīyate sendriyasaṃhatiḥ, ityucyate tadā 
karaṇatvenaiva tasyāḥ siddherna cetanātmakakartṛrūpatvena 
prāgpradarśitāhaṃpratyayaviṣayatā yuktā atyantabhedāt | nanu 
hantyātmānamātmanā itivat abhinne'pi kalpito'tra kartṛkaraṇādibhedo 
bhaviṣyati, iti | satyaṃ yadyabhedaścaitanyenāsyāḥ siddho bhavet, 
proktānumānena tu bheda eva siddhaḥ, ityayuktametat | nanu-

ahaṃ cakṣurahaṃ śrotraṃ sarvendriyatanustvaham |

ityādiśrutisiddho'trābhedo bhaviṣyati, iti |

ahaṃ jāyāhyahaṃ putro'haṃ gauraśvā dhanānyaham | 

	itivat pratyakṣādivirodhād 
gauṇatvenāpyāgamasyopapatterindriyacaitanikapakṣo'tyantāyukta eva || 49 ||

	bhūtacaitanikapakṣe'pyāha-

bhinnalakṣmā tu cidbhāvaḥ kṣmādernāpyaciterapi |
yaddṛṣṭervimatiḥ kṣmādeḥ pūrvavyakteḥ svasaṃsthitaḥ || 50 ||

	cidātmako bhāvacidrūpo'rtho nācetanasya pṛthivyādeḥ svabhāvo 
yuktastato bhinnalakṣaṇo yataḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati cetanācetanayoḥ 
parasparaparihārasthiralakṣaṇatvādghaṭatadabhāvayoriva naikatvam | kiñca 
yat yasmāt pṛthivyādeḥ sambandhinī yā dṛṣṭiḥ pṛthivyādiviṣayaṃ 
pratyakṣaṃ jñānaṃ tasyāḥ sakāśāccidbhāvasya vimatiḥ sattāsaṃśayo 
dṛśyate tatastayornaikatvam, yo hi yadātmakaḥ sa na tanniścayena niścita eva 
yathā ghaṭaniścaye mṛdādiḥ | yastu yanniścaye 
vimatyādhikaraṇabhāvāpanno nāsau tadātmako yathā ghaṭaniścaye 
piśācaḥ, śarīrākārapṛthivyādiniścaye ca sandigdhaścidrūpo'rthaḥ, iti 
na tadātmakaḥ | na cāyamasiddho hetuḥ 
kāyavāgvyavahārātmanānumānena paracitsvabhāvaḥ siddhaḥ, yataḥ sa 
citsvabhāvo'pi uktanayena grāhakatayā pūrvā pratyagrūpā yāsau tasya 
vyaktiḥ prakāśastataḥ svata ātmanaiva

grāhyasvabhāvāt purorūpāt svaśarīrātmanaḥ pṛthivyāderbhedena 
saṃsthitaḥ dhāraṇādivṛttyupalakṣitāt svaśarīrākārāt pṛthivyādibhūta-
pañcakādgrāhyātmano'tyantavilakṣaṇastadgrāhakarūpo'yamarthaḥ 
svataḥsiddho yato'to na bhūtasvabhāva ityarthaḥ | ata eva 
lokāyatapratikṣepeṇāsyāgatārthatā | tatra hi bhūtakāryatvenānityatvaṃ 
pratikṣiptam, atra bhūtavannityatve'pi tadātmatvamiti bhedaḥ || 50 ||

	antaḥkaraṇacaitanikāḥ sāṅkarṣaṇapāñcarātrāstu āhuḥ- 
kalpayitvāmapi puruṣaṃ bhavadbhirdharmajñānādidharmāṣṭakayukta-
mantaḥkaraṇamabhyupagatameva tadantaḥkaraṇamevobhayavādisiddhaṃ 
jñānarūpamātmatveneṣyatām, 
tadvyatiriktasyājñānarūpasyātmatvāsiddherjñānātmatve ca 
jñānadvayānubhavaprasaṅgataḥ pratyakṣavirodhaḥ, iti tānpratyāha-

ācaitanyātra vijñānaṃ karaṇe'ntarvyavasthite |

	bhavedetat yadyantaḥkaraṇamapi cetanamasmābhirabhyupagataṃ syāt 
tattu kāryatvād ghaṭādivadacetanameva, na ca kāryatvamasiddham, 
bhavadbhirapi mahāvibhūtikāryatvena tasyābhyupagamāt | atha 
tasmādanyadeva tannityamityucyate, tarhyātmaivāsau siddha iti 
nāntaḥkaraṇacaitanyavādaḥ |

kathaṃ tarhyantaḥkaraṇasya jñānaṃ dharmo bhavadbhirukta ityāha-

caitanyavyaktibhūmitvāttadvṛttirjñānamucyate || 51 ||

	jñāyate'nayā smṛtipratibhāsādāvasannihito'rthaḥ puruṣeṇeti 
kṛtvāntaḥkaraṇavṛttirupacārājjñānamucyate na tu mukhyataḥ | 
bodhātmano'pariṇāmitvena saṃskārāsambhavatastadānīmarthā-
sambhavāccānubhūyamānākārānupapatterantaḥkaraṇameva 
saṃskārākārarūpamabhyupagantavyaṃ yataḥ, evaṃ 
dharmādirūpatvamapyantaḥkaraṇasya tatsaṃskārarūpatayā na tu 
jyotiṣṭomādikriyātmakadharmādirūpatve neti vipañcitamasmābhiḥ 
svāyambhuvodyote tata evāvadhāryam- ityantaḥkaraṇasya dharmādimato 
jñānānabhyupagamāt puruṣa eva jñānātmakaḥ siddha iti yaḥ pumān iti 
padadvayaṃ vyākhyātam |

	prāgabhedasiddhau puṃsaḥ pramāṇaṃ nāstītyuktamadhunā tu 
bhedasiddhau pramāṇamāha-

ekasminneva kāle yat pratiliṅgaṃ sukhādayaḥ |
vibhinnā upalabhyante tenāṇubhinna iṣyate || 52 ||

	svasaṃvedanena hi pratiśarīraṃ svātmā sukhādyanubhavitṛtvena 
paraśarīraceṣṭādibhiśca duḥkhādiyuktātmāntarānumātṛkatvena 
yugapadeva saṃvedyate, na cānumeyo'numātānumātāvānumeya

eva yugapadeva sambhavatyantabhedādityuktam | atastayorbheda eva yuktaḥ | na 
ca parātmānumātṛtvaṃ tadānīmasiddhamasatyaṃ vā yujyate vaktuṃ 
svasaṃvedanasiddhatvātsukhādiprakāśakatvavat | nāpi 
tadālambano'sāvasatyo bādhakābhāvātsukhādivadityātmabhedaḥ satya 
eva || 52 ||

atra vedāntavidaḥ parasyābhiprāyamāha-

nāṇubhedaḥ sukhādīnāṃ kintu sādhanabhedataḥ |
bhedaścet

	pratiśarīramekatve'pi ātmano yathā grīṣmātapadahyamānava-
puṣo'kasmātsamprāptaśītatarajalanimagnādhaḥkāyasya 
yugapadevātapajalādisādhanabhedādūrdhvādhobhāvinoḥ sukhaduḥkhayo 
saṃvedanameva śarīrabhede'pi tasya bhaviṣyatīti |

	atrottaramāha-

	sarvaduḥkhādāvekā saṃvidbhavettataḥ || 53 ||

	yathā pratiśarīramekatvādātmano'nekasukhaduḥkhādiviṣayameka-
meva saṃvedanaṃ saṃvedyate, tathā yadi sarvaśarīreṣveka evātmā 
syāttataḥ sarvasukhaduḥkhādiviṣayamekameva saṃvedanaṃ syāt 
tadrūpatvādevātmanaḥ- iti sarvasya sarvajñatvaprasaṅgaḥ parasparaṃ 
bhedābhāvāt | satyamekaiva saṃvit bhrāntinibandhanastu bheda iti cet, na, 
asiddhatvāt | nanu svapnādāvasyāḥ siddha eva tannibandhano bhedaḥ | 
yaduktam-

na cetsyāt svapnapuruṣo bhramasyāsya nidarśanam | 
satyaṃ tadanyarūpeṇa pratibhātīti ko vadet || iti |

	na, tatrāpi saṃvidaḥ svātmani bhramābhāvājjāgratīva 
pratiśarīraṃ sukhādisaṃvidbhyo bhedaḥ satya eva | bhrāntaṃ hi 
vijñānaṃ sarvamālambane bhrāntam, na svātmani | ālambanaṃ 
hyanyathā bahiḥsthitamanyathā pratīyamānaṃ bhramaviṣayatāmāpadyate | 
yatpunarbodhaikasvabhāvaṃ vastu tadyathaiva cakāsti tathaiva 
sadatatsvabhāvasya svasaṃvedyatve ca tatsvabhāvatvamiti svaśarīravartina 
eva sukhādivedanasyābhedaḥ, na tu 
paraśarīravartino'numānasiddhasyānumātṛtayaiva tato bhedena 
saṃvedanādityātmabhedaḥ satya evetyuktam | 
nanvitaretarābhāvasavyapekṣatvādbhedasyābhāvāviṣayatvācca 
pratyakṣasya na bhedaḥ svasaṃvitpratyakṣagamyaḥ | taduktam-

āhurvidhātṛ pratyakṣaṃ na niṣeddhṛ vipaścitaḥ | iti |

netaretarābhāvasavyapekṣo bhedaḥ, kiṃ tarhi siddhe bhede 
taditaretarābhāvasiddhiḥ | sarvaikye hi na kasyacit kutaścidabhāva iti 
ghaṭārthī paṭadarśanāt kimiti nivartate, pramāṇāpramāṇavibhāgaśca na 
syāt sarvasya sattāmātrāvyabhicārāt- iti svasaṃvedanena 
parātmānumātṛtayā svātmābhedena prakāśamānaḥ satya eva, nahi 
svasaṃvitsiddhamavastu aparavedyatvād brahmavat- iti bhinnā evātmāna iti 
siddham || 53 ||

	pariṇativedāntavidaḥ saṃhitāpāñcarātrāścāhuḥ- satyaṃ bhinnā 
eva jīvātmānaḥ, te tu paramakāraṇādanaśvarād brahmapadavācyāt, yadi 
vā nāratyaṇākhyād avyāpakā eva ghaṭādivat 
svakāraṇalayasvabhāvāścotpadyante- iti | tānapi anumānena 
pratikṣipannāha-

caitanyādeva puruṣo na vikārī na cāvibhuḥ |
niyamenāvibhuryasmādvikārī cāpyacetanaḥ || 54 ||

	yaścetano nāsāvavyāpako layasvabhāvo vā tadyathā 
paramakāraṇam, yastu avyāpako layasvabhāvaśca nāsau cetano yathā 
ghaṭādiḥ, cetanaśca jīvākhyaprāṇasaṃyukto'yamātmā 
svasaṃvedanasiddho bhavadbhirabhyupagataḥ, iti nāvyāpako 
layasvabhāvaśca yuktaḥ, api tu nityavyāpakasvabhāva eva, 
dikkālayorgrahītṛtvena tadavacchinnatayā sarvadaivāvabhāsanācca | nahi 
grāhyeṇa ghaṭādinā grāhako'vacchettuṃ śakya ityuktam | na ca 
dikkālayorapi grāhyatāṃ muktvānyo grāhakaikasvabhāvenātmanā 
sambandhaḥ sambhavati | tadidamuktam-

anavacchinnasadbhāvaṃ vastu yaddeśakālataḥ |
tannityaṃ vibhu cecchantītyātmano vibhunityate || iti ||

	nanvevaṃ vyāpakatayātmano dūre'yamarthaḥ iti pratyayo na syāt, 
na ca śarīrāpekṣayeti vācyaṃ tasyāpramātṛtvāt, atyalpamidamucyate 
sarvasarvajñatāprasaṅga eva vācyaḥ | na, suryātapādermeghādivad 
bodhasyāvaraṇasambhavāt | yadyevaṃ kāryakaraṇādibhiḥ svasthāna eva 
tadvyudāsāttasyābhivyaktirityabhivyaktyapekṣayā 
dūratvādūratvapratibhāso'pyupapadyata eva ityadoṣaḥ || 54 ||

yadyapi avyāpako layasvabhāvaścāyamātmā 
sarvārthadarśanāsattvādasarvajñatvād ghaṭādivat, ityatrānumānaṃ 
bhavadbhirupanyastaṃ tadapyayuktamityāha-

vyatirekasya saṃhetāvanvayavyatirekiṇi |
sarvārthadarśanāsattvātsandigdhamubhayaṃ tava || 55 ||

	anvayavyatirekavati samyag hetau sarvārthadarśanāsattvāt, 
ityasminnabhimate bhavatāṃ paraṃ prati vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntasya 
paramakāraṇasyāsiddheḥ, īśvaramuktānāṃ nityavyāpakasvabhāvatvena 
svayamanabhyupagamāt sandigdho vyatirekaḥ |
	
	tasmiṃśca sandigdho saṃbandhirūpatvāt pūrvāparavadanvayasyāpi 
sandehāt, gomān aśvattvāt ityādāvivānaikāntikatā- ityahetutā, yo'pi 
kaiścid jīvātmanāmavyāpakatve layasvabhāvatve ca sattādinānvitatvāt, 
ghaṭādivaditi heturuktaḥ so'pyasiddha evātmanāmanvayāsiddheḥ | anvayo 
hi grāhyasya karmatāmāpannasya grahītuṃ śakyaḥ, na cātmā svātmana 
eva grāhyībhavati svātmani kriyāvirodhāt, sa hi grāhakatvenaiva 
pratyagrūpatayā sarvadā bhāsate- ityuktam |

	nanu tenaiva rūpeṇāsyānvayo'pi bhaviṣyatīti | na, 
tasyānekagrāhakaprakāśaviṣayatvāt, na caikaikātmasaṃvedane 
grāhakāntarasaṃvedanaṃ sambhavati tasya tato'nyatvena ghaṭādivat 
paratvāt, na hi ātmanāṃ parasparaṃ svasaṃvedanamātrāḥ saṅkīryante 
sarvasya sarvajñatvaprasaṅgāt- ityuktam | pratyekaṃ ca svātmavedane kasya 
kutrānvayaḥ | yadyevaṃ sarvajñaḥ sarvātmanāṃ grahīṣyatīti, 
tadapyayuktam, sarvajñātmanaiva vyabhicārāt | so'pi svasaṃvedanena 
parātmabhyo bhedenaiva tadgrahītṛtayā bhāsate yataḥ, na ca grahītari 
anvayaḥ sambhavatītyuktam | na ca tasya sarvajñatvādeva viparītagrahaṇaṃ 
sambhavati, tadyathā tenātmāntarebhya svātmā 
atyantānanvitarūpo'sabhavadbhrameṇa pratyakṣeṇa gṛhītastathaiva 
parātmānaḥ parasparabhinnāḥ samanvayaviṣayabhāvamatikramya 
vartamānāstena sākṣātkriyante- ityadoṣaḥ |

	ata evānumeyo'pi ātmanāmanvayo na sambhavati tadānīmanumeyāt 
anumāturanumātṛrūpatayā vaisādṛśyenaiva bhāsanāt svātmavat 
parātmanāmatyantavisadṛśatayaivānumānāt, tarhi asannātmā 
sattayānanvitatvādaśvaviṣāṇavat nāsya sattvānvayāt sattvamapi tu 
svarūpasattvāt sattāvat paramakāraṇavadvā, tasyāpi hi 
sattvānvayātkāryatvena paramakāraṇatvābhāvaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi 
anvayābhāve

sarvātmasvātmapadasya pravṛttiḥ | sādṛśyāt brahmaṇīva 
parātmaśabdasya |

	nanu tadapi agrāhyasvabhāve svātmani naiva grahītuṃ śakyam | 
satyam, vaisādṛśyaparihāreṇa tu kathañcidadūraviprakarṣeṇa 
vyavahārārthaṃ tat tatrocyate na paramārthataḥ | yat śrūyate neti 
netyātmagatiḥ iti darśitamasmābhirvistareṇa rauravavṛttiviveke 
paramokṣanirāsakārikāsu, iti tata evāvadhāryam | 
tadevamanvayākhyasyāsya hetorasiddhernāvyāpakatvaṃ layasvabhāvatvaṃ 
cātmanāmanumātuṃ yuktam, ityuktayuktyā nityavyāpakasvabhāvā eva 
te'bhyupagantavyā iti | yadyevaṃ sarvaśarīreṣu sarvātmanāṃ 
sannidhānād bhogasaṅkaraḥ | na, karmabhirniyamitatvāt | nanu teṣāmapi sa 
eva doṣaḥ | na, katṛbhedena bhedāt | kartṛtvaṃ hi cikīrṣāyogāt 
pratyātmaṃ bhidyate, nānyacikīrṣayā anyacikīrṣāvānupapadyate yataḥ, 
cikīrṣayaiva ca teṣāṃ kartṛtvamiti vakṣyāmaḥ || 55 ||

	adhunā sāṅkhyaṃ prati kartṛtvamātmanaḥ sādhayitumāha-

kṛtaṃ mayā karomīdaṃ kariṣyāmīti bodhataḥ |
vedaprāmāṇyataścāṇoḥ kartṛśaktistrikālagā || 56 ||

	kartṛtvaṃ tāvadidaṃ sarvānyakārakapravṛttinivṛttihetutvena 
svatantryātmakaṃ śarīrendriyaspandaṃ prāṇādipratyatnaṃ ca vidadhat 
sarvameva kṛṣyagnihotrādidṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalaṃ karma 
kurvadanubhūyamānamātmano na vidyate iti na śakyate vaktum, 
śarīrādikārakāntaprerakatvena pratyagrūpatayādhyātmaṃ 
jñātṛtvavadanubhavāt | nahi jñātṛtvasyādhyātmasvabhāvatve'nyadataḥ 
kāraṇamastītyuktam, jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajet iti 
viśiṣṭādhikārikartavyatopadeśakatvena śruterbhavadbhirapi 
pramāṇatvenābhyupagamāt | na cākartuḥ pāṣāṇatulyasya 
kāryatopadeśaḥ pramāṇatāmāsādayati, tatpramāṇānyathānupapattyāpi 
ca nāvidyamānaṃ puṃsaḥ kartṛtvamiti śakyaṃ vaktum | vidyamānaṃ ca 
tannityātmasvabhāvatvāt, kṛtaṃ mayetyādi sarvakālagatatvena ca 
parāmarśaviṣayatvāt, jñātṛtvavat nityamabhyupagantavyam | nanu 
prakṛtereva kartṛtvaṃ tadavivekāttu ātmani tadadhyāropaḥ, iti sāṃkhyāḥ 
| yadyevaṃ jñātṛtvamapi tasyā eva iti kṛtaṃ vyatiriktātmavādena | nanu 
prakṛterasyāḥ kāryakāraṇādirūpāyāḥ sarvadaiva jñeyatvena siddheḥ 
jñātṛtvaṃ nopapadyata eva | yadyevaṃ kāryatvenāsyāstathaiva siddheḥ 
kartṛtvamapi nopapadyate | na, svakāryaviṣayasya prakṛteḥ 
kartṛtvasyābhyupagamāt | yaduktam-

		prakṛtermahāṃstato'haṅkāraḥ | iti |

	na, jaḍatvenopādānakāraṇatvaṃ tatra tasyā na kartṛtvam, anyad hi 
upādānakāraṇatvaṃ svarūpānyathābhāvarūpaṃ 
jaḍavastusvabhāvatayaiva 
pariṇāmitvādyavyabhicārikṣīradadhyādiṣvadhyakṣasiddham, anyacca 
kartṛtvaṃ hitāhitaprāptipyāgaphalalipsayā 
sarvānyakārakapravṛttinivṛttikāraṇatvena svātantryātmakaṃ 
cidvastvavyabhicāri svasaṃvedanasiddhamiti kartṛtvasyāpi 
tatrāsiddherjñātṛtvasyeva ātmadharmataiveti | yaduktam-

pravṛttau ca nivṛttau ca kārakāṇāṃ ya īśvaraḥ |
apravṛttaḥ pravṛttau vā sa kartā nāma kārakaḥ || iti |

	nanu kartṛtvasyābodharūpatvena svasaṃvedyatvāsiddheḥ 
ghaṭāderiva nātmadharmatā, yadyevaṃ nityatvavyāpakatvāderapi 
taddharmatvena siddhasyaiva prasaṅgaḥ samānaḥ iti nairātmyam | na, tasya 
savitṛprakāśādivat tasmādabhedenaiva siddheriti cet, tarhi dāhakatvamiva 
savitṛprakāśāt bodhātmanaḥ kartṛtvamavibhinnameva 
svasaṃvedanasiddhimiti nātatsvabhāvam | taduktaṃ mokṣakārikāsu-

sarvajñānakriyārūpā śaktirekaiva śūlinaḥ |
kriyā tridhā tu vāmādyā gītā vyāpārabhedataḥ || iti || 56 ||

	api ca-

syātāṃ bījaikakartṛtve kṛtanāśākṛtāgamau |

	jagadupādānakāraṇasya prakṛtyātmana evaikasya kartṛtve 
pākādivyāpāreṇa idamodanādi kṛtaṃ mayā ityātmagatatvena 
kartṛtvatatparāmarśayorabhāvaprasaṅgaḥ syāt, akṛtasyaiva caudanādeḥ 
kutāścidaindrajālikopanītasyevāgamaḥ, iti sarvasya sarvadā sarvatra 
pratipattiḥ syāt tato dṛṣṭe'pi karmaphale 
pratyabhijñābhāvaprasaṅgato'nubhavavirodhaḥ |

	atra parābhiprāyamāha-

pariṇāmaprasaṅgaścet

	puṃsaḥ kartṛtve pariṇāmitā spandātmakatvāt kartṛtvasya |

etat pratikṣipati-

				na maṇyādāvadarśanāt || 57 ||

na spandātmakaṃ kartṛtvaṃ kriyārūpatvāt spandasya, na ca kriyā rūpaṃ 
kartuḥ, api tu tatra śaktatvamayaskāntamaṇerivāyaspandajanana 
ityuktamasmābhiranyatra-

jaḍaspandakriyāṇāṃ yā śaktiḥ sā kartṛtātmanaḥ |
vyāpteraspandarūpeṇa siddhāyaskāntavatsvataḥ || iti |

	nanu jaḍatvānmaṇyādīnāṃ kartṛtvamapariṇāmitvaṃ 
cāsiddhameva | satyam,  śaktatvenaiva kārakatvaṃ na spandātmakatvena- 
ityetāvanmātratvāt atropakṣepasyādoṣaḥ | tadevaṃ kartā ca ityetadapi 
pratijñāpadaṃ vyākhyātamiti || 57 ||

	taditayā yathāpratijñātasyātmano darśanāntaranirākaraṇena 
samyak parīkṣā vidhāyādhunā yuktimapi tathā parīkṣituṃ yairmuktau 
śivasamatvaśrutyanyathānupapattyāsyātmanaḥ sarvajñatvādyupapadyate- 
ityucyate tān pratikṣipati-

jñātaṃ mayā vijānāmi jñāsyāmīti prabodhataḥ |
trikālaviṣayā puṃso bodhaśaktistadāpi sā || 58 ||

	yadyatra nāsti tattatrotpadyetāpi āmalakādāviva paścātpītatvam, 
asti ca puṃsastrikālaviṣayaṃ jñatvādi prāgapīti na tattadotpadyate tadeva 
tu sarvajñatvamiti utpattimattve ca ghaṭāderivānityatvāt mukteranirmokṣaḥ |

	nanu sarvaviṣayaṃ jñānamadhunānupalabhyamānatvāttadotpatsyate, 
ityāha-

jñānaśaktistu cicchaktiḥ sā vyaktā jñānamucyate |
vyaktirāvaṇāpāye puṃśaktyorarthasaṅgamaḥ || 59 ||

	jñānaśaktireva cicchaktiḥ, sā ca 
vakṣyamāṇasyāvidyādyāvaraṇasyāpāye'rthaprakāśanapravṛttau 
jñānamucyate, na tu jñānatacchaktyorbhedaḥ sarvadaikarūpeṇa 
grāhakātmanaḥ saṃvedanāt nārthabhedena bheda ityuktamato na 
tasyotpādastadeti || 59 ||

	kastarhi baddhābaddhāvasthayorviśeṣastasyā ityāha-

bhave pāśoparaktā sā muktau pāśābhibhāvikā |
niyatārthā bhave muktau sarvārthā vyañjakāśrayāt || 60 ||

	cakṣuṣastamolakṣaṇasyevāvaraṇasya sadbhāvāsadbhāvakṛto 
viśeṣo'syāḥ viṣayālpatvamahattvātmako'pi idānīṃ kalādermuktau 
īśvarātmanaśca vyañjakasyālpatvamahattvakṛto na tu svarūpabhedakṛta iti 
muktau jñānakriyayoḥ sarvaviṣayatvasyābhivyakterīśvarasamatvaṃ na tu 
utpatteriti || 60 ||

atha trikālaviṣayameva caitanyaṃ puṃsaḥ prāṅneṣyata iti, tatrāpyāha-

trikālaviṣayaṃ puṃsaścaitanyaṃ yadi neṣyate |
kālāderiva bhedastu tatastasya prasajyate || 61 ||

	sarvasyāsya meyarāśeḥ kālatrayeṇa vyāptatvāt tasya ca 
pūrvāparāderiva sambandhirūpatvenānyatarāgrahaṇe grahaṇāsambhavāt 
tadvyāpyasyāpi asya meyasyāsiddheḥ kālakālādermāyīyasyeva 
ajñatvaṃ puṃsa ityavaśyaṃ trikālaviṣayaṃ prāgapi tasya 
caitanyamabhyupagantavyam, tato muktau tasyābhivyaktireva yuktā notpattiriti 
|| 61 ||

	pāśupatāstu muktasyeśvaraguṇasaṅkrāntestatsamatvamāhuḥ, anye 
punarīśvaraguṇāveśādbhūtāviṣṭapuruṣavaditi | tān pratyāha-

viśeṣaḥ śaktitaśceti nānyo'sau patiśaktitaḥ |
kriyāśaktiryato'rthānāṃ dṛṣṭā yā cidvatāmapi || 62 ||

	proktanayena muktasyāyaṃ sarvajñatvādilakṣaṇo viśeṣaḥ 
parameśvaraśaktibhya ityayamapi pakṣo na yuktaḥ yenānyo'sau ātmā 
tābhyaḥ śaktibhyo bhinnacetanatvena pūrvaṃ siddheḥ || 62 ||

	kriyāśaktistasyāpītyuktaṃ kimata ityāha-

saṃjñānaṃ bhavato'pyantastattvasaṃjñamanaśvaram |
saṃskṛteḥ pratipakṣaḥ syātsaṃvidaścāpyasambhavāt || 63 ||

	tathāpi etadantaḥsaṃvijñānātmakaṃ 
jñatvakartṛtvarūpamātmatattvākhyamavinaśvaraṃ tanmuktau saṃskārāt 
hetoḥ pratipakṣaḥ syāt naśvaraṃ bhavet saṃsārabhāvinyā api 
saṃvido'syāḥ kriyāyāścābhāvāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati- 
yattatsaṃsārāvasthāyāṃ tasyātmīyaṃ jñatvakartṛtvamabhūt 
tanmuktyavasthāyāmapi yadyasti amukta eva tadā iti 
kimīśvaraguṇasaṅkrāntyādinā kalpitena, atha vinaṣṭamityucyate na tarhi 
nityaḥ puruṣo jñatvakartṛtvayorvināśāt tadvyatirekeṇānyasya 
puṃso'saṃvedanācceti svāgamavirodhaḥ || 63 ||

	sarvāgamavirodhaścātretyāha-

mahājñāneṣu sarveṣu nityāṇorbhāṣitā citiḥ |
sarvāgamavirodhitvāttadasthairyaṃ na yuktimat || 64 ||

nahi draṣṭurdṛṣṭermanturvā mateḥ parilopo vidyate ityāgamanirasto'yaṃ 
pakṣaḥ | yaduktam-

	ekasya pratibhātaistu kṛtakānna viśiṣyate | iti || 64 ||

	yuṣmadāgamasiddho'yamartho bhaviṣyatītyāha-

sarvajñānakriyāvyaktirmuktiḥ śaive'pi bhāṣitāḥ |
sāpyevaṃ yujyate puṃsā nitye jñānakriye yadi || 65 ||

	yaduktaṃ śrīmadraurave-

sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā ca śivaśaktisamanvitaḥ | ityādi |

vistareṇa ca saṅkrāntipakṣanirāsaḥ paramokṣanirāsakārikāsu 
ācāryeṇa darśita iti tata evāvadhāryaḥ || 65 ||

yo'pi 
buddhisukhaduḥkhecchādveṣaprayatnadharmādharmasaṃskārāṇāṃ 
navānāmātmaguṇānāmatyantābhāvo mokṣa iti 
naiyāyikavaiśeṣikairucyate, yaśca prakṛtiviyogo mokṣa iti sāṃkhyairuktaḥ 
so'pyukta ityāha-

na jñānarahite mokṣe tadupāye pravartanam |
na hi kaścitpadārthajño mohasiddhyai pravartate || 66 ||

	sarvo hi prekṣāpūrvakārī hitāhitayoḥ svarūpaṃ nirūpya 
tatprāptityāgāya sādhanaviśeṣe pravartate, na vyasaniyatā | na ca svātmano 
jñānanāśo mūrchādyavasthāpāṣāṇāditulyatvaṃ 
kasyacidanunmattasya hitatvenāvabhāsata iti na kasyacidapi tadupāyabhūte 
śāstrādau pravṛttirutpadyata iti, anarthakameva tat | nanu nidrāyāṃ 
pravṛttirdṛśyata eva | satyam, svapnapratyayarūpatvāttasyā na doṣaḥ | 
yadāhuḥ-

vṛttayaḥ pañcatayyaḥ kliṣṭākliṣṭāḥ |
pramāṇaviparyayavikalpanidrāsmṛtayaḥ || iti |

	suṣuptātmakatve'pi dhātusāmyādijanitaśarīrapuṣṭyātmakāntara-
sparśahetukasukhaduḥkhādyupalambhāt sukhamahamasvāpsam ityādi 
parataḥ parāmarśāt prāṇapreraṇādiprayatnaviśeṣayogāccāsyāḥ 
pratyayarūpataiva | yadāhuḥ-

preraṇākarṣaṇe vāyoḥ prayatnena vinā kutaḥ | iti || 66 ||

	nanu atyantaduḥkhābhitaptānāṃ 
bhṛgvagnyanaśanāmbubhirmaraṇaṃ dṛśyata ityāha-

duḥkhanāśāya no yuktaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhānvitaṃ varam |

	teṣāmapi na duḥkhanāśāyaiva tapasi tasmin pravartanam, api tu 
tasmāt tapaso viśiṣṭamāmutrikaṃ sukhātmakamapi 
phalamanubhavitumāgamatastathāpratīteriti varaṃ saṃsāro'smānmokṣāt 
na tu ajñatvamapuruṣārthatvād apravṛtyaṅgaṃ mokṣa ityabhyupagamya 
caitaduktam, na tu ātmasvabhāvatvāt nityatvavyāpakatvayoriva 
jñānasyābhāvo brahmaṇāpi śakyaḥ kartumityuktaṃ prāk |
	pradīpanirvāṇavādinastu śākyāḥ sarvaiva vijñānavāsanā 
sākṣāt pāramparyeṇa cāvidyātṛṣṇānubandhitvāt duḥkhātmikaiva, na tu 
kadācidapi sukharūpeti sarvaduḥkhavāsanāvicchedāyaiva pravartamānāḥ 
kāraṇanivṛttidvāreṇārthāt kāryātmanaḥ svajñānasantānasya 
paranirvāṇāya mumukṣavaḥ paryavasyanti, ataḥ śūnya eva mokṣaḥ, 
ityāhuḥ | tān pratyāha-

vāsanā sarvaduḥkhā syānna bhavaddarśanaṃ vinā || 67 ||

	yuṣmaddarśanavyatirekeṇa kasyānyasyaitat saubhāgyaṃ yat 
bhavakāle sarvaṃ duḥkham, śūnyastu atyantābhāvo mokṣa iti | 
pratyakṣānumānabādhito'yaṃ pakṣaḥ prākpratipādita iti 
upahāso'nekajanmaduḥkhānubhavatatparityāgakleśairapi 
lokāyataphalasyaiva bhavatāṃ prāpyatvāt | taduktamācāryeṇa 
paramokṣanirāsakārikāsu-
	mūḍhānāṃ jyeṣṭhamallāste yeṣāṃ svātmā vinaśyati | iti |

	eṣa ca prasaṅgo vedāntavidāṃ pāñcarātrāṇāṃ ca samānaḥ | 
tairapi brahmaṇi nārāyaṇākhyāyāṃ ca parasyāṃ prakṛtau 
jīvātmānāṃ layo muktirabhyupagatā yataḥ | tasmāt proktanayena 
sarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvābhivyaktireva parapuruṣārthabhūto 
mokṣo'bhyupagantavyaḥ, nānyo'sambhavāditi || 67 ||

	yadi mukteḥ prāgapyasti puṃsaḥ sarvajñatvādi tataḥ kathaṃ kārya 
na karotītyāha-

sarvadā sarvabhāveṣu puṃśaktyorapravṛttitaḥ |
siddhamāvaraṇaṃ cānyat karmākhyādbhavabandhanāt || 68 ||

pramāṇāntareṇa siddhasattākasya 
sūryamaṇīndriyāderarthasyāvaraṇakṛtaḥ kāryānupayogaḥ siddha iti 
proktanayena siddhasattākasya sarvajñatvāderāvṛtatāṃ 
kāryānupayogo'numāpayatītyāvaraṇasiddhiḥ | tacca na karma api tu 
karmākhyād bandhanādanyadityuktaṃ svāyambhuvavṛttāvācāryeṇa-
	yadyaśuddhirna puṃso'sti saktirbhogeṣu kiṃ kṛtā | ityatrāntaraṃ 
tata evāvadhāryam | bhavabandhanādapi anyadavidyātmakaṃ 
malābhidhānamityapyuktaṃ tatraiva- karmataśca śarīrāṇi ityādinā || 68 ||

	kiṃ tadbhavabandhanamityāha-

bandhanāni guṇāntāni sāṃkhyādyairbhāṣitānyatha |
pradhānādīni cānyāni śaivairevoditāni tu || 69 ||

	bhūtatanmātrendriyāhaṅkārabuddhiguṇaparyantāni tāvat 
sāṃkhyādidarśanāntaraprasiddhānyevāsmābhirapi ucyante | 
athānantaraṃ guṇakāraṇaṃ pradhānaṃ tadādīni rāgavidyākalāntāni 
darśanāntarāprasiddhāni śaiverevocyante | yaduktaṃ sṛṣṭikrameṇa-

tato'dhiṣṭhāya vidyeśo māyāṃ sa parameśvaraḥ |
kṣobhayitvā svakiraṇairasṛjattaijasīṃ kalām ||
kalātattvādrāgavidye dve tattve sambabhūvatuḥ |
avyaktaṃ ca tatastasmād guṇāṃścāpyasṛjatprabhuḥ || iti |

	etāni tu tattvabhūtabhuvanātmakāni bhave bandhanāni, na tu 
abhave'tibhave vetyarthaḥ || 69 ||

	atha kimāgamenaivaitāni darśanāntarāprasiddhāni bhavatāṃ 
siddhāni | na anumānenāpi ityāha-

yaḥ proktaḥ kāpilairnyāyo jagadgauṇatvasiddhaye |
āgamānugṛhītatvānnyāyaḥ sādhuḥ sa eva hi || 70 ||

	yenaiva sāṃkhyairācaitanye sati anekatvāt buddhyādīnāṃ 
ghaṭādīnāmiva kāraṇapūrvakatvānumānena guṇāḥ 
kāraṇamavasthāpitāḥ, tenaiva guṇānāmapi ācaitanye

sati, anekatvād avyaktākhyaṃ kāraṇam, 
avyaktarāgavidyākālakalānāmapi pratipuruṣaṃ bhinnānāṃ māyā 
asmābhiḥ sādhyata ityuktaṃ bhogamokṣe-

guṇānāṃ kāryatāsiddhau yo heturabhidhāsyate |
sa kṣityādikalāntānāṃ yojyaḥ kāraṇasiddhaye ||

	ityabhidhāya,

ācaitanye tvanekatvasaṃkhyāsaṃbandhahetutaḥ |
teṣāṃ kāraṇapūrvatvaṃ siddhaṃ buddhighaṭādivat || iti |

	yathā cāyaṃ nyāyo na sāṃkhyopajña evāpi tu 
asmadāgamaparidṛṣṭo'pi tathādarśitamācāryeṇa rauravavṛttau-

	rāgavidyākalāvyaktaguṇabuddhisamudbhavam |

ityetasūtravyākhyāyāṃ tata evāvadhāryam || 70 ||

	nanu ca paraiḥ puruṣāvidyāparamāṇvādyanyakāraṇajanyatve'sya 
jagato nyāyo'nyaḥ prokta iti viruddhavyabhicārīva satpratipakṣatvādayaṃ 
nyāsābhāsa ityāha-

anyakāraṇajanyatve nyāyo ya uditaḥ paraiḥ |
sarvāgamaviruddhatvānnyāyābhāsaḥ sa ucyate || 71 ||

	yathā puruṣādīnāṃ cetanatvena pariṇāmāsambhavād 
avidyāyāścāvastutvāt paramāṇūnāṃ cācaitanye sati anekatvataḥ 
kāraṇāntarapūrvakatvena nityatvāsambhavānna jagatkāraṇatvamiti 
anenānumānena-

ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānāṃ sarūpāḥ |
ajo hyeko juṣamāṇo'nuśete jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo'nyaḥ ||

ityādi vedetihāsapurāṇādibhiścāgamairvādhitatvāt 
ayamevānyakāraṇajanyatvanyāyo nyāyābhāso na pūrvapradarśitaḥ 
tathoktaṃ bhogamokṣe tata evāvadhāryam, tataścāpratipakṣatvād asya 
sāṃkhyanyāyasya na viruddhavyabhicāriṇa iva nyāyābhāsatā api tu

samyaghetutaiveti || 71 ||

	nanu sukhādīnāmātmaguṇatvena prakṛtidharmatvāsambhavād 
yathā guṇakāraṇatāsiddhau jagato'sya sukhādyanvayāt iti sāṃkhyokto 
heturasiddhatvāt nyāyābhāsastathāyamapi bhaviṣyatītyāha-

sukhādyanvayabhāvo hi nāsiddhaḥ kapiloditaḥ |
bhogyatvādrūpavadyasmātpuṃguṇā na sukhādayaḥ || 72 ||

	yaḥ kadācit prakāśate nāsau puruṣaguṇo yathā rūpādiḥ, yaśca 
puruṣaguṇo nāsau kadācit prakāśate yathā jñatvādiḥ | kadācicca 
sukhaṃ kadācicca duḥkhaṃ rāgādayaśca kadācit prakāśanta iti na te'pi 
ātmaguṇāḥ, kintu prakṛtisvabhāvā eva iti nāsiddhatvāt, asyāpi 
hetornyāyābhāsatvamiti kuto'yasyāpi asya bhavet || 72 ||

	atha sāṃkhyapradveṣāt kāraṇāntarakalpanayā vinā 
duḥkhamāsyate bhavadbhistadāstyeva tadityāha-

kāraṇāntarakḷptau vā yāvacchaive prabhāṣitam |
kalādikāryajātaṃ tu saṃsthāpyaṃ tāvadeva hi || 73 ||

	tadapi sāṃkhyābhyupagamenāsmābhiradhikaṃ māyākhyaṃ 
kāraṇaṃ kalādiguṇāntasya kāryajātasyoktamiti nānyat vyavasthāpyaṃ 
pramāṇābhāvāditi || 73 ||

	ataścedamarthasiddhamityāha-

evaṃ kṛte kṛtaṃ viddhi sarvāgamasamarthanam |
anyathā kriyamāṇe tu śuṣkatārkikatā bhavet || 74 ||

	ātmani hi vyavasthāpite sarva eva vedādirapi āgamaḥ samarthito 
bhavati

tasya hi ātmā jñātavyaḥ ityāderadhyātmarūpasya jyotiṣṭomena 
svargakāmo yajeta ityādeśca karmakāṇḍātmanaḥ sarvasyātmārthatvāt | 
tadasamarthane tu nāstikapakṣāśrayataḥ sarvasyāgamārthasyopekṣitatvād 
bauddhalokāyatāderiva śuṣkatārkikataiva pramāṇabādhitābhyupagatā 
bhavediti || 74 ||

	naraparīkṣopasaṃhārāya īśvaraparīkṣopakramāya ca ślokaḥ-

pumānitthaṃ samākhyātaḥ śaṅkaraṃ vacmi sāmpratam |
yataḥ sa bhagavān puṃsāṃ vidhātā bhogamokṣayoḥ || 75 ||

	puruṣasiddhipūrvikā hi īśvarasiddhirityuktamatra hetvantaram, yataḥ 
sa eva puṃsāṃ bhogamokṣayoḥ kartā tataḥ puruṣaparīkṣāpūrvaṃ tasya 
parīkṣeti | ata eva bhogamokṣe'pi īśvaraparīkṣā saṅkṣepato vihitā, atra tu 
vistareṇetyapaunaruktyam || 75 ||

|| iti śrīnārāyaṇakaṇṭhasūnorbhaṭṭarāmakaṇṭhasya kṛtau 
nareśvaraparīkṣāprakāśe naraparīkṣānāma prathamaṃ kāṇḍam || 1 ||



yogatantra � granthamālā

[15]

śrīmadācāryasadyojyotirviracitā

nareśvaraparīkṣā

śrīmadrāmakaṇṭhācāryakṛtayā
prakāśa ṭīkayā

saṃvalitā

kulapateḥ pro. rāmamūrtiśarmaṇaḥ prastāvanayā vibhūṣitā

sampādakaḥ

ācārya śrīrāmajīmālavīyaḥ

ācāryo'dhyakṣaśca
sāṃkhya- yogatantrāgamavibhāgasya
evaṃ
prati- kulapatiḥ
sampūrṇānandasaṃskṛtaviśvavidyālayasya
vārāṇasī

vārāṇasyām

2056 tame vaikramābde	1921 tame śakābde	2000 tame khraistābde



atha dvitīyaṃ kāṇḍam

atha puruṣaparīkṣānantaramīśvaraparīkṣā | nanu 
yuktametattantrāntarīyāṇāṃ vaktum, iha tu jñatvakartṛtvasvabhāve puṃsi 
āvaraṇabhāvābhāvābhyāṃ savitarīvālpamahāviṣayatve 
svasaṃvedanayogyatānumānābhyāṃ pūrvoktarītyā pratipādite 
svalpamahatorīśvarayoḥ siddhiḥ sthitaiva 
jñatvakartṛtvarūpatvādīśvarasyeti kimanyayā tatparīkṣayā | satyametat, 
jagaddhetubhūtānādīśvaraparīkṣā tu iyamanyaivetyavirodhaḥ | tathā hi na 
tāvadasāvasmadādīnāmātmavat svasaṃvedanapratyakṣasiddho 
bhinnātmarūpatvena tasya tato'ntyatvāt, nahi ātmanāṃ 
svasaṃvedanamātrāḥ saṅkīryanta ityuktam | nāpi indriyapratyakṣeṇa 
parasparaṃ svabhāvādevendriyātītatvena teṣāṃ siddheḥ, 
taccharīrāṇāmindriyānatītatvasiddhāvapi 
viśiṣṭajñānakriyātmanorīśvaretarayorasiddheḥ | yadāhurjaiminīyāḥ-

na ca kaiścidasau jñātuṃ kadācidapi śakyate |
svarūpeṇopalabdhe'pi sraṣṭṛtvaṃ nāvagamyate ||
na ca tadvacanenaiṣāṃ pratipattiḥ suniścitā |
asṛṣṭvāpi hyasau brūyādātmaiśvaryaprakāśanāt || iti |

	kintu kāryavyavahārādeḥ kumbhakārādiriva so'pi 
svakāryājjagallakṣaṇāt hetoranumeya iti darśayitum, yaiḥ-

		na kadācidanīdṛśaṃ jagat |

iti vadadbhirjaiminīyalokāyatairjagannityatvābhyupagamenāsya 
hetorasiddhatvamudbhāvyate, tāṃstāvat pratikṣeptumāha-

kṣmādeḥ sāvayavatvena kumbhavatkāryatā matā |

	yatra yatra sāvayavatvaṃ tatra tatra kāryatā yathā ghaṭādau, yatra tu 
kāryatvaṃ nāsti tatra sāvayavatvaṃ nāsti yathā ātmādau | sāvayavatvaṃ 
ca sarvasyaiva pṛthivīparvatādeḥ sanabhaścakrasya dṛṣṭajanmano 
jagato'taḥ kāryatvameva na nityatvamiti vakṣyamāṇasyāsya 
hetornāsiddhatā | atra copādānakāraṇavattaiva tāvajjagataḥ sādhyeti 
darśayati ||

yadyevamindriyaparamāṇvāderjagadbhāgasya 
sāvayavatvāsiddherbhāgāsiddho'yaṃ heturityāha-

ācaitanye tvanekatvādajñātaproktadharmaṇaḥ || 1 ||

	proktaḥ sāvayavatvākhyo dharmo yasyāsau proktadharmo 
ajñātaśvāsau proktadharmā ceti | atra ca raktaḥ paṭo na bhavati iti nyāyena 
viśeṣaṇāṃśasañcāritvāt niṣedhasya proktadharmaviṣayamevājñānaṃ 
na dharmiṇyapītyarthād gamyate tasya ajñātaproktadharmaṇo'siddha-
pūrvoktasāvayavatvādidharmasyendriyaparamāṇvāderapi 
jagadbhāgasyācaitanye sati pratipuruṣaṃ bhedenānekatvād 
vṛkṣaghaṭāderiva kāryatā mateti sambandhaḥ | ācaitanye'pi sati 
citrapaṭāderiva citrāvayavayuktasya kṣmāderjagato'sya 
saugatairekatvenābhyupagamādasya hetorasiddhatvamiti pūrvo heturuktaḥ, 
tataśca hetudvayena sāvayavaniravayavātmakaṃ sarvameva 
jagadupādānayuktaṃ siddhamato na bhāgāsiddhateti na kadācidanīdṛśaṃ 
jagat iti pakṣo'numānanirākṛta ityarthaḥ | na ca 
sāmānyādibhirvyabhicārasteṣāmapi pakṣīkṛtatvāt | nāpyasya 
kadācidanīdṛśaṃ jagat ityabhyupagamasya prayojanamasti evamapi bhagavato 
bhūteśvarasya hetvantareṇa adhiṣṭhātṛtayāsiddheḥ | yaduktam-

idaṃ bhūtamayaṃ viśvamabhraśyaducitasthitiḥ |
sthitervyanakti cidrūpaṃ hetuṃ jīvaccharīravat || iti |

	kiñca na pratijñāmātrādarthasiddhiḥ ityatra heturvaktavyaḥ | nanu 
pṛthivīparvatādi jaganna kadācidanīdṛśamutpattyabhāvayoradarśanāt, 
tathātve vā pramāṇābhāvād ātmavadityatra heturastyeva | na, asiddhatvāt, 
nahi kalpādyantabhāvyutpattivināśādarśanamadhunā siddhamasmadādeḥ, 
pratijñārthaikadeśaśca tathā bhūtairvṛkṣādibhiranaikāntikaśceti | etena 
sthityekasvabhāvatvādadyavad ityevamādayo'pi hetavaḥ prayuktāḥ | yo'pi 
karturasmaraṇādvedavad iti heturucyate so'pyasmākamasiddhaḥ, 
dyāvāpṛthivī janayan deva ekaḥ ityāgamaviruddhaśca | dṛṣṭāntaścāyaṃ 
sādhanadharmāsiddho vedasya racanāpūrvakatve 
nābhinavakaviviracitavākyavad 
akartṛkatvāsiddherityalamasaddhetūpanyāsanirāsairiti | nanu sanniveśavataḥ 
kasyacinnityatvāśaṅkā kena vāryate dhūmādervahnyādibhyo'nyato 
bhāvāśaṅkeva | sambandhagrāhakāt pramāṇādeveti vakṣyāmaḥ | 
proktaṃ ca bhavadbhirapi-

utprekṣate hi yo mohādajātamapi bādhakam |
sa sarvavyavahāreṣu saṃśayātmā kṣayaṃ vrajet || iti || 1 |

	nanu vādyasiddho'yaṃ heturyato mūlapṛthaktvavādibhi 
sāṃkhyabhedaiḥ pṛthivyādīni tattvāni nityānīṣyante | tadantargatāśca 
bhavanta ityāha-

tattvādibhuvanāntaṃ tu kalādikṣmāntamīritam |
śaivaiḥ kāryaṃ hi tajjātaṃ kumbhavanna svabhāvataḥ || 2 ||

upādānādi tasyeṣṭaṃ vimatiḥ kartṛkārake |
ataḥ sa sādhyate'smābhistatkartā śaṅkaraḥ sphuṭam || 3 ||

	na kevalaṃ paraprasiddhaṃ pṛthivyādyeva yāvatsarvameva 
tattvabhūtabhāvabhuvanātmakaṃ pṛthivyādikalāprāntaṃ 
jagadasmābhirvṛkṣaghaṭādideva kāryamiṣyate, hīti yasmāt 
tasyopādānakāraṇaṃ māyā, ādigrahaṇātsahakārikāraṇaṃ karma 
proktam | ato na mūlapṛthaktvavādibhiriva tat svabhāvato 
nityatvenāsmābhiriṣyate, api tu mūlaikatvavāditayā 
ghaṭādivatkāryameveti na vādyasiddhatā | 
tataścobhayavādisiddhādasmāddhetorvakṣyamāṇanayeneśvara eva 
sādhyaḥ || 3 ||

kathamityāha-

adṛṣṭakartṛ kāyādi kāryaṃ prajñāvatā kṛtam |
kumbhavat

	yadyatkāryaṃ tattat viśiṣṭajñānakriyāyuktakartṛpūrvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ 
yathā ghaṭādi, kāryaṃ ca tanukaraṇabhuvanādyadṛṣṭakartṛkaṃ 
sarvameva jagat pratipāditam, tasmādapi 
viśiṣṭajñānakriyātmakakartṛpūrvakaṃ siddhamiti yastasya kartā sa 
parameśvaraḥ siddhaḥ | atrāhuḥ kāryatvasya kartṛpūrvakatvarūpatvāt 
pratijñārthaikadeśo'yaṃ heturiti | tadayuktam | sāṃkhyasaugataiḥ 
kāryatvābhyupagame'pi jagataḥ kartṛpūrvakatvāniṣṭhairata eva 
upādānakāraṇavattaivātra kāryatā vivakṣiteti darśitam | nāpyananvayaḥ 
dṛṣṭakartṛkeṣu kāryeṣvanvayasiddheḥ | adṛṣṭakartṛkamevātra pakṣīkṛtam 
| yastvāha- kimanena anumitānumānena, sāvayavatvādityayameva hetuḥ 
kartṛpūrvakatvasiddhau kasmāt nopādīyata iti, satyam upādīyeta yadi 
tāvanmātrasyaiva jagatastatsādhayitumabhipretaṃ syāt, 
niravayavasyāpyabhipretamiti naivamupāttam |

na cātra gṛhāntaḥpraviṣṭasūryaraśmiṣūpalabhyamānaistra-
sareṇvādibhiranaikāntiko heturadṛṣṭakartṛpadena dharmiṇo 
viśeṣitatvādityāha-

		nāvipakṣatvādanekāntastrasādibhiḥ || 4 ||

	vipakṣe hi heturvartamāno'naikāntikaḥ prameyatvādiḥ | na cātra 
trasādayaḥ ādigrahaṇāt sikatopalādayo vipakṣāsteṣāmapi 
adṛṣṭakartṛpadena pakṣīkṛtatvāt, api tu ātmādaya eva | sandigdho hi 
dharmaḥ sādhyo na siddho bādhito vā | yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

sandigdhahetuvacanādvyāpto heturanāśrayaḥ | iti |

	adṛṣṭakartṛkeṣu ca sarveṣveva kāryeṣu kartṛpūrvakatvaṃ 
tatsādhakabādhakapramāṇābhāvāt sandigdhaṃ parasyeti 
hetvabhidhānaviṣayatvāt trasādiṣvapi vāstavapakṣatvayogāt sādhyameveti 
te'pi pakṣīkṛtā, na tu vyabhicāribhayeneti | na ca pakṣa eva 
vyabhicāraviṣayaḥ dhūmādagnyanumānādāvapi tathā prāpteḥ 
sarvānumānābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | niścitasādhyābhāvaśca vāstavo 
vipakṣaḥ | na ca trasādiṣu kenacidapi pramāṇenākartṛpūrvakatvaṃ 
niścitamiti | kuto vipakṣatā, 
karturadarśanamātrasyotsannamaṭhakūpādibhirvyabhicārāt | kūpādāviva 
trasādiṣu na kadācidasmābhiḥ kartā dṛṣṭa iti cet, ata 
evāsmadādyagocaro'sāvāveśādau piśācādivat tatra sambhāvyamānaḥ 
pramāṇena vinā nāstīti na śakyate niścetumiti sandigdha eva | 
tatastrasādiṣu vipakṣatā kṣapayituṃ na śakyā | tarhi tṛṇalatādīnāṃ 
dṛśyamānebhyaḥ eva pṛthivyādibhyo hetubhyo 
janmadṛṣṭeradṛṣṭakartrantarakalpane'navasthāprasaṅgādatra 
vipakṣatvamiti tairanekāntaḥ ! yadāhuḥ-

yeṣu satsu bhavatyeva yattebhyo'nyasya kalpane |
taddhetutvena sarvatra hetūnāmanavasthitiḥ ||
śastrauṣadhādisambandhāccaitrasya vraṇarohaṇe |
asambaddhasya kiṃ sthāṇoḥ kāraṇatvaṃ tu kalpyate || iti |

kathaṃ tarhi te'pi kadācinna bhavanti | sahakāriṇo varṣātapāderabhāvāt | 
tasyāpi kathamabhāvaḥ | nanu adṛṣṭavaśāt tarhi tṛṇādīnāṃ dṛṣṭeṣveva 
kāraṇeṣu satsu bhāvo na siddha iti adṛṣṭasyāpi apratikṣepānna vipakṣatā | 
nanvadṛṣṭaṃ karmaivāsmābhiriṣyata iti cet, na, viśeṣe pramāṇābhāvād 
adṛṣṭasya piśācādeḥ karturabhyupagamād hetukartuśca kvacid 
devatātmanaḥ | yaduktam-

ye'pi mantravidaḥ kecinmantrānkāṃścana kurvate |
prabhuprabhāvasteṣāṃ sa taduktanyāyasiddhitaḥ || iti |

tasyātrāpi sambhavānnaiva piśācādirastīti jaiminīyāḥ |

yadāhuḥ-

pramāṇapañcakaṃ yatra vasturūpe na vidyate |
vastusattāvabodhārthaṃ tatrābhāvapramāṇatā || iti |

	tadayuktaṃ pramāṇanivṛttāvapi, ata eva 
bhāvasiddherbhūnihitadravyavat | na cātrāpi pramāṇaṃ nāstyeva 
puruṣamātrāsambhavino'tīndriyārthasyāvisaṃvādinastadānīṃ tatra 
jñānasya siddheḥ | tadapi karmavaśāt tadānīṃ puṃsa āvirbhavatīti ced 
yuktam, yadi tasya tadāvirbhūtaṃ bhavet, sa tvapasmṛtestadānīṃ 
mūḍhavedana eva siddhaḥ | nahi idaṃ mama jñānamidaṃ netyatra 
svānubhavavyatirekeṇānyat pramāṇamasti, nāpi 
gacchatastṛṇādijñānasyeva tasya bhavato'pyanupalakṣaṇaṃ yuktaṃ 
niścayātmakatvāt | yadāhuḥ-

na ca niścayavijñānaṃ svātmanaiva na vidyate || iti |

	tataḥ puruṣavedanānupraveśād devadattādijñānavat 
tato'tyantabhinnaṃ tat saṃvinmohakartustadānīṃ kasyacit sātiśayasya 
piśācādestat jñānamiti kāryato'bhyupagantavyam | na karmaṇāṃ 
jaḍatvāditi na piśācāderasmadādyagocarasyāpi tatra karturabhāvaḥ | nanu 
tathāpi karmaṇastatra hetutvamastyeva | kimataḥ karturadṛṣṭasyāpi 
sambhavāt tṛṇādāvadṛṣṭakartṛpūrvakatvasaṃśaya iti na vipakṣatā, api 
tu proktanayena vāstavaṃ pakṣatvameveti | na ca karmaṇo'pi kṛṣyāderiva 
cetanānadhiṣṭhitasya kāryajanakatvamiti vakṣyāmaḥ | tattṛṇādīnāṃ 
dṛṣṭahetutve'pi parairadṛṣṭakāryatvenābhyupagamāt,

pratyutātra sapakṣatvamiti matvā trasādibhireva 
vipakṣatvāśaṅkayānaikāntikatvaṃ parihṛtamācāryeṇa, na tu tairapīti || 4 
||

	nanvasiddha evāyaṃ heturiti parābhiprāyamāha-

dhīmatkṛtaṃ tu yatkāryaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tasyaiva darśanāt |
dhīmato'numitiryuktā dehāderna viparyayāt || 5 ||

	yadeva hi yasya kāryatvena siddhaṃ tadeva tasyānumāpakaṃ na 
kāryamātraṃ dhūmādīvāgnyādeḥ, na śakaṭādiviśiṣṭameva 
ghaṭapaṭaprāsādanagarādikāryaṃ kartṛjanyaṃ siddhamiti tata eva 
viśiṣṭajñānātmakartranumānaṃ yuktam, na tu kāryaśabdavācyatve 
tadvijātīyāttanubhuvanakaraṇādeḥ kāryāntarāt tatra tatkāryatvāt iti 
hetorapyasiddheḥ | yadāhuḥ saugatāḥ-

siddhaṃ yādṛgadhiṣṭhātṛbhāvābhāvānuvṛttimat |
sanniveśādi tadyuktaṃ tasmādyadanumīyate ||
vastubhede prasiddhasya śabdasāmyādabhedinaḥ |
na yuktānumitiḥ pāṇḍudravyādiva hutāśane ||
anyathā kumbhakāreṇa mṛdvikārasya kasyacit |
ghaṭādeḥ kāraṇātsiddhyedvalmīkasyāpi tatkṛtiḥ ||
jātyantaraprasiddhasya śabdasāmānyadarśanāt |
na yuktaṃ sādhanaṃ gotvādvāgādīnāṃ viṣāṇivat || iti || 5 ||

etat pratikṣipati-

kāryatvādeva kumbhāderdehādirna vilakṣaṇam |
kāryaṃ ca na kvacid dṛṣṭaṃ prajñāvatkāraṇaṃ vinā || 6 ||

	na tāvadadṛṣṭakartṛkeṣu tanukaraṇabhuvanādiṣu 
kāryatvamasiddhaṃ bhavatām, yena dṛṣṭakartṛkebhyo 
vijātīyatvābhyupagamo yuktaḥ syāt, jagataḥ sarvasyānityatvena 
samanantarapratyayopādānābhyupagamāt | nanu āśrayāṇāṃ bhedāt 
tadapi bhinnameva | na, tena rūpeṇa teṣāmapi abhinnatvāt 
śāvaleyabāhuleyādīnāmiva gotvena, śabdaghaṭādīnāmiva vā 
kṛtakatvena | nahi anyathā kṛtakatvenānityatvaṃ ghaṭādivat 
śabdādāvanekavṛttyādibhirvānekatvaṃ bhūtakaṇṭhamālāguṇavat 
sāmānyādau śakyamupapādayituṃ bhavadbhiḥ | tena rūpeṇa teṣāṃ 
samānataiva, yaduktam-

sādhyadharmasāmānyena samāno'rthaḥ sapakṣaḥ || iti |

	na kāryatveneti cet, jitaṃ jigīṣubhirabhinavaiḥ 
prasiddhanyāyanihnavena, asiddhatādyāndhyamutpādayadbhiḥ | kathaṃ vā 
sanniveśavatāṃ sarveṣāṃ 
prameyatvasattvakāryatvaśūnyatvānātmatvādibhireva na 
sanniveśavattvenāpi samānatā | nanu sanniveśo nāma nānyat kiñcit 
sanniviṣṭebhyaḥ | bālabhāṣitametat, sato'pi kimanyatsattvaṃ kāryādvā 
kāryatvam | satyam, bhinnānāmapi teṣāmekavimarśajanakatvena 
samānateṣyate | yaduktam-

ekapratyavamarśākhyajñānādekārthasādhane |
bhede'pi niyatāḥ kecitsvabhāvenendriyādivat ||

jvarādiśamane kāścitsaha pratyakameva vā |
dṛṣṭā yathā vauṣadhayo nānātve'pi na cāparā || iti |

	tarhi sanniveśavattvenāpi sarvasanniveśavatāṃ samānatvaṃ pratīya-
mānaṃ tadvadeva bhaviṣyatīti keṣāñcit jātyantarābhidhānamayuktameva 
| akartṛpūrvatvasiddhau teṣāṃ jātyantaratvamekaśabdavācyatve'pi syāt, 
upādānakāraṇabhedasiddhāviva pāṇḍutvāderupalabhyamānākā-
rānyatvaniścaye vā gośabdavācyānāmiva vāgādīnāṃ navānāṃ na tu 
tatsaṃśaye | sambandhagrahaṇakāla eva mahānasadṛṣṭāt dhūmād 
atidūravartitvenādṛṣṭāgnisambandhasya dhūmāntarasyaivāgnyupādāna-
tāyāṃ sandehamātrādasiddheḥ, adarśanamātrasya ca sandehaphalatvā-
dityuktam | tarhyasiddhatvādeva teṣāṃ dṛṣṭakartṛbhyo jātyantaratā syāt | 
na, ata eva tasya sādhyatvād dhūmāderivāgnyādyupādānatā dūravartini 
parvatādau | sādhanasya hyasiddhatā dūṣaṇaṃ sādhyasya tu bhūṣaṇa-
mevetyuktam | atha vyāptirasiddhā kāryamātrasya kartṛmātreṇeti, ucyate, 
ghaṭādīnāṃ viśiṣṭakartṛtvenaiva darśanāt kva vā dhūmamātrasya 
vahnimātreṇa vyāptiḥ siddhā bhavatām | dhūmaviśeṣo'pi hi kaścideva 
mahānasādāvagniviśeṣajanyaḥ siddhaḥ | nanu tārṇapārṇāderviśeṣa-
vyabhicāre'pi tato dhūmamātrasya darśanāt | na tadāpi nirviśeṣasya 
sāmānyasya pratyakṣeṇāsiddheḥ sa eva tadavastho doṣaḥ | satyam, ata 
evārthasāmarthyādanumānato vā sarvatrāgnijanyo dhūmo dhūmatvāt, 
mahānasadṛśyabhūmavadityapratiṣṭhitabhedasyāgni-mātrasya 
vyāpakasyātrāpratiṣṭhitabhedaṃ dhūmamātraṃ vyāpyaṃ liṅgatayā 
pratīyate | yaduktam-

sāmānyaviṣayaṃ proktaṃ liṅgaṃ bhedāpratiṣṭhite || iti |

yadyevamihāpi ghaṭapaṭaśakaṭādikāryeṣu kartṛviśeṣasya 
kumbhakārādervyabhicārāt, apratiṣṭhitabhedameva kāryamātraṃ 
kartṛmātrasya vyāpyaṃ hetutvena siddhamiti na vyāpterapi asiddhiḥ 
pratibandhamūlaiva hi vyāptiriti vyāptividaḥ | satyam, yadi pratibandho 
bhavet, dṛṣṭāntādhīnaiva tu atra vyāptiḥ, sā ca tatputrādeḥ 
śyāmatvānumānavat vyabhicāriṇyeveti ananumānam | idamapi 
cikitsakopadeśavat, śraddheyameva | pratibandho hi dhūmāgnyāderivātrāpi 
pratyakṣasiddha eva | dhūmāgnyorhi kāryakāraṇabhāvo na 
dṛṣṭāntasiddhapratyakṣopalambhamantareṇa kṛtakarvānityatvayorvā 
tātātmyamanekavṛttyanekatvayorvā anyato'vagamyate | bhavadbhirapi 
sambandhasādhakapramāṇaviṣayatayā ca dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhanavākyeṣu 
prayujyate, na dṛṣṭāntamātratayeti, tadabhāvād dṛṣṭāntamātrayoge'pi 
śyāmastatputratvāt ityevamāderanumānāvaskarasya vyabhicāritā 
tadatrāpi upādānakāraṇasyeva karturapi kāryeṇa saha kāryakāraṇabhāvo 
dṛṣṭānte pratyakṣopalambhapramāṇasiddha iti tadbalādeva sarvasya 
saryasyopādāneneva kartrāpi vyāptiḥ siddhetyavyabhicāraḥ | yadyevaṃ 
dṛṣṭakartṛkeṣyeva pratibandhasiddheḥ sā sidhyati 
nādṛṣṭakartṛkeṣvanyatvāt | pralāpamātrametat, anyatvamātrasya 
dṛṣṭakartṛkeṣu dṛṣṭāgnisambandheṣu ca dhūmādiṣu 
darśanenānaikāntikatvāt | athādṛṣṭakartṛkataiva 
dṛṣṭakartṛkebhyo'nyatvamucyate tadapi 
tathābhūtairghaṭādikairanaikāntikameva |

	atha tatra ghaṭādirūpatayā ananyatvamihāpi kāryarūpatayeti na 
viśeṣaḥ | nanu yajjātīyo yataḥ siddhaḥ, tata eva tasyānumānaṃ yuktam, na 
vijātīyāt, valmīkastūpāderiva kumbhakārasya | bhinnajātīyaṃ ca 
dṛṣṭakartṛkāt, adṛṣṭakartṛkaṃ kāryamiti na tataḥ kartranumānaṃ yuktam 
| na, tena rūpeṇa teṣāṃ bhedāsiddherityuktaṃ kṛtakatveneva 
śabdaghaṭādīnāṃ sattveneva vā sarvapadārthānām | na hi kṛtakatvasyā-
nekavijātīyavastubhinnasyānekavijātīyavastubhinnenānityatvena 
tādātmyam, sattvena vā kṣaṇikatvasya bhavatāmupapadyate | nanu tena 
rūpeṇa teṣāṃ samānataiva, na vijātīyatā | nanu viruddhametad 
vijātīyameva na dharmabhedāt śāvaleyabāhuleyāviva gotve ca | tarhi 
etadeva sādhu, yasya yathābhūtena saha sambandhaḥ siddhaḥ, sa tathā 
bhūtameva pratibandhabalāt gamayati | kimatra sajātīyatvāsajātīyatveneti | 
yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

yadīdaṃ svayamarthānāṃ rocate tatra ke vayam | iti |

ataśca valmīkastūpādeḥ kumbhakāraḥ- vyabhicāre'pi 
cetanāyuktakartravyabhicāro'styeveti, tatrāpi sa eva sādhyaḥ | kiñca kātra 
jātirabhipretā, yadapekṣayā sajātīyatvāsajātīyatvavyavahāraḥ | nanu 
bhavatāmiva sadṛśataiva | yadyevam, sattveneva sarvabhāvānāṃ 
kāryatvena sarvakāryāṇāṃ sadṛśatvamiti tadevāvinābhāvāt 
kartṛsiddhau heturucyata iti | yajjātīyo yataḥ siddhaḥ tajjātīyādevātra kartā 
anumeya ukto na vijātīyādvā tacchabdamātrāvācyāditi vāsiddhatā | 
kathaṃ tarhi pāṇḍutvāderapi jāgnijanyatā | tadabhāve'pi makkolādiṣu 
tasya darśanāt sattvasyevākāryabhūteṣvātmādiṣu | na cākartṛkaṃ 
kiñcit kāryaṃ siddham, yena tatra vartamānasyāsya hetorvyabhicāraḥ 
sambhāvyeta | parvatatṛṇāderapi adṛṣṭakartṛkatvena siddheruktatvāditi na 
pāṇḍutvādervahnijanyatvamiva kāryasya kartṛjanyatvamasiddhamiti || 6 ||

	atha yathābhūtenaiva yasya pratibandhaḥ siddhastathābhūta eva, 
tasmādanumīyate ityayameva grahastatrāpyāha-

na ca yadvyaktisambaddhā vyaktiryaiveha sammatā |
tasya evānumānaṃ syātpunastadupalabdhitaḥ || 7 ||

	yadyevam, sakalasajātīyavijātīyavyāvṛttena 
svalakṣaṇenaivāgnyādinā mahānasādau dhūmādeḥ svalakṣaṇasyaiva 
pratibandhaḥ siddhaḥ, tasya cānanuyāyitvāt, na dharmyantare'numānaṃ 
sambhavati, nāpi tatraiva pratyakṣasiddheranumānāyogāditi nivṛttā, 
idānīmanumānavārtā | jitaṃ 
pratyakṣaikapramāṇavādibhirlaukāyatikaireva | 
tasmātsarveṇānumānavādinā samānyaviṣaya evānumānaṃ pramāṇaṃ 
kāryamihetyato'pyasya nāsiddhatā || 7 ||

	anye tu śarīrayuktakartṛpūrvakatvādisādhanāt, 
dharmaviśeṣaviparītasādhano'yaṃ viruddhaḥ 
śarīrayuktakartṛpūrvakatvenaiva ghaṭādau dṛṣṭānte'sya 
hetorvyāptisiddherityāhuḥ |

yathāsiddhe ca dṛṣṭānte bhaveddhetorviruddhatā |
anīśvaravināśyādikartṛkatvaṃ prasajyate ||

śarīrādervinā cāsya kathamicchāpi sarjane |
śarīrādyatha tasya syāttasyotpattirna tatkṛtā || iti |

etadeva pratikṣipati-

siṣādhayiṣatāṃ nyāyaḥ sādhyadharmaviparyayam |
pareṣāṃ hi śarīrāderdṛṣṭenaiva nivartyate || 8 ||

	sādhyadharmasyāśarīrakartṛpūrvakatvasya viparyayaṃ 
kāryatvādevāsmād hetoḥ sādhayitumicchatāṃ pareṣāṃ 
tanukaraṇāderdharmiṇaḥ sa viruddhasādhananyāyaḥ pratyakṣeṇaiva 
nivāryate | svakāryakaraṇaspandādeḥ kāryasya śarīrāntaraṃ vinaiva 
icchāyuktakartṛpūrvakatvena 
āntarasparśanendriyasvasaṃvedanapratyakṣābhyāṃ siddheḥ kāryatvaṃ 
sarvatrāvinābhāvāt tāvanmātrameva gamayati, na tu 
śarīrayuktakartṛpūrvakatvamapi vyabhicārāt | tanna, 
dharmaviśeṣaviparītasādhano'yaṃ heturityarthaḥ | anyathā 
kṛtakatvādayo'pi śabdādau ghaṭādivat 
pṛthubudhnodarādyavinābhāvenānityatvaṃ gamayeyuḥ | dhūmādayaśca 
parvatādāvapi mahānasādāvivāparvatavartinaṃ vahnimiti sarvasya 
dharmaviśeṣaviparyayamasādhanād hetvabhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | atha tatra ya 
evāvyabhicārī dharmaḥ sa eva sādhyo nānyo vyabhicārādityucyate | 
tadihāpi samānamityaviruddhatā | nanu kṣaṇikatvādarthānāmatrāpi 
pūrvadehalakṣaṇasahakāriṇo dehino deśāntare dehikṣaṇo'nya 
evotpadyate, tanna, kāryaṃ dehayuktakartṛpūrvakatvaṃ vyabhicaratīti 
viśeṣaviruddha evāyamiti kaścit | yadāha dharmakīrtirapi-

tasmātpṛthagaśakteṣu yeṣu sambhāvyate guṇaḥ |
saṃhatau hetutā teṣāṃ neśvarāderabhedataḥ || iti |

	satyam, yadi kṣaṇikatvamasya syāt, tattu pratikṣiptamātmasiddhau, 
tatpratikṣepamūlatvāttasyāḥ | api cāśakunābhidhānasvabhāvasyaiva 
bhavataḥ sarvatra tadabhidhānaṃ daurbhāgyameva prakāśayati, kṣaṇikatve 
kriyāyāḥ sambhavābhāvena kartṛtvasyāpyasiddheḥ | ko'sau dharmo yasya 
viśeṣaviparyayasādhanatā | nirākṛte hi kṣaṇikatve siddhe'bhyupagate 
vātmani kartṛtve vā īśvaraparīkṣāprastāvo yuktaḥ, nānyathā | 
tanmadhye tu kṣaṇikāśraye vikalpo'bhyupagamanirākṛta eva | nanu 
sthairye'pi tadviṣayatvāt spandakriyāyāḥ śarīraṃ tasyāpekṣaṇīyameva | 
bālapralapitametat, ghaṭādivaddhi kāryatayā tadapekṣaṇīyam, na tu 
śarīratayā | nanvacaladavasthaṃ caladavasthānirvṛtyarthaṃ 
tadapekṣaṇīyameva | satyam, ghaṭādivaddhi kāryatayetyuktam | 
nanvaśarīrasya jñānakriyayoranupapattiḥ | yadāhuḥ kumārilāḥ-

aśarīrohyadhiṣṭhātā nātmā muktātmavadbhavet || iti |

tadayuktaṃ na hi dṛṣṭe'nupapannaṃ nāma, dṛṣṭāntasya 
sādhyadharmasiddho muktātmanāṃ sarvajñatvayoge'pi, 
īśvarasamatvābhyupagamāt | tarhi vijñānakaivalyādivadbhaviṣyati | ne 
teṣāmaśarīratvaṃ tathātvahetuḥ, api tu mala evetyuktamanyatra |

yo jñaścāyamadehatvādindriyādyaprasiddhitaḥ |

ityetadeva sabdāntareṇāha | tenāpi mīmāṃsakopapattyupajīvitvaprasiddhiḥ 
kevalaṃ bauddhānāṃ prakāśitā bhavati nādhikaṃ kiñcit | yadāhuḥ-

pūrvapakṣeṣu tairuktā yā jaghanyopapattayaḥ |
tābhirdurlabhavabdhābhirmattā valganti bhikṣavaḥ || iti |

	kathaṃ vā kāryakaraṇādyabhāve jijasya sarvaviṣayaṃ 
jñānamiṣyate bhavadbhiḥ | nanu tasyāpi śarīramastyeva | tatkiṃ 
karmajamakarmajaṃ vā, karmajaśarīrayoge'smadādīnāmivāsarvajñatā, 
akarmajaṃ tu śarīramavahnyādibhyo dhūmādīva niyatakāraṇajanyatvāt, 
na sambhavatyeva | nanu karuṇatayā tasya śarīraṃ bhaviṣyati | satyam, yadi 
tasyāstatra hetutvaṃ pratyakṣato'numānādvā siddhaṃ bhavet | 
nanvāgamād bhaviṣyati yaduktam-

tṛtīyasthānasakrāntau nyāyyaḥ śāstraparigrahaḥ | iti |

	satyam, yadi pratyakṣānumānaviṣayasthānadvaye'sya viśuddhiḥ 
syāt, sā tu nāstītyuktamātmasiddhau | nanu 
dvayānubandhāttasyāstṛṣṇāderiva śarīrahetutvam | tarhyavidyānvayāt 
pudgalasyeva kāruṇikasyāpi asarvajñatā | nanu nirmāṇaśaktyaiva tasya 
śarīramiti bauddhāḥ | na tarhi īśvarāsiddhiḥ | nanu tathāpi tasya 
śarīramastyeva | yadyarthapratipattyupāyatayā, tarhi asmadādivat 
so'pīndriyādiparatantrabodhatvādasarvajña eva, nahīndriyāṇāṃ 
sarvārthatā sambhavati pradīpādivat parimitārthaprakāśakatvasiddheḥ | 
nanu bhāvanābalato vinivṛttasamastacittadoṣasyendriyānapekṣayaiva 
sarvārthadarśino'pyadhiṣṭhānamātreṇa śarīramupayujyata eva | 
yadyevam, kuḍyāderaviśiṣṭaṃ tat na śarīratāmāsādayati | yadāhuḥ-

sānnidhyamātratastasya puṃsaścintāmaṇeriva |
niḥsaranti yathākāmaṃ kuḍyādibhyo'pi deśanāḥ || iti |

	śarīrānapekṣayaiva tasya sarvaviṣayaṃ jñānam, icchāmātreṇa ca 
nirmāṇaṃ kāyādau kartṛtvam, anyasyāpi ca mañjuśrīprabhṛteḥ 
sātiśayasyābhyupagatameva bhavadbhiriti

tadvadbhagavato'pi viśvādhiṣṭhānād viśvakāyatvasiddhe na 
dharmaviśeṣaviparītasādhanatā | nāpi kumbhakārātmano'nīśvaratvaṃ 
jñatvakartṛtvātmakatvādīśvaratvasya kumbhakārādāvapi 
svakāryaviṣayasya bhāvāt | na hi tasya tatra jñānābhāve kartṛtvavaikalye 
vā kumbhakartṛtvaṃ sambhavati, akumbhakārasyeva, nāpi 
vināśitvamātmanaḥ prāṅ nityatvasya sādhitatvāt tat na dṛṣṭānte'pi 
anīśvaravināśyādyapi siddhamato na itthamapi viśeṣaviruddhatā | etena-

icchānantaravṛttistu dṛṣṭānte'pi na vidyate |
acetanaḥ kathaṃ bhāvastadicchāmanuvartate ||

ityetadapi prayuktaṃ svadehaspandādau kumbhakārādestathaiva dṛṣṭatvāt | 
atha śarīrādanyo ghaṭādiḥ na, icchānantaravṛttiḥ dṛṣṭa ityucyate, 
tadapyayuktamicchayā vinā tasyāpi asiddheḥ | nahyanicchuranyecchurvā 
kumbhakāro ghaṭaṃ vartayan dṛṣṭaḥ | nanu icchāmātrasya tatrāhetutvaṃ 
śarīrāderapi, apekṣaṇāt | kimataḥ, nahi sahakāryapekṣākāraṇasya 
kāryaṃ vihanti karmaṇo dharmādeḥ sukhādāviva śarīrādyapekṣeti saiva 
sarvatrāvyabhicāriṇīti kāryādanumīyate | na ca śarīrādanyeṣu icchayā 
karaṇamasiddhameva | 
siddhamantrāṇāmicchayaivāhiviṣāgnyādiṣvanyathākaraṇadarśanāditi 
vakṣyatyācāryaḥ svayamevottarakāṇḍe | proktaṃ ca saugataiḥ-

mantrādyupaplutākṣāṇāṃ yathā mṛcchakalādayaḥ |
anyathaivāvabhāsante tadrūparahitā api ||

	yadvā īśvaraḥ saśarīra eva svanirmāṇakāyāntarādhiṣṭhānena 
pūrvaṃ svaśarīramupasaṃhṛtya jagatkṛtyaṃ sampādayati | anādirhi tasya 
parapurapraveśina iva śarīraprabandha ityapi tatraivācāryo vakṣyati |

ataśca yaduktam-

anekāntaśca hetuste taccharīrādinā bhavet |
utpattimā/śca taddeho dehatvādasmadādivat ||

ityetadapi apahastitameva | taccharīrasya sanniveśavattve'pi 
nityatvānabhyugamāditi māyāgarbheśvarāṇāṃ ca śarīrayoge'pi 
aiśvaryamiṣyata eva | yaduktamācaryeṇa mokṣakārikāsu-

kalāyoge'pi cāvaśyāḥ kalānāṃ paśusaṅghavat |
vaśyāstāḥ paśubhiḥ sārdhaṃ teṣāṃ te taduparyataḥ ||

ityavirodhaḥ || 8 ||

	nanu dṛṣṭānto'tra sādhyadharmāsiddhaḥ kāryasyeśvarakartṛtvena 
dṛṣṭānte ghaṭādāvasiddheḥ yathāsiddhena viruddhaścetyuktaṃ 
nirmalatvasarvajñatvecchāmātrakartṛtvādyātmakaṃ hi 
aiśvaryamanīśvarakumbhakārādāvadṛṣṭameva | yadāha-

kumbhakārādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ ghaṭādau yadi ceṣyate |
neśvarādhiṣṭhitatvaṃ syādasti cetsādhyahīnatā || iti |

	etapyayuktamityāha-

malādipāśamuktatvaṃ sarvajñatvādidharmatā |
svecchānantarakāritvaṃ kulāle yanna dṛśyate || 9 ||

sarvametanmaheśasya setsyati hyarthaśaktitaḥ |
nānumānādito nātaḥ sādhyahīnaṃ nidarśanam || 10 ||

	kumbhakārādīnāmapi svakārye nirmalajñānatā sarvajñatvaṃ 
svecchākāritvādyapi ceśvaratvaṃ siddhameva, anyathā 
ghaṭādikaraṇāsambhavādityuktam | atha yathābhūtaṃ tat 
tanukaraṇabhuvanādisambandhisādhyaṃ tathābhūtam, na dṛṣṭānte 
dṛṣṭamiti ucyate | vahnyādirapi parvatasambandhī 
mahānasādāvadṛṣṭatvāt na dhūmādinā sādhya iti 
sarvānumānābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | nanu dhūmamātraṃ vahnimātre 
vyāptaṃ siddhameva | kva yathā, na tāvat, dṛṣṭānte viśiṣṭasyaiva 
mahānasādau dhūmasya viśiṣṭādevāgnito bhāvadarśanāt | nanu 
taduttarakālabhāvinā pratyabhijñāpratyakṣeṇa yadvārā tadbhāvinaḥ 
sakṛdapi tato bhāvādityanumānena vastuśaktisāmarthyena vā 
sāmānyaviṣayā vyāptiravatiṣṭhata eva | vyavatiṣṭhatām, parvatavartī tu 
vahniviśeṣaḥ kathaṃ sādhyaḥ, na hyasmād 
vyāptisādhanādanumānāderapi tasya siddhiḥ viśeṣe vyāptyabhāvāt | 
nanu sāmānyākṣipto viśeṣa evāpratiṣṭhitabhedo vyāptiviṣaya iti cet, kiṃ 
parvatavartyeva, atha so'pi | yadi parvatavartyeva siddhasādhanatā 
vyāptigrahaṇakāla eva tasya siddheḥ svānubhavabādhaśca | atha 
so'pītyucyate, tarhi niyamo'nanumeya eveti siddhasādhanataiva | yadāhuḥ-

		sāmānye siddhasādhyatā

iti deśāntaravartinyagnau pravṛttiprasaṅgaśceti | nanu 
pakṣadharmatābalānniyatasya dhūmasya grahaṇāt niyato vahniḥ pratīyate- 
pramāṇamanumānaṃ hi trirūpālliṅgato'rthadṛk, ityavirodhaḥ | yadāha 
dharmakīrttiḥ-

	sāmānyaṃ ca sādhyaṃ na ca siddhasādhyatā 
tadayogavyavacchedasyāsiddheḥ iti | yadyevamihāpyapratiṣṭhitabhedaḥ 
kāryaviśeṣa eva kartṛviśeṣa vyāptaḥ siddha iti | tanukaraṇabhuvanādeḥ 
kāryātmano'rthasya śaktyā tatkarturapi viśiṣṭasya 
svakāryaviṣayamamalatvasarvajñatvecchāmātrakartṛtvādyapīśvaratvaṃ 
sidhyatīti na dṛṣṭāntaḥ | sādhyavikala ityadoṣaḥ | tarhi kumbhakārādivat 
svakāryādanyatra tasya ajñānamanīśvaratā vā syāt | kvānyatra 
tatkāryatanukaraṇabhuvanādivyatirekeṇānyasyābhāvāt | nanvayat nāstīti 
kutaḥ | astu, tatpuruṣārthasādhanaṃ cet, tat kāryameva tadasādhane tu 
tasmin śaśaviṣāṇādāviva tasyājñānaṃ bhūṣaṇameva | yadāhuḥ-

arthakriyāsamarthasya vicāraiḥ kiṃ tadarthinām |
ṣaṇḍasya rūpavairūpye kāminyāḥ kiṃ parīkṣayā ||

ityavirodhaḥ | nanu kumbhakārādikāryameva tasya kāryādanyat | satyam, 
kumbhakārādiracanānyathānupapattestu tatrāsyājñānādi na 
sambhavatyeva | na hi kumbhakartṛtvaṃ kumbhakārasyāhetukamapi tu 
īśvarahetukamityuktam | na ca kumbhājñāne tatkartṛracanā yataḥ || 10 ||

	nanu yaḥ kartā nāsāvīśvaraḥ sarvajño vā yathā kumbhakāraḥ, 
kartā ca tanukaraṇāderbhavadbhirabhyupagato'taḥ 
so'pyanīśvaro'sarvajño vetyādipratihetuprakṣipto'yaṃ pakṣa ityāha-

kulālasamatāṃ patyuḥ parasya proktahetutaḥ |
anumitsorviruddho hi svarāddhāntena jāyate || 11 ||

	yadyevaṃ yatra dhūmastatrāparvatasambandhī vahniḥ yathā 
mahānasādau dhūmaśca parvate tasmāt na parvatavartī tatra vahniriti na 
kvacid evaṃvidhasya pratiprayogāvaskarasya nivṛttiriti sarvānumānābhāvo 
bhavatāṃ bhavet || 11 ||

	atra parābhiprāyaḥ-

tavaivāyaṃ virodhaścet

	yasya hi bhūyo darśanagamyā vyāptiḥ tasyaivāyaṃ 
viśeṣāntarāśrayo viruddhāvyabhicāriprasaṅgadoṣaḥ nāsmākaṃ 
pratibandhanibandhanatvāt tasyāḥ |

sattvadhūmādayo hi hetavaḥ 
tādātmyatadutpattibhyāmanityāgnimātravyāptāḥ siddhā na 
tadviśeṣavyāptāḥ tadvyabhicārādvastuno rūpāntarasambhavācceti 
kutasteṣāṃ viruddhāvyabhicārisambhavaḥ |

	ācārya āha-

			na mānaṃ sādhvidaṃ yadi |
arthaśaktyā niruddhatvātsādhutvaṃ cāsya tatkutaḥ || 12 ||

	yadīdamanumānamapi na sādu na pratibandhamūlaṃ bhavet, 
bhavedevāsyā'pi pratiprayogasambhāvanā, yāvatā arthasya sādhyasya 
kartuḥ śaktiḥ kāryajanakatvaṃ tayā vyāptatvād asyāpi 
kartṛtvānumānasya sādhutvaṃ kāryānumānarūpatā iti 
tatpratiprayogodbhāvanaṃ kutaḥ, na hi vastubalapravṛtte'numāne pratiyogī 
sambhavati ityuktaṃ yataḥ || 12 ||

	nanu yathā viśeṣāśrayaḥ pratiheturna sambhavati tathā sādhye'pi 
viśeṣo na syāt tataśca sāmānye siddhasādhyatā doṣa evetyāha-

arthaprāptā viśeṣāśca nāgneranumitasya hi |
vinivārayituṃ śakyā mahato'pi prayatnataḥ || 13 ||

	na hyanyadeśasthena vahninā taddeśastho dhūmo janyata iti 
deśakālākāraniyatadhūmagrahaṇasāmarthyādagnestaddeśakālākāra-
yogavyavacchinnā viśeṣā anumīyanta eveti, na siddhasādhyatā doṣaḥ 
pramāṇasyaiva, viśeṣaniṣṭhatvāt naivaṃ pratihetvāśritaviśeṣaniṣṭhaḥ 
pramāṇavyāpārastasyāniyatabhedāśrayatvena samānyāśrayatvāditi 
tasya viśeṣāśrayo doṣaḥ kāryasamā jātiriti naiyāyikāḥ | yadāhuḥ-

sādhyenānugamāḥ kārye sāmānyenāpi sādhane |
sambandhibhedāddoṣoktirdoṣaḥ kāryasamo mataḥ ||

ityadoṣaḥ || 13 ||

	anye tu śrāvaṇaḥ śabdaḥ itivat siddhasādhānatvādayukto'yaṃ 
pakṣa ityāhuḥ, tān darśayati-

bījakarmāṇubhiḥ kecidatrāhuḥ siddhasādhyatām |

	jagataḥ kartṛpūrvakatvaṃ hi bhavadbhiḥ sādhyate, sa 
copādānakāraṇatayā bījaśabdavācyaḥ pradhānākhyaḥ siddha eva, 
tatkiṃ sādhyata iti sāṃkhyāḥ | evaṃ karmabhya eva jagat, atastānyeva 
kartṛbhūtāni siddhānīti jaiminīyāḥ | yadāhuḥ-

kasyaciddhetumātratvaṃ yadyadhiṣṭhātṛteṣyate |
karmabhiḥ sarvajīvānāṃ  tatsiddheḥ siddhasādhanam || iti |

	aṇava eva dṛṣṭaphalasya karmaṇaḥ kartāraḥ siddhāstatkiṃ sādhyate 
dṛṣṭaphalebhyo'nyasya karmaṇo'sambhavāditi laukāyatikāḥ | yadāhuḥ-

caitanyaviśiṣṭaḥ kāyaḥ puruṣaḥ | iti |

	etat krameṇa pratikṣipati-

na pūrvābhyāṃ viśiṣṭatvāddhiyā sādhyasya sā bhavet || 14 ||

	na tāvat pūrvābhyāṃ bījakarmabhyāṃ sā siddhasādhyatā bhavet, 
kuta ityāha- viśiṣṭatvād dhiyā sādhyasyeti | siddhasādhanatā hi 
pakṣadoṣaḥ, na cāsmābhirjagataḥ kartṛmātrapūrvakatvaṃ sādhyate, api tu 
viśeṣo buddhimatkartṛpūrvakatvamityuktam-

adṛṣṭakartṛkāyādikāryaṃ prajñāvatā kṛtam | iti |

	na ca bījasya karmaṇo vā baddhimattvamiti kutaḥ siddhasādhyatā | 
nanu cetanānyeva karmāṇīti saugatāḥ dānādicetanāvyatirekeṇa 
karmāntarasyānabhyupagamāt | astu evaṃ viśeṣe'rjane, 
anekabhāvājitānāṃ tu teṣāmanekavāsanā, rūpatvenāvijñātānāmapi 
sattvābhyupagamānna kiñcidetat || 14 ||

	lokāyatānāmapyāha-

sādhyasya dharmiṇopātteḥ kāyāderna parairapi |

	na parairapi aṇubhiḥ siddhasādhyatā sādhyadharmiṇaḥ 
tanukaraṇabhuvanāderupāttatvāt, na ca bhavatāṃ tanukaraṇāderaṇavaḥ 
kartāraḥ siddhā iti kutaḥ siddhasādhyatā | nanu ghaṭādesta eva kartāraḥ 
siddhāḥ satyam, ata eva te'tra dṛṣṭāntīkṛtāḥ | nanu jalabudbudavat jīvā 
vicitradehayuktā dṛṣṭebhya eva kāraṇebhyo 
vicitrasvabhāvebhyastadanvayavyatirekānuvidhānād bhavantīti anuminumaḥ 
| na, asiddhatvāt kāraṇasya hi vegetaravātavarṣāderbhede kāryāṇāṃ 
jalabudbudādīnāṃ mahattvāmahattvādiviśeṣaḥ siddha eva, na ca 
jīvānāṃ kāraṇabhedaḥ siddhaḥ śukraśoṇitāderaviśeṣāt, 
kāraṇabhedamantareṇa ca kāryabhede viśvasyāhetukatvaprasaṅgaḥ, 
ahetośca deśakālākāraniyamāyogādityuktam | nanu 
śukraśoṇitādīnāmapi svarūpabhedo'styeva | yadyevam, ghaṭapaṭavad 
bhinnatvād bhinnarūpameva sarvadā kāryaṃ kuryuriti kāryāṇāṃ 
jīvatayā

samānarūpatānupapattiḥ | na śukraśoṇitādyātmanā kāraṇānāmapi 
sadṛśatvāditi cet, tarhi kāryāṇāmapi sadṛśatvaṃ syāditi 
jīvānāmṛddhimattvetaratvādiviśeṣo na syāt | nanu sadṛśatve'pi 
ayaskāntamaṇyādīnāmiva teṣāṃ keṣāñcit kaścid adṛṣṭa eva 
dhanādyākarṣakaḥ svabhāvo'stīti | nanu so'pyahetuko na 
sambhavatītyuktam | yadyevamadṛṣṭahetuko bhaviṣyati | na 
tarhyadṛṣṭakāraṇapratikṣepa iti yatkiñcidetat ||

	evaṃ bījādīnāṃ kartṛtvamabhyupagamyoktam | adhunā tu tadeva na 
sambhavatītyāha-

nācaitanyātpravṛttiḥ syātsvātantryādbījakarmaṇām || 15 ||

	pravṛttau ca nivṛttau ca kārakāṇāṃ ya īśvaraḥ sa 
svātantryātkartetyuktam | na cācetanānāṃ tatra svātantryamataḥ 
kāraṇatve'pi na kartṛtā || 15 ||

	acenasyāpi kartṛtvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti parābhiprāyamāha-

payovaccet

yadāhuḥ sāṃkhyāḥ

vatsavivṛddhinimittaṃ kṣīrasya yathā pravṛttirajñasya |
puruṣavimokṣanimittaṃ tathā pravṛttiḥ pradhānasya || iti |

atrottaramāha-

		na sādhyatvānmṛddaṇḍāderadarśanāt |

	na hi payasaḥ svātantryaṃ siddhyati acetanatvāt, mṛddaṇḍādivat 
tadapi kartṛprayuktamevetyuktam |

	nanu sattvatamasorakriyārūpatve rajasaḥ kriyātmakatvenopagamāt 
parasparopakārasvabhāvatvācca pradīpādīnāmiva guṇānāṃ kartṛtvaṃ 
bhaviṣyati | yaduktam-

prītyaprītiviṣādātmakāḥ prakāśapravṛttiniyamārthāḥ |
anyonyābhibhavāśrayajananamithunavṛttayaśca guṇāḥ || iti |

	etatpratiṣeddhumāha-

aṅgāṅgitvaṃ ca sattvādeḥ svābhāvyānnoktahetutaḥ || 16 ||

	acetanatvādeva teṣāṃ na svabhāvataḥ parasparopakāraḥ, kintu 
pradīpādivadeva cetanakartṛprayuktānāmiti nācetanasya kartṛtvasiddhiḥ || 
16 ||

kiñca-

sthitvā kriyā ca bījasya nācitaścetanaṃ vinā |

	yo yaḥ sthitvā pravartate sa cetanādhiṣṭhito yathā ghaṭādiḥ | 
samastavikāropasaṃhāreṇa ca mahāpralaye sthitimanubhūya sargādau 
pravartate pradhānam | atastasyāpi cetanāntareṇādhiṣṭhātrā 
bhavitavyamitīśvarasiddhiḥ ||

	atra paraḥ

nanu jñānaṃ pradhāne'sti

	pradhānakāryasya buddherjñānābhyupagamāt pradhānamapi 
jñānātmakaṃ sidhyatīti kimanyadanumīyate | yaduktam-

adhyavasāyo buddhirdharmo jñānaṃ virāga aiśvaryam |

sāttvikametadrūpam | iti ||

etadapi pratikṣipati-

			nācaitanyānmṛdādivat || 17 ||

	tadayuktaṃ vyaktāvyaktayoracetanatvena bhavadbhiruktatvāt, 
triguṇamaviveki viṣayaḥ sāmānyamacetanaṃ prasavadharmi | vyaktaṃ tathā 
pradhānam | iti || 17 ||

	kathaṃ tarhi tatkārye buddhau jñānaṃ bhavadbhiriṣyata ityāha-

astu vā śaktirūpatvādagrāhi tadasatsamam |

	buddhau jñāyate'neneti jñānaṃ 
prakāśātmakamasmābhirabhyupagamyate, na tu jñaptirūpameva tasya 
puruṣaikasvabhāvatvāt | tathābhūtamapi tat pradhāne kāraṇātmani na 
vyaktam, api tu śaktyātmanāvasthitamiti, adhyavasāyātmanaḥ 
svakāryasyākaraṇādasatsamam | tataścādhyavasāyātmano'pi 
jñānasyābhāvānnāsya kartṛtvamiti kartrantarasiddhiḥ ||

	yaduktam-

sthitvā kriyā ca bījasya nācitaścetanaṃ vinā |

iti tadanaikāntikamiti sāṃkhyāḥ-

sthitvā vyāpriyamāṇatvāt śambhorapi pareraṇam || 18 ||
na vā bījasya

yuṣmatparikalpitasyāpīśvarasya sthitvā kriyā ca vidyate, na ca ghaṭādivat 
cetanādhiṣṭhitatvamiti, anaikāntikatvādasya hetornātaḥ pradhānasyāpi 
cetanādhiṣṭhitatvam ||

kārikābhāgenānaikāntikatvaṃ pariharati-

			no sāmyaṃ prajñāvānīśvaro yataḥ |

	atra hi prakaraṇādācaitanyaṃ hetuviśeṣaṇaṃ gamyata iti 
nānaikāntikatā ||

	nanu kāryānekatvaṃ kartranekatvena vyāptamityatrāpi 
ghaṭapaṭāderivaṃ tanukaraṇabhuvanādeḥ kāryasyonakatvāt pācakādivat 
svaparaprayuktānekakartranumānaṃ yuktamato neśvarasiddhiriti | yadāha 
maṇḍanaḥ-

sānniveśādimatsarvaṃ buddhimaddhetu yadyapi |
prasiddhyetsanniveśāderekakāraṇatā kutaḥ ||

iti paramatamupakṣipya parākaroti-

anekaṃ devadattādi dṛṣṭaṃ loke dhiyānvitam || 19 ||

sthitvā pravartamānaṃ hi pākādāvapareritam |
pareritamapītyasmāt saṃśayo na maheśvare || 20 ||

	bhavedetat, yadi parasparāṅgabhāvo'sya tanukaraṇabhuvanādeḥ 
kāryajātasya siddho na bhavet | tasmiṃstu sati prasādādyavayavātmanaḥ 
kāryajātasya sthapatyādiriva eka eva sātiśayajñānādiyuktaḥ kartā 
sidhyati na bahava ityadoṣaḥ | ataśca yadapyāha-

rathādyavayavā nānātakṣanirmāpitā api |
dṛśyante jagati prāya upakāryopakārakāḥ ||

iti tadanenaiva pratikṣiptamekabuddhyā vinā nānākartṛnirmāpitānāmapi 
rathādyavayavānāmupakāryopakārakabhāvāsiddheḥ || 20 ||

	yadyevaṃ so'pi sthapatyādivadanyaprayukto bhaviṣyatīti, 
etattiraskṛtaye hetumāha-

upariṣṭādyato devaṃ vakṣyāmo'tiśayāṅkitam |

yatastameva paraprakarṣayuktaṃ pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ, tato naiṣa doṣa 
iti ||

paroktaṃ codyadvayamanyadapi darśayati-

āste pratīkṣamāṇastu supte jagati kiṃ haraḥ || 21 ||

sarvadā yena sargādi svatantro'pi karoti hi |
anukampāparatvācca sukhādyeva sṛjatvasau || 22 ||

mohaduḥkhādi yatpuṃsāṃ kurute tanna yujyate |

	prayojanavaśena prekṣāvantaḥ kāryaṃ kurvanti | na ca tasya 
mahāpralaye prajāpateḥ sargākaraṇe prayojanamutpaśyāmaḥ | yadāhuḥ-

prayojanamanuddiśya na mando'pi pravartate |
na ca prayojanaṃ tena syātprajāpatikarmaṇā || iti |

	atha dayayaiva sarvadā pravartate nātmaprayojanenetyucyate, 
tatrāpyāhuḥ-

sṛjecca śubhamevaikamanukampāprayojitaḥ |
athāśubhādvinā sṛṣṭiḥ sthitirvā nopapadyate ||

ātmādhīnābhyupāye hi bhavetkiṃ nāma duṣkaram |
jagaccāsṛjatastasya kiṃ nāmeṣṭaṃ na sidhyati || iti |

etasya  tāvaccodyābhāsatvamāha-

nahi sanmānasiddhatvādasattvaṃ parikalpyate || 23 ||

codyairitthaṃ prakārastu parairupahṛtairapi |

	na hi pramāṇasiddhaṃ vastu puruṣakalpanābhiranyathā kartuṃ 
śakyaṃ tāsāṃ tatpratibandhābhāvena tatrāpravṛtteḥ | yadāhuḥ-

avikalamanumānaṃ vidyate kartṛsiddhau
yadi capalavikalpairīdṛśaiḥ kāryasiddhiḥ |
avikalamanumānaṃ nāsti vā kartṛsiddhau
yadi capalavikalpairīdṛśaiḥ kāryasiddhiḥ || iti |

tatra prathamaṃ codyamapākaroti-

bījānāmaṅkurakaraṃ kālakṣetrādyapekṣate || 24 ||

yathā kṛṣībalo loke tathaiva parameśvaraḥ |
puṃsāṃ sambhogakālaṃ tu paripākaṃ ca karmaṇām || 25 ||

jagatprasūtikālaṃ ca jagadyonerapekṣate |

	aviratānantabhogasādhanatvenāpacitataraśaktikatvāt prakṛteḥ 
kāryakāraṇānāmiva kṣetrādīnāmiva vāvaśyaṃ 
sarvakāryopasaṃhāreṇa kasyāñcidapi daśāyāṃ viśramaḥ 
pralayābhidhāno'bhyupagantavya iti vakṣyāmaḥ | tatra cāsya 
bhogasādhanakṣamatvotpādanaṃ kṛṣībalādeḥ 
kṣetrabījādipratīkṣāyāmiva prayojanamiti na niṣprayojanatā ||

	dvitīyamapi codyaṃ pariharati-

yena yādṛgvidhaṃ karma kṛtaṃ tasya tathāvidham || 26 ||

vidadhāti phalaṃ puṃsaḥ karmāpekṣitayā prabhuḥ |

nāsmābhirīśvarāt sukhaduḥkhādiphalaṃ 
puṃsāmadvaitavādibhiriveṣyate yenaiṣa doṣaḥ syāt | yaduktam-

na buddhibhedaṃ janayedajñānaṃ karmasaṅginām |
joṣayetsarvakarmāṇi vidvān yuktaḥ samācaran || iti |

	api tu jyotiṣṭomabrahmahatyādervastubhūtātsvakarmataḥ 
śubhāśubhādanuṣṭhitāditi ko'yaṃ paryanuyogaḥ | yadyevaṃ 
puruṣavyāpārādeva phalamiti kimīśvareṇa | satyam, yadi 
karmasamanantarameva bhojanāderiva jyotiṣṭomādeḥ phalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ 
bhavet, tatrāmutrikaṃ phalaṃ naihikamiti tasyāpi kāryatve sati 
kālāntarabhāvitatvāt kṛṣyādiphalasyeva saṃvidhānarakṣādikartrā 
puruṣamātrasya vakṣyamāṇanayenāsambhavāt tataḥ sātiśaya- 
jñānādimatānyena bhāvyamiti karmasadbhāve'pi īśvarasiddheradoṣaḥ |

	athānantapuruṣakarmaphalasaṃvidhānādāvasya prayojanābhāvāt 
pravṛttirayuktā | yadāhuḥ-

bahuvyāpāratāyāśca kleśo bahutaro bhavet || iti |

atrāpyāha-

idameva dayālutvaṃ yattabhauvopapādayet || 27 ||

anyathā karmavaiphalyaṃ harāsattva ivāpatet |

	na | atra dayayaivāprāptānāthapālana iva nṛpatyādeḥ 
pravṛttisiddhiḥ | anyathā prayojanābhāvāt tasyātra pravṛttyanabhyupagame 
saṃvidhānādikartrā vinā kālāntarabhāviphalānāṃ kṛṣyādīnāmiva 
karmaṇāṃ vaiphalyamīśvarābhāvapakṣa iva bhavediti 
saṃsārasyābhāvāt nāmiśraṃ pariṇamate iti nyāyena 
malasyāpyapariṇatermokṣābhāva iti sarvocchedaprasaṅgaḥ |

	atra paraḥ-

asvātantraprasaṅgaḥ syātranu karmādyapekṣiṇaḥ || 28 ||

	sāpekṣo hyasamartho bhavati | yadāhuḥ-

tathā cāpekṣamāṇasya svātantryaṃ pratihanyate | iti |

etat pariharati-

svātantryānyāprayojyaṃ yatsvayamanyattvapekṣate |
prayuṅkte kāraṇādīni taiśca naiva prayujyate || 29 ||

kartuḥ svātantryametaddhi na karmādyanapekṣitā |

	nṛpatyādīnāṃ hyanyena svātantryeṇāprayojyatvaṃ yat yacca 
svayameva gajaturagādisādhanānāmāharaṇamāhṛtānāṃ ca 
yathopayogaṃ niyogastat svātantryamucyate, na tu 
nigrāhyānugrāhyakarmānapekṣayaiva nigrahānugrahakarmānuṣṭhānam | 
unmādo hi sa kathyate, na tu svātantryamiti | evaṃ bhagavato'pi 
karmāpekṣitayā na svātantryahāniranyaprayojyatvābhāvāditi |

	atrāhuḥ yo yatkartṛsvabhāvaḥ sa tatkarotyeva yathā vahnirauṣṇyam, 
na ceśvaro mahāpralaye kāryaṃ karotīti na kartṛsvabhāvaḥ iti | etat 
pratikṣipati-

na ca kṛtsnopasaṃhāre'pyāste sarvakriyojjhitaḥ || 30 ||

jagacchaktyātmanā dhatte jagadyoniṃ ca niṣkriyām |
vipācayati karmāṇi puṃso viśramayatyapi || 31 ||

anugṛhṇātyanugrāhyanirodhyānniruṇaddhyapi |

	jagat janmagamanādiyuktaṃ tattvabhāvabhūtabhuvanātmakaṃ 
kalādikṣityantaṃ sarvameva vyaktirūpatayopasaṃhṛtya śaktyātmanā 
tatprakṛtiṃ ca tadānīmupasaṃhṛtajagadvyāpārāṃ dhatta 
ityuktamācāryeṇa satkāryopanyāsato bhogamokṣe | karmāṇi ca tadānīṃ 
mandavegatayā prakṛtisaṃskārībhūtāni pācayati, puruṣāśca 
bhavabhramaṇakhinnān viśramayati, tadānīṃ pariṇatamalāṃśca 
kāṃścidanugrāhayati, vijñānakevalapralayakevalayorapyanugrahasya 
śrutatvāt, nirodhyāṃścāpariṇatamalān niruṇaddhi, ityasiddhatvāt 
hetorasya nākartṛtvasiddhirīśvara iti | śrīmṛgendre'pi uktam-

svāpe'pyāste bodhayan bodhayogyān
rodhyān rudhyan pāyayan karmikarma |
māyāśaktirvyaktiyogyāḥ prakurvan
paśyan sarvaṃ yadyathā vastujātam ||

iti śrīnārāyaṇakaṇṭhasunorbhaṭṭarāmakaṣṭhasya kṛtau 
nareśvaraparīkṣāprakāśe īśvaraparīkṣānāma
dvitīyaṃ kāṇḍam || 2 ||



yogatantra � granthamālā

[15]

śrīmadācāryasadyojyotirviracitā

nareśvaraparīkṣā

śrīmadrāmakaṇṭhācāryakṛtayā

prakāśa ṭīkayā

saṃvalitā

kulapateḥ pro. rāmamūrtiśarmaṇaḥ prastāvanayā vibhūṣitā

sampādakaḥ

ācārya śrīrāmajīmālavīyaḥ

ācāryo'dhyakṣaśca
sāṃkhya- yogatantrāgamavibhāgasya
evaṃ
prati- kulapatiḥ
sampūrṇānandasaṃskṛtaviśvavidyālayasya
vārāṇasī

vārāṇasyām

2056 tame vaikramābde	1921 tame śakābde	2000 tame khraistābde



atha tṛtīyaṃ kāṇḍam

	tadiyatā svadarśanasiddhamīśvaraṃ pramāṇataḥ pratipādya 
paradarśanaprasiddheśvarasvarūpaṃ tatpratikṣepāyāha-

parātmā sarvavittasmāccidacinti bhavanti ca |

	sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā ca bhavadbhirīśvaraḥ kathyate, sa ca 
paramātmaiva samastacetanācetanopādānakāraṇabhūto'stu kiṃ 
vyatirikteneśvareṇeti pariṇatavedāntavidaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ-

puruṣa evedaṃ sarvaṃ yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhāvyam | iti |

	nanu cetanānāṃ nityatvamuktamata kathaṃ tato bhāvaḥ | satyam, 
abhyupagamyāpi etaducyata ityadoṣaḥ |

	etatpratikṣipati-

tasmiṃśca bījamukhye tu sarvamuktamanuktavat || 1 ||

	yuktametat, yadi bījatayopādānakāraṇatvenāsāvabhyupagato 
bhavadbhirna bhavet, tathābhūte tu tasminnabhyupagate 
sarvametannopapadyata eva ||

	tatraitāvannopapadyata ityāha-

na nimittaṃ vinaikatvāttadbījaṃ kāryakṛnmatam |

	yadbījaṃ tatsahakāryapekṣayā kāryajananasvabhāvaṃ na 
kevalameva, yathā śālyādibījam | jagato bījaṃ ca paramātmā 
bhavadbhiriṣyate, na ca tasyānyaḥ sahakārī bhavati puruṣa evedaṃ 
sarvamityabhyupagamato dvaitapakṣasya pratikṣiptatvāt tato 
jagallakṣaṇakāryānupapattireva | taduktam- nāmiśraṃ pariṇamate iti ||

	na ca kośakāreṇāyamanaikāntiko heturityāha-

yato lālākuvindo'pi stambhādikamapekṣate || 2 ||

	so'pi hi lālāmātreṇa kośamabhiracayan 
stambhādyādhāramapekṣata eva | yaduktaṃ jaiminīyairapi-

na ca niḥsādhanaḥ kartā kaścitsṛjati kiñcana |
nādhāreṇa vinā sṛṣṭirūrṇanābheriveṣyate ||
prāṇināṃ bhakṣaṇāccāpi tasya lālā pravartate | iti || 2 ||

	kiñca viruddhayorekopādānatā nopapadyata ityāha-

cidacijjanakatvācca cidaciddharmabhāgbhavet |

	yathābhūte hi kāryaṃ tathābhūtameva tasyopādānakāraṇaṃ 
yathā hemamṛdādisvabhāvasya rucakaghaṭādeḥ hemādi, cetanācetanaṃ 
ca kāryaṃ paramātmopādānaṃ bhavadbhiriṣyata iti tasyāpi 
cetanācetanaviruddhasvabhāvatāprasaṅgaḥ | na, atathābhūtādapi 
sūryakāntādervahnyādyutpattidarśanato'naikāntikatvāt | na, ata eva 
sūryātapasyaiva sūryakāntasahakāriṇastatropādānakāraṇatvāt | etena 
candrakāntādapāṃ vyajanādeśca pavanādīnāmutpāde candraprabhā-
pavanādīnāmeva candrakāntavyajanādisahakāriṇāmupādānatva-
manusandheyam | avavarakādiṣvapi tūlādibhramaṇataḥ pavanasattāsiddheḥ 
| gomayādervṛścikotpāde'pyasmākaṃ cetanasyānutpādāt 
taccharīropādānakāraṇasādṛśyamastyevetyanaikāntikatvābhāvād atra 
cetanācetanaviruddhasvabhāvatāprasaṅgastadavastha eva ||

	kimata ityāha-

ekasminneva kāle ca tadvirodhānna tadbhavet || 3 ||

	viruddhayośca tayorvirodhādeva bhāvābhāvayoriva naikatra yugapat 
sambhava iti tasyāpi ekasya viruddhadvayasvabhāvasyāsambhava eva || 3 ||

	cetanasyopādānatvābhyupagamenaitaduktamadhunā tu tadeva 
nopapadyata ityāha-

upādānatvatastaddhi tattvādivadacetanam |
prayojanamataḥ kārye prajñāvantamapekṣate || 4 ||

	upādānakāraṇaṃ hyacetanasvabhāvameva yathā mṛdādi, 
upādānakāraṇaṃ ca paramātmābhidhānaṃ bhavadbhiriṣyata iti 
tadapyacetanameva, acetanaṃ ca cetanādhiṣṭhitaṃ kāryakāraṇasvabhāvaṃ 
mṛdādi niścitamatastasyāpi cetanenādhiṣṭhātrā bhavitavyamiti 
īśvarasiddhiḥ | kathaṃ tarhi puruṣa evedaṃ sarvam iti śrutiḥ | ata eva 
tādarthyāt āyurghṛtam itivadupacāreṇa ityavirodhaḥ || 4 ||

anye tu-

mahataḥ paramavyaktamavyaktātpuruṣaḥ paraḥ |
puruṣānna paraṃ kiñcitsā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ ||

ityādiśrutisiddhaṃ puruṣasyaivātreśvaramataḥ kimanyenetyāhuḥ | tān 
pratikṣipati-

ātmānutpannadehādibhinnāvyaktadṛkkriyaḥ |
bījādyato na jānāti na ca kiñcitkaroti ca || 5 ||

	puruṣo'pyanutpannakāryakaraṇaḥ svadehādau heturvā syād 
utpannakāryakaraṇo vā | tatra bījādavyaktākhyādanutpannaṃ dehādi 
yasya, abhinne'vyatirikte'nabhivyakte ca dṛkkriye yasya sa yataḥ 
suṣuptāvasthanaravanna kiñcijjānāti karoti vā tataḥ kathaṃ svadehādeḥ 
kartā || 5 ||

nāpi dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ pratyakṣavirodhādityāha-

na cāpi labdhadehādiḥ svadehādeḥ sa kāraṇam |
saṃsiddhameva tattasya kutaścid dṛśyate yataḥ || 6 ||

	na hi parapurapraveśino yogina ivātmanaḥ sadehasya 
svadehāntarakartṛtvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ taddehādi tasya pitrāderhetvantarādeva 
dṛśyate yataḥ || 6 ||

	kiñca-

nimittaphalajātīnāṃ dehādeścāpyabodhataḥ |
bījādīraṇasāmarthyavirahānna parasya ca || 7 ||

	yo hi kartā sa kāryasya ghaṭāderiva mūlyādi 
nimittamudakadhāraṇādi phalaṃ mṛdādyupādānaṃ śarīracakrādi tu 
sahakāri jānan kartṛtvabhāvaḥ siddhaḥ | na ca puruṣaḥ svaśarīrasya 
nimittaṃ prāgarjitāni karmāṇi vicitrabhogātmakaṃ phalaṃ 
viśiṣṭaśukraśoṇitādyupādānamāhārādi sahakāri ca jñātuṃ śakta iti 
tadanabodhāt hetorasya svaśarīranimittabījādipreraṇasāmarthyābhāvataḥ 
parasyāpi labdhadehasya puruṣasyākartṛkatvamiti || 7 ||

svasiddhena bhavatāmatrānaikāntikatvamiti paraḥ

adṛṣṭvaiva mano yadvanniyuṅkte pudgalastathā | 
niyokṣyate hi karmādīn

yathaivāntaḥkaraṇaṃ svarūpeṇājñātvā puruṣo niyuṅkta iti 
bhavadbhiriṣyate tathaiva bījakarmādīnapi svaśarīrahetūnajñātvā 
prayokṣyata ityajñātṛtvasyākartṛtvasiddhāvanaikāntikatā |

	kārikābhāgenaitatparihāraḥ

				asiddhatvānusādhanam || 8 ||

	bhavedanaikāntiko yadyantaḥkaraṇasyādṛśyatvamasmākaṃ 
siddhaṃ syāt, tattu vṛttyātmanā sarvadā jñānaśakterjñeyamevetyuktaṃ 
tattvasaṅgrahe-

ravivatprakāśarūpo yadi nāma mahāṃstathāpi karmatvāt |
karaṇāntarasāpekṣaḥ śakto grāhayitumātmānam || iti |

tasyāpi jñātasya preryatvāt jñānaṃ vinātra kartṛtvānupapattireva |
abhyupagamyāntaḥkaraṇasyādarśanamanaikāntikatvaṃ pariharati-

tatrāpi prerako jñātā sarvastasyāmito yataḥ |

	kāryatvāttasyāpi ghaṭāderiva cetanena prerakeṇa 
bhavitavyamitīśvarasiddhiḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ-

		dhiyo yo na pracodayāt | iti |

naiyāyikā aṇumanasorādyaṃ karmādṛṣṭakāritamiti |

ata eveśvarādhīnaḥ pumān bhoge'pi paṭhyate || 9 ||

	tathā cāhuḥ

na devā daṇḍamādāya rakṣanti paśupālavat |
yaṃ hi rakṣitumicchanti buddhyā saṃyojayanti tam || iti ||

	atra codayanti-

pitarau nanu putrādeḥ kuruto bhogasādhanam |

	mātā ca pitā ca tāveva yataḥ putrasya duhiturvā 
śarīramutpādayantau dṛṣṭau, ataḥ kathaṃ nātra puṃsaḥ kartṛtvamiti |

	etat pratikṣipati-

na dehamātrataḥ sthūlādvyatiriktaṃ kalādikam || 10 ||

tadyogaṃ vṛttilābhaṃ ca nityavyāpakapakṣayoḥ |
ayonijau ca tau kāryaṃ kuryātāṃ no parasyahi || 11 ||

	sthūlameva dehaṃ tāvutpādayantau siddhau na tu 
vyatiriktamindriyādi, nāpi śarīrendriyābhyāṃ puṃso yogam, nāpi ca 
nityatvāt vyāpakatvādātmanaḥ sarvaśarīrayoge'pi viśiṣṭenaiva śarīreṇa 
tajjñānakriyayorvṛttilābhaṃ kurutaḥ nāpyayonijaṃ 
maṣakamakṣikādiśarīraṃ tau kartuṃ sambhāvyete hīti yasmāttasmāt na 
yuṣmatparikalpitasyāsya buddhyādeḥ parasya puṃso'tra 
kartṛtvamityadhyāhāraḥ tataḥ kartrantarasiddhiḥ || 11 ||

	evaṃ sthūlaśarīrakartṛtvamabhyupagamyaitaduktaṃ tadeva tu 
nopadyata iti darśayituṃ parāśaṅkāmāha-

pitarau kiṃ na kurvāte śarīrotpattaye budhāḥ |
bījasambandhamātraṃ cet

	yathā bījasambandhamātreṇaiva tayoḥ śarīre 
kartṛtvamevamindriyādāvapi bhaviṣyatīti kutaḥ kartrantarasiddhiḥ ||

	kārikābhāgenaitat pratikṣipati-

				tāvatā naiva tatkṛtam || 12 ||

	kāyādeḥ kāryādanyatvāt tatkārakabījasannidhānātmanaḥ 
kāryasya || 

tathā hi-

mṛccakramātrayogena kalaśo na kṛto bhavet |

nahi anyasmin kṛte'nyat kṛtaṃ bhavati ||

tadevānayoranyatvamāha-

racanā tatra kartavyā nānārthārādhanakṣamā || 13 ||

mṛdo'nyadevodakadhāraṇādyarthakāri ghaṭasya svarūpaṃ yataḥ ||

dārṣṭāntike'pyanyatvaṃ yojayati-

tathā kāye'pi racanā pumarthārādhanakṣamā |
dṛṣṭā sātaḥ parijñeyā kartṛtaḥ kuśalād budhaiḥ || 14 ||

	evaṃ bījasaṃyogarūpatvāt kāryādanyadeva nānābhogasādhanaṃ 
kāryamiti tasyānyenaiva sātiśayena kartrā bhavitavyamiti ||

atra kārikābhāgena parābhiprāyaḥ-

bimbātmā cet

	śukraśoṇitayoḥ kalalāvasthātyāgena ghanāvasthā bimbamucyate, 
tasmādevāhārādyupakṛtāt sā racanā na kartrantarāditi ||

	para etat pratikṣipati-

				na tatraiva hyanyādṛgapi dṛśyate |

	na bimbamātrāttasyāviśeṣādekarūpaiva putrādeḥ sarvasya racanā 
syāditi dṛṣṭavirodhaḥ ||
