punaḥ kārikābhāgena paraḥ

marutaścet

pavanasyaiva sarvatra racanākartṛtvasiddheḥ |

ācārya āha-

na tasyāpi svātantryaṃ citihānitaḥ || 15 ||

pūrvatrāpoditaṃ samyak svātantryaṃ bījagakarmaṇoḥ |

	yathā bījasyopādānakāraṇasya karmaṇaścācaitanyāt kartṛtvaṃ 
niṣiddhaṃ dvitīyasmin kāṇḍe tathā pavanasyāpi niṣeddhavyameva |

kiñca-

na cāpyeva vidhaṃ kāryaṃ prakṛṣṭajñāninā vinā || 16 ||

teṣu teṣu pradeśeṣu tvagāderyena na sthitiḥ |
yeṣu yeṣu sthitaṃ tatsyādaṇvarthakṛtaye'kṣamam || 17 ||

	nahi evaṃvidhalakṣaṇaṃ śarīrādikāryaṃ 
prakṛṣṭatarajñānādiyuktaṃ kartā vinā sambhavati yena teṣu teṣvatra 
śarīre śrotranetrādiṣu navadvāreṣu tvagādeḥ ādigrahaṇāt netrādeḥ 
pādatalādiṣu sthitirna dṛśyate yeṣvavasthitaṃ 
puruṣārthasādhanāyāsamarthaṃ bhavediti na kiñcijjñayoḥ kartṛtvam || 17 
||

atra punaḥ paraḥ

svargādyuddeśataḥ karma kurvataiva nareṇa tat | 
prayuktaṃ tacca dehādyairna vinā phalasādhakam || 18 ||

ato'bhisandhimātreṇa puṃsā sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ tviti |

na vayaṃ sākṣāt puruṣasya śarīrādisarvakartṛtvaṃ brūmaḥ yenaiṣa 
doṣaḥ syāt, api tu jyotiṣṭomena yajeta ityādi śrutisiddhaṃ svargādi karma 
kurvatā | tadapūrvākhyasaṃskāradvāreṇa prayuktam, tattu 
harītakyādiviśiṣṭapariṇāmākṣepeṇa virekādiphalamiva 
śarīrādyākṣepeṇa svaphalaṃ svargādi sādhayatīti phalābhisandhānena 
jyotiṣṭomādimātrameva kurvatā puruṣeṇa tadākṣiptaṃ sarvameva 
tadanukaraṇādikṛtamucyate, na punaḥ sākṣāt karmaṇīva tatrāsya 
kartṛtvaṃ pramāṇābhāvādityadoṣaḥ |

	noddeśasyānaikāntikatvādityāha-

na vyāpakamidaṃ yasmānnirayādau na vidyate || 19 ||

	na hi narakaduḥkhādyuddeśena brahmahatyādikarma puruṣaiḥ kriyate, 
api tu dṛṣṭaphalānusandhānena kāmādereveti 
narakaphalādyanupapattisteṣām || 19 ||

	atha
 avīcau narake kalpaṃ vaseyurbrahmaghātakāḥ |

iti śrutyantareṇa tatkartustadbhavatīti ucyate | bhavatu, utpādakastu asya na 
puruṣo'bhisandhānena vinā kartṛtvāsiddheriti tato'nyaḥ caurasya 
nigrahādau nṛpativadīśvaro'bhyupagantavyaḥ | tathā hi-

vyāptaṃ yaccāpi kumbhādi dṛṣṭaṃ yaddhīmatā kṛtam |
tattāvataiva tadvacca nirayādi bhaviṣyati || 20 ||

	yat kāryatvena vyāptaṃ ghaṭādi tat dhīmatā kṛtaṃ siddham, 
atastadapi tasya narakādi kāryatvenaiva ghaṭādivad dhīmatā kṛtamiti 
gamyate || 20 ||

	na ca vyavahitaphaloddeśenānuṣṭhitānāmapi karmaṇāṃ 
kartṛvyāpārāntarānapekṣayā phalajanakatvena vyāptiḥ siddhetyāha-

na cāpyuddeśamātreṇa tṛptiḥ syātkarṣaṇāditaḥ |
kriyāntaraṃ vinā tasmāt sāmagryantarato bhavet || 21 ||

tadvaddharmādapi kṛtātsvargādi syātkriyāntarāt |
tacceśvaraṃ vinā neti sa siddhaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 22 ||

	āhāraphalādyarthatvena hi kṛṣyādīni lokaḥ karoti na vyasanitayā | 
na ca kṛṣṭimātrādeva tatteṣāṃ phalamapi tu 
tadvardhanarakṣaṇāderbhojanāntāt kartrantaravyāpārādapi | tadvat 
svargādyuddeśenānuṣṭhitānna jyotiṣṭomādeḥ karmamātrāt svargo'pi tu 
tadbhogayogyaśarīrādijananakarmāntarasāpekṣāditi

tasyāpi karmāntarakartrā sātiśayena bhāvyamitīśvarasiddhiḥ || 22 ||

nanu so'pi puruṣa eva bhaviṣyati | yadāhuḥ-

kleśakarmavipākāśayairaparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvaraḥ | iti |

atrāpyāha-

pumbhiścāpekṣyamāṇatvātsa cānāvṛtadṛkkriyaḥ |
tataścāpāstapumbhāvastadvaidharmyādviniścitaḥ || 23 ||

	puruṣā hyavidyāvṛtatvāt kiñcijjñāḥ kiñcitkartāraścocyante | 
puruṣāśakyaśarīrakaraṇādikāryānyathānupapatyā tu īśvaraḥ 
kalpyamānastadvilakṣaṇo'nāvṛtadṛkkriyaḥ sidhyati na puruṣaviśeṣa eva 
tasyāpi puruṣatvāt tatrāsāmarthyaṃ sāmarthye vā na puruṣatvaṃ yataḥ || 
23 ||

	puruṣavādī siddhāntavādinaṃ pratyāha-

puṃstvādye kalpayantīśaṃ sarvajñānakriyojjhitam |
asiddhahetutaste'taḥ sthitā hyātmani darśanāt || 24 ||

	puṃsaḥ sarvajñatvādivirahāt tanukaraṇanirmāṇāsāmarthyamiti, 
avaśyaṃ tannirmāṇasamarthena sarvavidānyena bhavitavyamiti yaiḥ 
puruṣaviśeṣavādināmasmadasiddhādeva hetorīśvaro'nyaḥ sādhyate 
te'pi īśvarabuddhyā ātmanyeva puruṣaviśeṣe vyavasthitāḥ || 24 ||

	kathamityāha-

pumāneva hi kasmāttairneṣṭo'nāvṛtadṛkkriyaḥ |
mahatāpi prayatnena yadīśaḥ parikalpyate || 25 ||

	tanukaraṇādikāryānyathānupapattyā hi taiḥ sarvajñatvādiyuktaḥ 
kartā kaścit kalpanīyaḥ sa ca puruṣaviśeṣa eva kalpyo nānyaḥ | evaṃ hi na 
dharmantaramapi kalpitaṃ bhavatīti || 25 ||

	siddhāntavādī tvāha-

pramāṇābhāvato neṣṭaḥ sarvasarvajñadoṣataḥ |

	bhavedasiddho heturyadi puruṣaviśeṣasyeśvaratāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ 
syāt tattu nāsti asmadādivat tasyāpi buddhyādiyuktatvena 
bhavabhirabhyupagamāt tadayuktatve hi

mukta eveśvaraḥ syāt, na tu puruṣaviśeṣa iti asmatpakṣa eva | 
buddhyādiyogācca sarvajñatvaṃ tatpuruṣāntare'pi syāditi sarvasya 
sarvajñatāprasaṅgaḥ |

	kimata ityāha-

na syustataśca śāstrāṇi loke cotsārakādayaḥ || 26 ||

svātmanā pāpakartṛtvaṃ na hi sarvārthadarśinaḥ |
yujyate dṛśyate vāsya duḥkhaṃ nānāprakārajam || 27 ||

	tataḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvajñatvāt sarvasya śāstraparokṣābhidhānādeḥ 
svayamanarthahetūpasevāyā nānāprakāraduḥkhavedanasya 
cābhāvaprasaṅgastadaprasaṅge hyasarvajñā eva 
pumāṃso'bhyupagantavyāstato nāsiddho heturiti 
puruṣavyatirikteśvarasiddhiḥ | kathaṃ tarhi puruṣānna paraṃ kiñcit 
ityādiśrutayaḥ | tadupakārāyaiva pravṛtterīśvarāderapi sarvasyetyadoṣaḥ 
|| 27 ||

	śāstramevākṛtakaṃ vedākhyaṃ 
vidhiniṣedhātmakasvājñāvyatikrameṇa puṃsaḥ pratyavāyaphalaṃ 
vidadhadīśvaro nānya iti jaiminīyā ityāha-

śāstrataḥ karmaniṣpattiḥ karmataśca kṛtātphalam |
śāstraṃ cākṛtakaṃ tena kiṃ hareṇeti

etat kārikābhāgena pratikṣipati-

						nārthavat || 28 ||

naitadyuktam || 28 ||

kuta ityāha-

karmasāphalyakaraṇamuktaṃ śambhoḥ prayojanam |
upariṣṭātpravakṣyāmaḥ pummuktikaraṇaṃ param || 29 ||

	uktameva yato vyavahitaphalaṃ karmakṛṣyādivaccidanadhiṣṭhitaṃ na 
kāryaṃ kartuṃ samarthamiti vakṣyāmaḥ || 29 ||

	nanu dṛṣṭe sati nādṛṣṭakalpanā yukteti | atrāpyāha-

svargādisiddhaye yadvat sthairyaṃ kḷptaṃ karmaṇaḥ |
maheśaḥ kalpyatāṃ tadvattaṃ vinā nāpi tadyataḥ || 30 ||

	yadyevaṃ dṛṣṭadevatoddeśāt vahnau somaprakṣepādyātmano 
jyotiṣṭomādeḥ puruṣakarmaṇo'pavṛktatvādāmutrikaphalasya 
svargāderanupapattireva | nanu svargādiphalaśrutyanyathānupapattyā

tadadṛṣṭena saṃskārarūpeṇānapavṛktamiti kalpyate, tarhi adṛṣṭasyāpi 
kalpanāsambhavāt īśvaro'pi kalpanīyastena vinā tasyāpi 
phalajanakatvāsiddheruktatvāt || 30 ||

	adhunā racanārūpatvāt śāstrasyākṛtakatvaṃ na sambhavatīti 
tatprāmāṇyānyathānupapattyāvaśyaṃ bhavadbhistatpraṇetā 
īśvaro'bhyupagantavya iti darśayituṃ śabdanityatvaṃ tāvat 
pratikṣeptumāha-

nityaṃ cedvarṇajātaṃ syāt sarvadā kiṃ na gṛhyate |

	upalabhyasvabhāvasya hi nityatve svabhāvāvināśāt 
sarvadaivopalabhyatāprasaṅgaḥ ||

	kārikābhāgena paraḥ

avyaktatvāttu

	na gṛhyate ityanuvartate | vyaktasya hi tasya sarvadopalabhyasvabhāvatā 
nāvyaktasya tenāvyaktatvānna gṛhyata iti ||

	ācārya āha-

tadvyakternityatve doṣanityatā || 31 ||

	śabdasya vyaktirapi yadi nityā tataḥ sarvadā kiṃ na gṛhyata ityasya 
doṣasya nityatvāpattireva || 31 ||

kārikābhāgena parābhiprāyaḥ-

anityā cet

athaitaddoṣabhayādanityā vyaktiḥ śabdasyetyucyate |

ācārya āha-

			kuto hetoḥ

kena pramāṇena bhavadbhiranityatvamavagatam | 

paraḥ pramāṇamāha-

					kadācidbhāvatastviti |

kadācitkatvāt sā vyaktiranityā vidyudādivat ||

atrāpyāha-

śabde'pi tatsamānatvādanityatvaṃ na vāryate || 32 ||

	yadyevaṃ sthānakaraṇābhighātādeḥ svakāraṇasya bhāve śabdaḥ 
kadācit upalabhyeta tadabhāve ca nopalabhyeta iti vidyudādivadanityo'stu, 
kimanyayā tadavyaktyā parikalpitayeti || 32 ||

	nanu tadabhāve'nupalambhaḥ stimitavāyūparuddhaśrotratvāt puṃso 
badhirasyeva śabdaviṣayo'nyathāsiddhastat na varṇānityatvasādhanāya 
paryāpta iti | tadayuktam, yataḥ

marutistimite mānaṃ na varṇāvarakaṃ kvacit |

	sthānakaraṇābhighātābhāvakāle vidyamānā api varṇāḥ 
stimitavāyūparuddhatvāt śrotreṇa na gṛhyanta ityatra pramāṇaṃ nāstyeva 
| udrikte hi vāyau tatra śūlādayaḥ syuriti stimitagrahaṇam | kiñca 
śrotroparodhasyāviśeṣāt sthānakaraṇābhighātakāle'pi varṇā na 
śrūyeran ||

	nanu tajjena marutā tadanīmabhivyaktatvāt śrūyanta iti | na, 
asiddhatvādityāha-

pṛṣṭhasthārṇāgrahānnāpi varṇānāṃ vyañjako marut || 33 ||

	prakāśako hi vyañjako nāvarako viruddhatvāt, vāyuśca 
varṇānāmāvarako dṛṣṭo yatastatpṛṣṭhasthānāṃ 
vāyyanugatānāmarṇānāṃ varṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ nāsti nivāte hi 
śabdagrahaṇāt tato na vāyorvyañjakatvasiddhiḥ || 33 ||

	atha sthānakaraṇābhighātajena marutā na varṇopalabdhiryenaiṣa 
doṣaḥ syādapi tu śrotrābhyāśamāgatya śrotrastaimityaṃ nivartate tato 
varṇopabdhiriti | atrāpyāha-

kampādyabhāvato nāsāvāyāta iti gamyate |

	sparśaśabdadhṛtikampaliṅgo hi vāyuḥ, liṅgābhāve ca 
vaktṛmukhāt śrotuḥ śrotrābhyāśamāgacchatīti kuto'vagamyatām ||

	kiñca

karṇacchidre hi sampūrṇe sarvārṇagrahaṇaṃ bhavet || 34 ||

	vyañjakena tena śrotrarandhre vyāpte sati āvaraṇasya nivṛttatvāt 
sarvaśabdagrahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ || 34 ||

	atra paraḥ

sāmarthyaṃ vyañjakasyedṛggamyate kāryataḥ kila |
saṃśliṣṭamapi no varṇān vyanaktyekasya darśanāt || 35 ||

nityatve hi śabdasya grahaṇānyathānupapattyā sthānakaraṇābhighātajasya 
pavanasya śrotropakāradvāreṇa vyañjakatvaṃ yathā kalpyate 
tathaivaikaikavarṇopalambhāt pratiniyataśrotraśaktyupakāreṇa 
pratiniyatasthānakaraṇābhighātajasya vāyoḥ 
pratiniyatavarṇavyañjakaśaktikalpanāt, naiṣa prasaṅga iti || 35 ||

	etat pratikṣipati-

saṃsiddhe vyaktikartṛtve tasyedaṃ yuktimadbhavet |

	vyaktikartṛtve vyañjakatve tasya vāyoḥ siddhe kadācit tasya 
viśeṣasya varṇaviśeṣavyañjakatvamucyamānaṃ śobheta tadeva 
tvasiddhamityuktam ||

	abhyupagamyāpyāha-

na cāyaṃ vyañjako dharmaḥ pradīpādau pradṛśyate || 36 ||

	yuktaḥ cakṣuḥśrotrādīnāṃ vyañjakānāṃ rūpaśabdādau 
vyaṅgye śaktibhedo viṣayabhedād asya tu pradīpādeḥ rūpādivadeka eva 
śabdo viṣaya iti tadavāntarabhedena śaktibhedakalpanamayuktameva || 36 ||

	kiñca-

siddhe sthairye tu varṇānāṃ vyañjakaṃ parikalpyate |
sthairyaṃ teṣāṃ kuto hetoḥ

	santamase hi sparśādinā ghaṭādīnāṃ sattve siddhe 
pradīpādirvyañjakaḥ sidhyati, tatsiddhau hi mṛccakradaṇḍādiḥ kāraka eva 
uktaḥ | tadihāpi sthānakaraṇābhighātādīnāmabhāve'pi varṇajātasya 
sattvasiddhau yadi pramāṇaṃ bhavet, bhavedeva teṣāṃ vyañjakatvam | 
tadabhāve tu tatkārakataiva yukteti ||

	paraḥ kārikābhāgenātra pramāṇamāha-

					pratyabhijñānatastviti || 37 ||

	sa evāyaṃ varṇa iti sthiravastuviṣayāt pratyabhijñānākhyāt 
pratyakṣādeva teṣāṃ sthairyamiti || 37 ||

	etat parākartoti-

sandigdhaṃ pratyabhijñānaṃ tadatadbhāvadarśanāt |
śabde samyaktvamasyeti naitaddhetvantaraṃ vinā || 38 ||

taditi sthairyamataditi asthairyaṃ tayordvayorapi bhāve tasya 
pratyabhijñānasya darśanāt na vidmaḥ kimatra parvatādāviva 
sthairyaviṣayameva taduta dhārājvālādāvivāsthairyaviṣayatvāt, 
bhrāntamiti sandehānna sādhanam | ataḥ sthairyasiddhaye 
pramāṇāntarameva vaktavyam || 38 ||

	atha kvacit bhrāntadarśane'pi pratyabhijñānasya na sarvatra 
bhrāntatā anubhūyamānārthaviṣayatvādālocanajñānavat taduktam-

svataḥ sarvapramāṇānāṃ prāmāṇyamiti gṛhyatām |
nahi svato'satī śaktiḥ kartumanyena pāryate || iti |

	api tu bādhakasadbhāvādeveti | atrāpi bādhakaṃ pramāṇamāha-

anekatvamavarṇasya gamyate kakhagādivat |
samakālamanekaistu narairuccāryate yataḥ || 39 ||

	pratipuruṣamakārādayo varṇā bhinnā 
yugapadbahubhiruccāryamāṇatvāt kacaṭatapādivarṇavat | nanu 
vyañjakatvādeṣāṃ kārakatvamasiddhameva | satyam, yadi 
teṣāmabhāve'pi varṇasattve pramāṇaṃ syāt, tadabhāve tvabhāvasiddhau 
naiṣāṃ vyañjakatvam, api tu kārakataivetyuktaṃ tannāsiddhatā || 39 ||

	evamabhyupagamya pratyabhijñānaṃ 
bādhitatvādasādhanamityuktam, adhunā tu tadeva nopapadyata ityāha-

pratyakṣaṃ pratyabhijñānaṃ nākṣāduparatādyataḥ |
varṇeṣu vāsanāvyaktismaraṇādanugamyate || 40 ||

	nātra pratyabhijñānaṃ pratyakṣamapi tu tadābhāsa eva, yato 
nirvikalpānantaratvena tadviṣayāvasāyatayā tatpramāṇamiṣyate | 
yaduktam-

asti hyālocanaṃ jñānaṃ prathamaṃ nirvikalpakam |
bālamūkādivijñānasadṛśaṃ śuddhavastujam ||
tataḥ paraṃ punarvastudharmairjātyādibharyayā |
buddhyāvasīyate sāpi pratyakṣatvena sammatā || iti |

uccāritapradhvaṃsitvena copalambhāt 
sarvavarṇānāmindriyavyāpāroparatau pratyabhijñānaṃ bhavat ayamiti 
puro'vasthitārthā saṃvedanāt, na pratyakṣārthaviṣayamapi tu 
anubhavasaṃskāraprabodhātmanaḥ smaraṇādevopajāyamānaṃ sa eveti 
parāmarśasāratvāt pratyakṣābhāsameva | yaduktam-

yadi tvālocya sammīlya netre kaścidvikalpayet |
na syātpratyakṣatā tatra sambandhānanusārataḥ || iti || 40 ||

	syādetat pratyabhijñānyathānupapattyā katipayakālāvasthāyitvaṃ 
śabdasya bhaviṣyatītyāha-

kālaṃ na cāpi tāvantaṃ sthairyaṃ śabdasya sambhavet |
sthairye vā sarvanityatvaṃ pratyabhijñānadarśanāt || 41 ||

	evaṃ dhārājvālāderapi sarvasya sthairyakalpanāprasaṅga iti na 
kaścidanityaḥ syāt || 41 ||

	upasaṃhartumāha-

itthamakṣarajātasya nityatvaṃ nopapadyate |
padatadyogavākyānāṃ dūrotsāritameva tat || 42 ||

	evaṃ prayokturvya~jakatvāsambhavena kāraṇatvād 
varṇānāmanityatvasiddhestadārabdhānāṃ padānāṃ 
tatsaṃyogātmanaśca vākyavrātasyāpyanityatvaṃ siddhamiti vedasya 
prāmāṇyānyathānupapattyā īśvaraḥ kartābhyupagantavyo yo bauddhaiḥ 
sarvajñatvaduḥsādhatayā bhavadbhirna saṃśritaḥ na tu doṣadarśaneneti | 
yaduktam-

yadi tvādau jagatsṛṣṭvā dharmādharmau sasādhanau |
tathā śabdārthasambandhān vedān kaścitpravartayet ||
jagaddhitāya vedasya tathā kiñcinna duṣyati |
sarvajñavattu duḥsādhamiti taistanna saṃśritam || iti || 42 ||

	atra paraḥ

asthairye padayogānāṃ vijñātasamayaṃ padam |
naṣṭamanyadidānīṃ syādarthajñānaṃ tataḥ katham || 43 ||

padānāṃ tatsaṃyogātmanāṃ ca vākyānāmasthairye sati vijñātaḥ 
samayaḥ saṅketo yasmin pade tat padaṃ subantādi 
naṣṭamanyadevābhinavakaviviracitamutpannamiti tato'kṛtasaṅketāt 
tasmādarthajñānābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ || 43 ||

	uttaramāha-

sādṛśyānnanu nānyatvaṃ sārūpyeṇa nirvāyate |
kiṃ kurmaḥ śabdanityatve na yato mānamasti hi || 44 ||

	śabdānāṃ hi anityatve pramāṇasiddhe'rthānāmiva 
sāmānyanibandhanamarthapratyāyanādikāryamupapadyata eva | 
sādṛśyaṃ sāmānyamasmākaṃ sāṃkhyānāmivetyavirodhaḥ || 44 ||

	atra parābhiprāyaḥ-

athāsmādeva nityatvaṃ śabdānāṃ parikalpyate |

	arthapratyāyanānyathānupapattyā nityatvaṃ śabdānāmucyate |

	tadayuktamityāha-

syāttato'kṣinikocādestattulyatvānna nityatā || 45 ||

	anityānāmapi akṣinikocakaṣāṅkuśābhighātādīnāmartha-
pratyāyanadṛṣṭerasyānaikāntikatvāt || 45 ||

	evamapi neśvarakartṛkatvasiddhiratreti paraḥ-

śāstrojjhito na kālo'sti pralayo nopapadyate |
prabhātamapi naivātastena śāstramakṛtrimam || 46 ||

	brāhmaṇena ṣaḍaṅgo vedo'dhyeyaḥ 
garbhādhānādivaidikasaṃskārasaṃskṛto brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇājjāto 
brāhmaṇaḥ ityāryāgamāvailakṣaṇyenādhikārisaṃvalanayaiva vedasya 
vyavasthiterbījāṅkurādipravāhavadanityatve'pi anidaṃ prathamatvamiti | 
ata eva śāstraśāsyādyujjhitasya pralayābhidhānasya kālasya 
tannirmāṇādeścāharmukhasyāsambhavānneśvarakartṛko vedaḥ | 
taduktam-

pralaye'pi pramāṇaṃ vaḥ sarvocchedātmake nahi | iti || 46 ||

etat pratividhatte-

koṭiśo maraṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā jantūnāmavasīyate |
sambhavatyeva kālo'sau yatrāśeṣajanakṣayaḥ || 47 ||

	maṣakamakṣikādīnāmasaṃkhyātānāṃ jantūnāṃ yugapat 
sṛṣṭiḥ saṃhāraśca dṛṣṭa iti sarvaprāṇabhṛtāṃ jantūtvādeva 
kasmiṃścit kāle yugapat sṛṣṭyā saṃhāreṇa ca bhavitavyamiti 
sargapralayasiddheradoṣaḥ || 47 ||

	atra parābhiprāyaṃ kārikābhāgenāha-

karma tādṛgvidhānaṃ cet

	karmanibandhano hi śarīrādeḥ saṃhāro nāhetukaḥ, na ca 
mahāpralayanimittaṃ karma kiñcit śrūyata iti hetvabhāvādayuktaḥ | 
taduktam-

	na cāpyanupabhogo'sau kasyacitkarmaṇaḥ phalam | iti ||

	atrottaraṃ tadbhāgenaivāha-

					maraṇaṃ naiva karmaṇaḥ |
kintu tatkṣayataḥ

	śarīropasaṃhāro'pi upabhogābhāvātmakatvānna karmaphalamapi 
tu upabhogapradakarmakṣayākṣipta eva tadvat sarvopasaṃhāro'pītyadoṣaḥ 
| kathaṃ tarhi āyuṣkāmo hiraṇyadakṣiṇena yajet maraṇakāmaḥ 
sarvasvāreṇa yajet iti āyuṣyānāyuṣyakarmaśrutayaḥ 
bhogapradakarmāntarākṣepatadupasaṃhārārthā eva, na hi bhogaṃ vinā 
kevalamevāyurapapadyate bhogapadakarmasadbhāve vā maraṇaṃ yataḥ |

	nanu tathāpi mahāpralayānupapattiḥ karmāntarāṇāṃ 
bhogapradānāṃ bhāvādityāha-

		svāpo'pyanyasyāparipākataḥ || 48 ||

	karmāntarasyāparipakvatvāt pūrvasya copabhuktatvāt 
kiyatkālaparipākāpekṣākṣiptaḥ svāpaḥ suṣuptābhidhāno'yaṃ siddho 
na tu karmaphalamiti mahāpralayasyāpi svāpāt karmaparipākākṣiptaiva na 
karmaphalateti | yaduktam-

	na cāpyanupabhogo'sau kasyacitkarmaṇaḥ phalam | 

	iti tat siddhasādhanameva || 48 ||

	atha tadānīṃ kathaṃ karmaṇāmavipākaḥ | 
vipākahetvasāmarthyādityāha-

koṭiśo hi kṣayaṃ dṛṣṭvā karmaṇāmavasīyate |
samakālaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vipākaḥ sambhavatyapi || 49 ||

	asaṃkhyātānāṃ hi maṣakamakṣikādīnāṃ kādācitkaḥ sargaḥ 
pralayaśca dṛśyamānastatprakṛtīnāmatyantamalpatvena 
tāvanmātropabhogato'pacitaśaktitvāt sukumārataravanitāderiva 
karmāntarākṣiptabhogadānāsāmarthyaṃ viśramataśca 
taddānasāmarthyaṃ karmavipākahetutvenānumīyate, anyathā 
kālāntare'pi maṣakādīnāṃ pakṣiṇāmiva bhāvaḥ kena na syāt | nanu 
kālaviśeṣa eva teṣāṃ hetuḥ | kimataḥ so'pi 
karmavipākāvirbhāvanayaiva | tadvat prakṛtyantarāṇāṃ mahattve'pi 
anavaratānantapuruṣabhogasādhanatvenāpacitataraśaktitvāt 
karmānurūpabhogadānāsāmarthyataḥ karmavipākakaraṇatvābhāvo 
viśramataśca tatparipākasāmarthyamanumīyata iti mahāpralayasiddhiḥ | 
yaduktam-

na ca karmavatāṃ yuktā sthitistadbhogavarjitā |
sarveṣāṃ tu phalāpetaṃ na sthānamupapadyate |
karmaṇāṃ vāpyabhivyaktau kiṃ nimittaṃ tadā bhavet |

iti tat pratikṣiptameveti || 49 ||

	atra parābhiprāyaḥ-

aśeṣakarmanāśe ca punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ kutaḥ sthiti |

	evamapi mahāpralayaḥ karmavināśa eva paryavasyatīti punaḥ 
saṃsārābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | taduktam-

	aśeṣakarmanāśe vā punaḥ sṛṣṭirna yujyate | iti ||

etat pariharati-

dehāntaraphalaṃ karma kṣīyate naiva mṛtyunā || 50 ||

	nābhuktaṃ karma naśyati iti niyamānna mahāpralayo 
dehāntaropabhogyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ kṣayahetuḥ, api tu uktanayena 
tatparipākaprayuktatvāt tatparipākahetureveti naiṣa prasaṅga iti || 50 ||

	itaśca pralayasiddhirityāha-

kṣmādeḥ sākāratā proktā janmanastasya pūrvataḥ |
gamyate jagataḥ svāpastasmātprākca jagatkṣayaḥ || 51 ||

yatsākāraṃ kāryaṃ 
tadatadākāradupādānakāraṇādudbhavasvabhāvam, yathā vaṭādiḥ 
vaṭabījādeḥ | sākāraṃ ca jagat pṛthivyādikāryamatastasya yat janmataḥ 
pūrvaṃ tadavibhāgākārātmanā bhāvyamityapi pralayasiddhiḥ || 51 ||

	api ca-

bhaviṣyatpralayaścāpi kāryatvādeva gamyate |
kṛtsnanāśo'pi kāryasya dehādeḥ sampradṛśyate || 52 ||

	kṛtakatvādbhuvanādeḥ śarīrādivat sarvātmanā nāśena 
bhavitavyamiti bhaviṣyato'pi mahāpralayasya siddheḥ śāstrojjhitasyāpi 
kālasya sambhavādavaśyaṃ tatkartrā bhavitavyamitīśvarasiddhiḥ | kathaṃ 
tarhi adhikārisaṃvalanā | ata eva bījāṅkurapravāhavat 
tatpūrvikaivetyadoṣaḥ || 52 ||

	śāstrasyeśvarakartṛtvaniṣedhāya paroktaṃ hetvantaramāha-

dharmataḥ patyuraiśvaryaṃ dharmadhīḥ śāstratastataḥ |
śāstraṃ pūrvatra siddhaṃ cet

	yadyadaiśvaryaṃ tattat śāstrārthānuṣṭhānātmakāt dharmādeva 
yathā yoginām | aiśvaryaṃ ca jagatkarturabhyupagataṃ bhavadbhiriti, tadapi 
dharmādeveti dharmāvagamahetoḥ śāstrasyāpi 
pūrvasiddherneśvarakartṛkatā |

	atrottaraṃ kārikābhāgenāha-

				kalpe'tīte tadiṣyate || 53 ||

	yadyatra śāstrasya pūrvatvamātrameva sādhyate, tat 
siddhasādhanameva pratikalpaṃ sargavat śāstrasyāpi sattvāt | atheśvarāt 
pūrvatvaṃ sādhyate tato'siddho heturityāha-

kintvaiśvaryaṃ maheśasya dharmānnetyabhidhāsyate |

	yadi hi dharmasādhyatvaṃ tadaiśvaryasya siddhaṃ syāt, syādeva 
śāstrasyeśvarāt pūrvamapi siddhiḥ | na tu taddharmasādhyamapi tu 
anādītyagre vakṣyati |

	abhyugamyāpi pravāhanityeśvaravādyabhiprāyeṇāha-

astu vā dharmatastasmādanityaṃ nityatāṃ vrajet || 54 ||

ato'nyadeva tacchāstraṃ jātā taddharmadhīryataḥ |
jagaddhitāyā tenānyadidānīṃ tacitaṃ matam || 55 ||

pravāhanityeśvaravādināṃ hi īśvaraḥ sarvagat śāstraṃ nirmāya 
tāvadeva jagatkṛtaṃ vidhatte yāvat 
tadvihitaśāstrādarthānuṣṭhāneneśvaro hyanyaḥ siddhaḥ | tasmiṃstu 
āgate niyogivat svavyāpāraṃ sarva śāstraṃ copasaṃhṛtya svātmārāma 
eva bhavati | tataḥ siddheśvaro'sadāsthayaivānena 
jagatkṛtyamupasaṃhṛtamityavagatya niśākālaṃ pratīkṣya sarvagat 
śāstraṃ nirmāya jagatkṛtyaṃ tāvad racayati 
yāvatpunastadvihitaśāstrādanuṣṭhānakrameṇeśvaro'nyaḥ siddha 
ityevamanyo'nyaśceti sargasaṃhāravat śāstramanityamapi pravāhato 
nityatvādīśvarakartṛkameva || 55 ||

	punarīśvarakartṛkatvaṃ śāstrasyāmṛṣyamāṇaḥ paraḥ-

śāstrādartheṣu vijñānamato no kṛtakaṃ hi tat | 
kṛtakādvacaso yasmādvivakṣā sampratīyate || 56 ||

	yadāha śāvarabhāṣyakāraḥ-

	laukikāddhi vākyādevamayaṃ puruṣo veda iti bhavati pratyayo 
vaidikāttvevamartha iti | (1-1-2)

	ato vivakṣātiraskāreṇa yataḥ śāstrādartha eva pratyayaḥ tato 
laukikavanno kṛtamiti | no niṣedhe || 56 ||

	etadapi pratikṣipati-

naitasmādakṛtatvaṃ syātpratyakṣāderasambhavāt |

	akṛtatvaṃ hi śabdasyoktanayena pratyakṣanirākṛtamato nānumānena 
sādhayituṃ śakyam |

	yadapi uktaṃ laukikādvacaso vivakṣā pratīyate iti 
tadapyasiddhamityāha-

vākyācca sarvataḥ pūrvamarthe dhīraviśeṣataḥ || 57 ||

	vaidikādiva laukikādapi vākyāduccāritamātrādeva 
viditapadapadārthasambandhasya prathamamartha evaṃ pratipattiḥ bhavati, 
tatraiva autpattikena sāmayikena vā sambandhena tasyābhidhāśaktisiddheḥ, 
na hi abhidhāyakatve tayorviśeṣaḥ yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi |

	ya eva laukikāḥ śabdāsta eva vaidikāsta eva ca teṣāmarthāḥ (1-1-30 
śā0 bhā0) iti | paścāt tu mukhyārthāsambhavena gauṇo lākṣaṇiko veti, 
tadidamuktaṃ bhavadbhirapi |

paurvāparyaparāmṛṣṭaḥ śabdo'nyāṃ kurute matim | iti || 57 ||

	yasya tu gauṇalākṣaṇikayorapi vyabhicāraḥ sa idānīṃ 
vakturviśiṣṭaṃ jñānaṃ gamayatītyāha-

vyabhicāro na yasya syādanyārthaṃ tatkathaṃ kṛtam | 

	kṛtaṃ laukikavākyam aṅgulyagre hastiśatayūthamāste 
ityādivākyavat yadeva sarvātmanā svārthe pratyakṣādibhirbādhyate tadeva 
tato'nyamarthaṃ vakturvañcanābhiprāyaṃ bhrāntijñānayogaṃ vā 
gamayati, yaduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

	pramāṇāntaramatra bādhyate na punarhastiśatayūtham | iti, 
yadyunmattādinā abhyāsakāmena anyārthapratipādanābhiprāyeṇa vā na 
prayuktaṃ bhavet | taiḥ prayukte tu na tamapi api tu 
vivakṣāmātrakamevetyuktam | yasya tu pratyakṣādibhirvyabhicāro nāsti 
tadvākyaṃ na vakturabhiprāyaṃ nāpi vivakṣāmātram, api tvarthameva 
pratipādayatīti | 
	
	atra kārikābhāgena paraḥ

jātatvāt saṃśayastantre

	yadyevamutpannatvenārthapratipādakatvena cāviśeṣāt laukikavākya 
iva śāstre'pi arthaviṣayaḥ saṃśayaḥ syāt, na ca bhavati 
tasmādanutpannameva taditi |

	bhāgāntareṇaitat pratikṣipati-

				ajātaṃ tarhi kiṃ bhavet || 58 ||

	yadi racanātmakamapi tantraṃ nānityam, ghaṭādyapi tathaiva syāt 
tataśca sarvasya nityatāprasaṅga ityuktam | kathaṃ tarhi na tataḥ saṃśayaḥ | 
saṃśayahetoḥ pratārakatvasyābhāvāt tadabhāvaścāptaprayuktatvāt 
pitrādivākyeṣviveti vakṣyāmaḥ || 58 ||

	ye tvāhurneha vyākaraṇe vastuto'rthaḥ śabdasya api tu vivakṣita iti 
tān pratyāha-

padārthānvayahīnaśca vākyārthaḥ syādalaukikaḥ |
na padaṃ vidyate yasmādvivakṣāpratipādakam || 59 ||

	na hi vivakṣā vākyasyārthaḥ padārthasya vākyārthānupapatteḥ 
padānyeva sambaddhāni vākyaṃ yataḥ, na ca padasyārtho vivakṣā api tu 
bāhya eveti vākyasyāpi sa eva yukta iti nāto vedasyākṛtakatvasiddhiḥ || 59 ||

punarīśvarakartṛkatāniṣedhe vedasya paro hetvantaramāha-

parokṣo nanu śāstrārtho viśeṣatvādalaiṅgikaḥ |
liṅgisāmānyagamakaṃ tadvyāptaṃ liṅgamiṣyate || 60 ||

aviditvā ca tatkaścit kathaṃ śāstraṃ kariṣyati |

	codanālakṣaṇo'rtho dharmaḥ sa ca kāryātmako na siddhasvabhāva 
iti na pratyakṣanimittaḥ | yadāha sūtrakāro jaiminiḥ |

	satsamprayoge puruṣasyendriyāṇāṃ buddhijanma tat 
pratyakṣamanimittaṃ vidyamānopalambhanatvāt iti | 
pratyakṣapūrvakatvāccānumānādivat samprati nimittatvam iti 
bhāṣyakāraśca, viśeṣitatvāccānanumeyaḥ dharmo hi jyotiṣṭomādireka 
eva sakalādhyāyibhistrayīto hotrudgātradhvaryukarmabhedena pratīyate, na hi 
pratyadhyāyinamanuṣṭhātāraṃ vā trayyāṃ tadarthasya vā 
jyotiṣṭomāderbhedaḥ śākhābhede'pi tatsvarūpābhedāt | yaduktam 
sarvaśākhāpratyeyamekaṃ karma |
ityekatvena viśeṣitvānna sādhyadharmasāmānyena sapakṣe siddhavyāptiḥ 
ata eva sādhyadharmasāmānyānumātuḥ liṅgasyāviṣaya 
itīśvarābhyupagame'pi tasya codanāvyatirekeṇa 
dharmāvedakapramāṇāsambandhādaviditasya copadeṣṭumaśakyatvānna 
codanākartṛtvasiddhiḥ |

	etat parākaroti |

prāmāṇye tasya saṃsiddhe vaktuṃ yuktamidaṃ budhāḥ || 61 ||

siddhameva yataḥ kuryādakṣādyaviṣaye matim |

	heyopādeyatadupāyaparicchedakatvaṃ hi pramāṇatvam | yadāhuḥ 
heyopādeyatattvasya sādhyupāyasya vedakaḥ |

	yaḥ pramāṇasamāviṣṭaḥ | iti |

	tasminnīśvaraprāmāṇye siddhe naitat vaktuṃ yuktamiti kākvā 
darśayati | tacca prāmāṇyaṃ tasya vakṣyamāṇasarvajñatvasiddhyaiva 
siddhamiti codanāvat tajjñānamapi akṣādyatītaṃ bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyat 
sūkṣmaṃ vyavahitaṃ viprakṛṣṭamevaṃjātīyakaṃ 
sarvamarthavagamayituṃ

samarthamiti kathaṃ na dharmaviṣayamapi syāt | nanu kāryatvāt dharmasya 
tadviṣayatvaṃ na sambhavatītyuktam | na phalādinānaikāntikatvāt 
phalakarmaṇyatirekeṇa ca kāryāntarasyāsiddheḥ | nanu niyogātmakameva 
tat | kimatastasyāpi preṣaṇādhyeṣaṇādirūpatvena 
svāmyādyāśayaviśeṣātmakatvataḥ pramāṇāntaragocaratvāt | tato'nya 
evāsau apauruṣeyaviṣayaḥ pramāṇāntarāgocara iti cet, na | tathābhūte 
sambandhagrahaṇābhāvena śabdādapratīterna hi alaukikaḥ śabdārthaḥ 
sambhavatītyuktam | na preṣaṇādyupādhidvāreṇa astyevātra vyutpattiriti 
cet, na, tarhyalaukiko'sau upādhidvāreṇa teṣāṃ 
pramāṇāntaraviṣayatvāditi, ata eva niyogasya niyoktṛvyāpārātmakatvāt 
laukikavākyavat vede'pi niyoktrā bhavitavyamitīśvarakartṛkatvasiddhiḥ | 
etadevāha-

na ca bādhāsti yenāsya tatprāmāṇyanivāraṇam || 62 ||

	na ca bādhakapramāṇamasti yeneśvarasya tasmin dharme 
pramāṇyanivāraṇamavedakatvaṃ siddhyet |

	nanu sādhakamapi pramāṇaṃ nāstītyāha-

itthaṃrūpaṃ tatastarhi sādhukāraṇajaṃ hi tat |

	tata eva tarhi bādhakapramāṇābhāvāt sarvajñasyetthaṃ rūpaṃ 
dharmāvedakamapi tat jñānaṃ siddhyati tasmācca sādhukāraṇajaṃ taditi 
||

	tadeva sādhukāraṇajatvaṃ tajjñānasyāha-

trikālārthāvisaṃvādi jñānaṃ ceśe'bhidhāsyate || 63 ||

	na hi trikālāmalavedinaḥ kāraṇadoṣo jñānānutpāde sambhavati | 
tarhi kālatrayānavacchedapratīterniyogasya tajjñeyatvāsiddhiḥ | satyam, yadi 
pratīyate, sa tu agnihotraṃ juhuyāt svargakāmaḥ ityādibhiḥ vākyaiḥ 
bhūtartamānatātiraskāreṇa bhaviṣyatsāmānya eva pratīyate | yadāha 
śāvarabhāṣyakāraḥ bhaviṣyaścaiṣo'rtho na jñānakāle'sti ityavirodhaḥ 
|| 63 ||

	ata evedapi siddhyatītyāha-

na cāpyasarvavitkaścidalpāyuśca karoti tat |
parasparāviruddhatvamahadavyāhatatvataḥ || 64 ||

vedasya kartari anumānataḥ siddhe parasparāviruddhārthatvena tasya pratītiḥ 
śākhāniruktaprātiśākhyādyaṅgabhedena atimahattvācca 
siddhārthapratipādakānāṃ tadekadeśānāṃ 
pratyakṣādivirodhasambhave'pyarthavādādināpyupapattitaḥ 
pramāṇāntaravyāghātāsambhavācca tatkartari api 
tathābhūtajñānādyatiśayasiddheḥ sarvajñatvānāditvādisiddhiḥ || 64 ||

	nanu bahavaḥ kathañcidaṃśāṃśikayā tathābhūtamapi vedaṃ 
kariṣyantītyāha-

bahūnāṃ karaṇe na syātproktalakṣaṇasaṅgatiḥ |

	tadānīṃ hi proktānāṃ mantrabrāhmaṇānāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca 
śākhānāmaṅgapratyaṅgādilakṣaṇānāṃ cānvayo na bhavet, 
bhinnakartṛkatvāt bauddhārhataśāstrāṇāmiva ||

	atha bahubhyaḥ samupajīvya eka eva tatra kartā bhaviṣyatītyucyate | 
tatrāpyāha-

ekasya śaktikḷptau syāt pradveṣaḥ kevalo hare || 65 ||

	evaṃ hi pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ vedaṃ vidadhānaḥ sa 
eveśvaraḥ siddha ityavivādaḥ || 65 ||

	etadeva draḍhayutumāha-

mahato bhūtapūrvasya liṅgamīśvaranirmite |
caritārthaṃ bhavettasmādīśa evātra yujyate || 66 ||

	mahato bahuprakārasya vede kartṛsamūhasya bhūtapūrvasya siddhau 
yalliṅgaṃ tadīśvaranirmitatve hi ekasmin caritārthamiti, na bahūnāṃ 
kalpanaṃ yujyate | yadāhuḥ-

kalpitaḥ kalpanaṃ hanti tata evārthasiddhitaḥ | iti || 66 ||

	itaśca sādhukaraṇajaṃ tajjñānamityāha-

na ca pratārakaḥ śarvo yasmātsarvajagatpatiḥ |
pratārya prāṇinastebhyaḥ kimapūrvamavāpsyati || 67 ||

	pratārakā hi rathyāpuruṣāḥ kena kenacit svaprayojanena pratārayanti 
| tasya tu parārthavṛtteḥ svaprayojanābhāvāt kutaḥ pratārakatā || 67 ||

kiñca-

parārthaṃ mantravādāśca yena sarvavidā kṛtāḥ |
sarvaśreyoyutāḥ sarve sa kathaṃ hi pratārayet || 68 ||

	paropakārāyaiva yena mantravādā rasarasāyanapātālaguṭikāḥ 
sarvasya kāmyamānasya śreyasaḥ sādhakā bahavo viracitāstasya 
pratārakatvaṃ na sambhavatyeva || 68 ||

	na cāpi puruṣāṇāmatra kartṛtvamityāha-

parokṣo mantravādārtho viśeṣatvādalaiṅgikaḥ |
setikartavyatāko'pi jyotiṣṭomādivannṛṇām || 69 ||

	puruṣāṇāṃ hi pratyakṣasiddhe 
harītakyādervirekādāvivānumānasiddhe vā cakṣurādeḥ 
paṭalādinivṛttyādāviva śāstṛtvaṃ syāt, na tu indriyātīte proktanayena 
viśeṣatvāccānanumeye jyotiṣṭomādeḥ svargādāviva | 
mantravādaśāstrasya cārtho'ṅgavrātenetikartavyatayā 
dhyānadhāraṇādikayā yuktaḥ phalasādhanatvena na pratyakṣasiddho 
viśeṣatvāccānanumeya iti na śāstṛtvaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ sambhavati-
itīśvarasya yuktam || 69 ||

	na ca vedāṅgānāmiva vedakartṛsiddhyaiva 
kartṛsiddhirmantravādānāmityāha-

prasiddhyasambhavāccāpi na vede garuḍādayaḥ |
kartāraḥ sarvaviccaiṣāṃ gamyate'nugrahaikadhīḥ || 70 ||

	prasiddhyā hi anādiprarūḍhyā trayyāmeva vedatā 
śākhāviśeṣāṇāṃ ca vedaviśeṣaśākhātvaṃ na pramāṇāntareṇa | na 
ca vedaviṣayā mantravādānāṃ prasiddhiriti bhinnaśāstratvena pṛthak 
teṣāṃ kartṛviśeṣasiddhau prayatnaḥ kṛtaḥ || 70 ||

	atra paro'sādhukaraṇajamīśvarasya jñānaṃ sādhayitumāha-

sarvajñānakriyāhīno vaktṛtvāttarhi śaṅkaraḥ |

	vaktṛtvādasmadādivad īśvaraḥ sarvajñānādirahitaḥ ||

	tadayuktamasiddhatvādityāha-

vaktṛtvaṃ śāstrakartṛtvaṃ naitasmāt proktadūṣaṇam || 71 ||

	nāsmadādivat vacanamātrakartṛtvaṃ vaktṛtvamatra siddham, api tu 
anyadeva tat śāstraprakartṛtvam, na ca tasyāsarvajñatvena vyāptiḥ siddhā || 
71 ||

viruddhaścāyaṃ heturityāha-

asmācca pratyuteśānaḥ sarvajñaḥ sampratīyate |
śāstraṃ hi kurvatānena sarvameva kṛtaṃ bhavet || 72 ||

phalajātaṃ yataḥ puṃsāṃ sarvaṃ śāstrātprasidhyati |

tadidamuktam |

na cāpyasarvavit kaścidalpāyuśca karoti tat |

iti || 72 ||

	ata evāha-

codyaṃ cedṛkkathaṃ kuryātprāguktākhilamānavit || 73 ||

	ajñasyaivaitat codyaṃ na tu pūrvoktapramāṇasaṃskṛtasyeti tadevaṃ 
sādhukaraṇajanitatvādīśvarajñānaṃ dharmādiviṣayamapi sambhavatīti 
śāstrasya pramāṇāntarāgocarārthatvāt, na īśvarakartṛkatvāsiddhiḥ || 73 
||

	atra svayūthyaḥ

svamatasyaiva mānatve yatnaṃ sarvaṃ karoti hi |
vedasyāpi pramāṇatve kiṃ mudhaiva kṛtaḥ śramaḥ || 74 ||

	sarveṇa hi prekṣāvatā 
svadarśanasyaivāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāpratikṣepāya yatno vidheyaḥ na ca 
darśanāntarasya niṣprayojanatvāt, aviśeṣaḥ 
sarvāgamaprāmāṇyasamarthanenānavasthāprasaṅgāccetīśvarakartṛtvena 
vedaprāmāṇyasamarthanametat bhavato'nupapannameva || 74 ||

	tadayuktam, tasyaivehāpekṣaṇīyatvānnāgamāntarāṇāmityāha-

vedaṃ vinā na karmāsti kalādi ca na tadvinā |
chettavyaṃ dīkṣayā sarvaṃ karmādīti prabhāṣitam || 75 ||

anujñātāśca vedoktā śaive varṇāśramādayaḥ |
vedasyāpi pramāṇatve yatno'smābhirataḥ kṛtaḥ || 76 ||

	yato'nekajanmasambhṛtaṃ dharmādharmātmakaṃ karma sarvasya 
kalādikṣityantasya tattvabhāvabhūtabhuvanātmanaḥ saṃsārasya 
hetubhūtaṃ dīkṣayā cchedyamihoktam | na cājñātasya 
cchedyatvamupapadyate na ca vedaṃ vinā tasyāvikalasya jñānaṃ 
sambhavati bauddhoktasya caityaśayanāderapi ārhatoktasya 
keśaluñcanāderdharmasya kathañcit tadekadeśatvāt, yaduktam |

sarvaḥ kleśo hi pāpasya kṣapaṇe paryavasyati iti | mlecchakalpitasya tu 
paraprāṇaharaṇāderdharmasyāpi adharmatayā tena vyāptatvāt | yataśceha 
vedoktā varṇāśramasthitiḥ pālyatvena śrūyate |

iti varṇāśramācārānmanasāpi na laṅghayet |
yo yasminnāśrame tiṣṭheddīkṣitaḥ śivaśāsane ||

	sa tasminneva santiṣṭhecchivadharmaṃ ca pālayet | iti 
sarvakarmavicchedopadeśānyathānupapattyā 
tattadvarṇāśramavyavasthāpanaśruteśca vedasyāsmābhiḥ pramāṇye 
yatno vihitaḥ nāgamāntarāṇāmiti || 76 ||

	nanu tasya pramāṇatvena siddhestathāpyayaṃ yatno niṣphala ityāha-

vedo mumukṣubhiścānyaiḥ pratyākhyāto yato bhavet |

	yato bauddhārhatādibhirvedasya prāmāṇyaṃ pratyākhyātameva 
tato'yaṃ yatnaḥ saphala eva |

	na ca tat pratyākhyātuṃ śakyamityāha-

vedaṃ vinā na jantūnāṃ kuto mokṣo bhavaṃ vinā || 77 ||

	na hi heyaṃ tatsādhanaṃ cāparijñāya mokṣa upapadyate | na ca 
vedaṃ vinā tasya sārvarūpyeṇa jñānaṃ sambhavatītyuktam | ato 
mokṣopadeśānyathānupapatyā tairapi vedaḥ 
pramāṇatvenābhyupagantavya eveti || 77 ||

	yadyevaṃ, śāntiṃ kurvato vetālotthāpanametaditi paraḥ | 

vede mokṣasya siddhatvātkiṃ śaivena prayojanam |

	evaṃ hi varṇāśramavyavasthāderiva mokṣasyāpi 
vedādevāvagateranuvādakatvenāprāmāṇyameva śāstrasyeti ||

	atha parasparānapekṣayaikārthapratipādakatve'pi śākhādvayasyeva 
vikalpataḥ prāmāṇyamanayorbhaviṣyatītyāha-

tatsthasyehādhikāritvādvikalpo'tra na yujyate || 78 ||

	yuktaṃ śākhādvayasya vikalpataḥ prāmāṇyaṃ samaśiṣṭatvāt | 
iha tūtpattiśiṣṭavedasaṃvidvyavasthitasya brāhmaṇāderadhikārāt vedata 
evāvagatiḥ prathamamavaśyaṃbhāvinī tasya- iti viṣamaśiṣṭatvād 
vikalpānupapatteraprāmāṇyameva || 78 ||

atha jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajet darśapaurṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo 
yajeta iti vākyorekasvargaphalatve'pi prayogabhūyastvāt 
phalabhūyastvenaivātraitayorāgamayorekaphalatve'pi 
prayogabhūyastvātphalabhūyastvena prāmāṇyaṃ bhaviṣyatītyāha-

	matadvayoktakaraṇaṃ phalabhūyastvato na tat |

	naitat yuktaṃ svargādeḥ phalasyeva mokṣasyātiśayāsambhavāt ||

	itaścāsyāprāmāṇyamityāha-

niryuktikaśca gaditaḥ kriyayā karmasaṃkṣayaḥ || 79 ||

	jñānādevājñānanivṛttidvāreṇa mokṣasya siddherdīkṣātmanaḥ 
karmaṇo mokṣahetutvapratipādakametat śāstram agninā siñcet iti 
śrutivadapramāṇameva | taduktam jñānāgniḥ sarvakarmāṇi 
bhasmasātkurute iti | mīmāṃsakasya cātra pūrvapakṣavāditvāt 
karmasaṃkṣaya eva mokṣa ityuktam || 79 ||

	iyat paravacanaṃ pratikṣeptumāha-

sarvamuktyuttamā gītā śivatā śivaśāsane |
phalebhyaścāpi sarvebhyo vijñeyā sādhikādhikā || 80 ||

	bhavedeṣa doṣo yadi puruṣatattvāvadhirevātrāpi mokṣo bhavet, atra tu 
śivatattvāvāptiḥ darśanāntarānadhigato'pūrva eva 
sarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvātmakaḥ śrūyata iti 
bauddhavaiśeṣikārhatasāṃkhyapāñcarātravedāntādyuktebhyaḥ 
krameṇa sātiśayebhyo mokṣebhyo'tiśayena sātiśayo jñeya iti kutaḥ 
pūrvokto doṣaḥ | yacchrūyate |

buddhitattve sthitā bauddhā guṇeṣu tvārhatāḥ sthitāḥ |
guṇamūrdhni sthitāḥ sāṃkhyā avyakte pāñcarātrikāḥ ||

	sthitā vedavidaḥ puṃsi | ityādi || 80 ||

etadevāha-

anuṣṭhānātirekācca phalādhikyaṃ vipaścitaḥ |
anuktamapi kalpyaṃ tatrāprāmāṇyamasambhavāt || 81 ||

	anuṣṭhānahetutvādanuṣṭhānaṃ jñānamucyate tasyāsya 
sarvānyāgamaprāpyasthānāntarapradarśanena proktakramataḥ pravṛtteḥ 
sarvāgamebhyaḥ sarvaviparyayatvenātiśayasiddhito

siddhito he paṇḍitāḥ sarvotkṛṣṭamanuktamapi mokṣātmakaṃ phalaṃ 
kalpanīyaṃ kimuktaṃ na sambhāvyata iti nātrāprāmāṇyadoṣaḥ 
pūrvoktaḥ || 81 ||

	nāpi dvitīya ityāha-

dīkṣayaivāpyate sarvaṃ naiṣṭhike'pyuditaṃ kvacit |
vedādyuktamatastatra naiṣphalyādvinivartate || 82 ||

	yuṣmadāgame'pi vedādivihitasya karmaṇaḥ kriyayā kṣaya 
upaniṣatsu śrūyate | 

yadottamādhikāre'smin dīkṣito vedavid bhavet |
kṣīyate'sya sadā karma sarvaṃ sukṛtaduṣkṛtam ||

iti || 82 ||

	na kevalaṃ jñānakāṇḍa eva yāvat 
karmakāṇḍe'pyetadvihitamityāha-

śubhayā kriyayā vede kṣayaḥ pāpasya coditaḥ |

	cāndrāyaṇādinā prāyaścittakarmaṇā duṣkṛtakṣayaśruterna 
niryuktikaḥ kriyayā karmasaṃkṣayo 
bhavatāmivāgamasiddhatvādityadoṣaḥ |

	nanu yuktiratrānumānamabhimatā cāndrāyaṇādīnāṃ divyena 
pratyayena ghaṭādinā śuddhihetutvaniścayāt | yaduktam-

adharmāttamaso dehe gurutvaṃ yatkṣayo'sya tu |
dharmato laghutotpādādghaṭataḥ sampratīyate || iti |

	atrāpyāha-

dṛṣṭaśca tatkṣayaḥ śaive kriyayaiva tulādinā || 83 ||

	ihāpi ghaṭadīkṣāyāmevaṃvidhā yuktirastyeveti na niryuktikaḥ 
kriyayā karmasaṃkṣayo gadita iti || 83 ||

	api ca-

dīkṣāto yadi neṣyeta karmābhāvaḥ kuto'nyataḥ |

	karmavādibhirhi kārye'rthe vedasya prāmāṇyābhyupagamāt 
jñānādipratipādakasyopaniṣadbhāgasya 
siddhārthatvenāpramāṇyaprasaṅgataḥ stutyarthatvenārthavādatayā 
karmakāṇḍānvayataḥ prāmāṇyasamarthanāt 
jñānayogātmano'nyasyāsiddheḥ kriyāviśeṣāt yadi kutaścit neṣyate, 
tadā vedasya mokṣaśāstratvābhāvaprasaṅgato bauddhādīnāmatra 
pramāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ iti mokṣaśāstratvānyathānupapattyāvaśyaṃ 
kutaścit

kriyāviśeṣāt karmābhāvo'bhyupagantavyaḥ yataścābhyupagamyate saiva 
dīkṣeti na niryuktikaḥ kriyayā karmasaṃkṣayaḥ |

	atra paro'nyathā karmābhāvamāha-

akṛteruttareṣāṃ syātpūrveṣāmupabhogataḥ || 84 ||

	tadidamuktam-

nityanaimittike kuryātpratyavāyajihāsayā |
mokṣārthī na pravarteta tatra kāmyaniṣiddhayoḥ ||

iti || 84 ||

	etat pratikṣipati

anekajanmasambhogyānyekasminneva janmani |
karmāṇi kurute tasmādrabhasād bhaṇitaṃ tvidam || 85 ||

	bhavedetat yadi pratijanma ekajanmabhogyānyeva puruṣaḥ karmāṇi 
kuryāt, anekabhavabhogyāni tu jyotiṣṭomādīni nānāphalāni 
brahmahatyādīni kurvan dṛśyata iti pūrveṣāmupabhogataḥ kṣayo'siddhaḥ, 
na hi narakasvargādiphalayorekajanmanyevāpabhogaḥ sambhavatīti 
pramāṇasvarūpāparyālocane naitat kathitaṃ bhavadbhiḥ || 85 ||

tataśca yadi nāmeha na kṛtaṃ karma janmani | 
janmāntaraṃ tathāpi syātpūrvasmādeva karmaṇaḥ || 86 ||

	pūrveṣāmupabhogata sarveṣāṃ kṣayasyāsiddherasmin janmani 
pratyavāyaphalahetorakaraṇe'pi pūrvatarakarmabhyo janmāntarāṇi 
santyeveti nityanaimittike kuryāt ityādyukto 
mokṣasādhananyāyo'naikāntikatvādayukta eva syāt || 86 ||

	evamanekabhavabhogyakarmasambhave yadeva tīvravegaṃ tadeva 
mandavegānyakarmopabhogopamardena svakāryamabhinirvartayat ekasyaiva 
janmano hetuḥ sampadyata iti karmāntarāṇāṃ 
tadupamardādevābhāvābhāvasiddhernānaikāntikatāsya hetorityāha-

anekabhavabhogyāni karmāṇi kurute pumān |
tatrāpyekasya sambhogātra nāśaḥ sarvakarmaṇām || 87 ||

tatra teṣu anekabhavabhogyeṣu karmasu madhyādekasya bhogāddhetorna 
sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ kṣayastadvadevābhuktatvād yat śrūyate |

	nābhuktaṃ karma naśyati | iti |

	api tu tuhinapātādyuparuddhabījavat kālāntareṇa phaladātṛtayā 
vāsanātmatayā tāvat tiṣṭhantīti atīvravegānāṃ karmaṇāṃ 
nāśāsiddhestebhyo janmāntarāṇi santyeveti naikasmin janmani 
pratyavāyaphalahetorakaraṇamātreṇa mokṣa iti | atha 
pratijanmaviṣayametadityucyate, tadayuktaṃ 
mlecchādijanmaviṣayatvenānadhikārato'sambhavāt tannityānuṣṭhānasya 
ca prāṇivadhāderniṣedhyatvena pratyuta janmāntarahetutvato viruddhatvāt || 
87 ||

	nanu yāvanna sarvakarmakṣayo na tāvanmanuṣyajanma, yaduktam-

ādyo hi dehaḥ puruṣārthamūla-
stato'pyanye karmamūlāḥ prapannāḥ |

iti manuṣyajanmānyathānupapattyānekabhavabhogyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ 
karmaṇāṃ kṣayasiddherbhavatyevāsmiñjanmani 
pratyavāyaphalahetorakaraṇān mokṣa iti | atrāpyāha-

antye janmani no sarva karma sambhujyate yataḥ |
sukhaduḥkhādibhedo'yaṃ janmanyatrāpi dṛśyate || 88 ||

	yadyevam, manuṣyajanmano'karmaphalatvād 
vaicitryābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | karmanibandhanaṃ hi sukhādivaicitryaṃ 
puṃsāmityuktam |

iha te karmaṇāmeva vipākaścintayiṣyati |
amutra bhavitā yatte taccintaya śubhāśubham ||

iti | tataśca-

	ādyo hi dehaḥ puruṣārthamūlaḥ |

ityasya bādhitatvāt agninā siñcet itivadaprāmāṇyato'pi uktametat syāt || 
88 ||

	etajjanmaprayāṇamadhyakṛtaṃ sarvameva śubhāśubhaṃ karma 
pradhānopasarjanabhāvena sammūrchitaṃ yugapadekameva janma 
kariṣyatītyevamanekabhavabhogyānāṃ karmaṇāmasambhavakalpanayā na 
proktadoṣo'tra bhaviṣyatītyāha �

indratvādiphalaṃ vede ucyate tatpṛthak pṛthak |
ājanmajanitebhyo'pi naikaṃ janma bhavatyataḥ || 89 ||

vājapeyena svārājyakāmo yajet iti svārājyamindratvameva tatphalaṃ 
karma ādigrahaṇāt svargādiphalaṃ jyotiṣṭomādi brahmahatyādi ca 
narakaphalaṃ gṛhyate | tat pṛthak pṛthak janma karotītyadhyāhāraḥ, 
parasparānapekṣayaiva śruterviruddhaphalatvācca | tato na sarveṣāṃ 
pradhānopasarjanabhāvena ekajamakaraṇakalpanopapadyate śrutivirodhāditi 
|| 89 ||

upasaṃhartumāha �

iṣṭaṃ ca tadanekebhyo yeṣāṃ tatkarma te bhave |

	ato'nekabhavahetukamanekabhavabhogyaṃ vā karmaiṣṭavyaṃ tat 
yeṣāmasti te'smin janmani pratyavāyahetorakaraṇe'pi saṃsāre vartanta iti 
nityanaimittike kuryāt ityanenāpi prakāraṇena vedasya 
mokṣaśāstratvānupapattestannirvāhāyāvaśyaṃ kriyāviśeṣaḥ kaścit 
karmakṣapaṇāya bhavadbhirabhyupagantavya iti sūktam �

dīkṣito yadi neṣyeta karmābhāvaḥ kuto'nyat | iti ||

	kecittu nābhuktaṃ karma naśyati iti śruterjñānino yogabalāt 
sarvakarmabhoga ityevaṃ jñānakarmasamuccayāt mokṣaṃ pratipannā na 
dīkṣayeti tanmatamupanyasya dūṣayati |

jñānī yogabalāt bhuṅkte sarvaṃ karmeti nārthavat || 90 ||

	kuta ityāha-

ajñānādervinā no yaddṛṣṭo bhogaḥ sukhādiṣu |

	vairāgyād viṣayavaitṛṣṇyātmakaṃ jñānaṃ rāgānubandhācca 
bhogaḥ karmata iti jñānakarmaṇorviruddhānubandhitvāt 
samyaṅmithyājñānayoriva samuccayāsambhavāt, yat avaśyaṃ 
nābhuktaṃ karma naśyati iti śrutibalāt jñānino'pi karma bhogenaiva 
kṣapaṇīyaṃ tadanyathānupapatterdīkṣayaiva kṣapaṇīyamiti niryuktikaśca 
gaditaḥ kriyayā karmasaṃkṣayaḥ iti nirastam | nanu tatrāpi bhogasya 
sambhavāt sa eva tadavastho doṣaḥ | na, 
cauryaprayuktamantrasāmarthyopahṛtamohādināmapi 
svapnabhogavattadānīṃ jñānino'pi upapatteradoṣaḥ |

	evaṃ karmakṣayānmuktimabhyupagamyoktamidānīṃ tu saiva 
nopapadyata ityāha-

kṣīṇe'pi karmajāte syānmalādernaiva saṃkṣayaḥ || 91 ||

sādhanaṃ pāśavicchityai śivoktaṃ parigṛhyatām |

astu
	vijñānayogasaṃnyāsairbhogādvā karmaṇaḥ kṣayaḥ || iti |

kṣīṇe'pi tasmin malasya ādigrahaṇādīśvarapreryatvasya ca naiva kṣaya iti 
vijñānakevalitvameva teṣāṃ na mokṣaḥ | yathā cāvidyāsmṛtyādeḥ 
prākṛtādvandhāt karmaṇaśca malo'nyo'bhyupagantavyaḥ 
tathoktamācāryeṇa svāyambhūvavṛttau yadyaśuddhirna puṃso'sti 
ityatrāntare, vyākhyātaṃ ca taṭṭīppake'smatpitrā tatra bhavatā 
bhaṭṭanārāyaṇakaṇṭheneti tata evāvadhāryam | tasya ca dravyatvānna 
jñānānnivṛttiḥ, api tu cakṣuṣpaṭalāderiva viśiṣṭayā kriyayeti 
vakṣyāmaḥ | ato-

niryuktikaśca gaditaḥ kriyayā karmasaṃkṣayaḥ |

ityasyāpi sadoṣatvānna pramāṇāntareṇa bādhitatvādapramāṇasya 
śāstrasyeti vaktuṃ yuktamiti ||

	adhunā na kevalamanumānataḥ 
svāgamācceśvarasiddhiryāvadyuṣmadāgamādapītyāha-

nityādanityato vāpi śāstrataścādhigamyate || 92 ||

	īśvarakartṛkatvena vedasya prāmāṇyaṃ pratipāditamastu yadi vā 
parābhyupagatenākṛtakatvena sarvatheśvarasiddhau 
nāprāmāṇyamityanavakḷptyā darśayati | tathā ca raudraṃ caruṃ 
nirvapretprajākāmaḥ sauryaṃ caruṃ nirvaped brahmavarcasakāmaḥ 
ityādau rudro devatā'syeti devatātaddhitaśrutyā rudrādidevatātvena 
codyate | nanu kārye'rthe vedasya prāmāṇyam, na siddhe devatādau | 
kathaṃ tarhi dadhnā juhoti, payasā juhoti ityādayo guṇavidhayaḥ tatra hi 
agnihotraṃ juhuyāt svargakāmaḥ ityato bhāvārthāprāptatvenānuvādato 
vidhānāsambhavāt dadhyādayo'rthāḥ siddhasvabhāvā eva 
tatsādhanatvena vidheyā iti tadviṣayaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ kena nivāryate | nanu 
vastusvābhāvyādeva bhāvārthaviṣayo vidhiḥ iti tasyasiddhenārthena 
sambandhāsambhavādatrāpi dadhyādisādhyabhāvārthavidhireva na 
dadhyādividhistato na siddhārthaviṣayatā | kutastarhi dadhyādīnāṃ 
tatsādhanatvāvagatiḥ | kārya ivātrāpi pramāṇāntarāsambhavād 
tadanuraktabhāvārthavidhyākṣepasāmarthyāt 
paśvekatvāderivopādānaśeṣatvena | tadevamastu alaṃ prakriyādausthyena | 
atrāpi devatoparaktabhāvārthavidhānasāmarthyād 
dadhyāderivopādānaśeṣatvena devatāyā api tatkārakabhūtayāḥ 
siddhiriti neśvarāsiddhirdevatāviśeṣarūpatvāttasyeti || 92 ||

	atra kārikābhāgena paraḥ-

devatā śabdamātraṃ cet

yuktaṃ dadhyādipadānāmatra 
pramāṇāntarasiddhārthaviṣayatvenopādānaśeṣabhāvayogyamarthaṃ 
pratipādayatāṃ taccodanātvam, devatāpadānāṃ tu 
prāmāṇāntarasiddhārthapratipādakatvenāsiddherna tathātvamiti 
ānarthakyāt, yadi paraṃ sāmasu stobhādīnāmiva svarūpeṇaiva 
kāryānvayo nārthadvāreṇeti codanātvāsiddherna devatā siddhiriti ||

	bhāgāntareṇaitatpratikṣepaḥ-

				nārthe vijñānato bhavet |

	yuktaṃ stobhādīnāmarthapratipādakatve pramāṇābhāvāt 
svarūpoccāraṇe naiva kāryānvayaḥ | devatāśrutīnāṃ tū raudraḥ 
paśurupākartavyaḥ tryambakaṃ yajāmahe brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇamālabheta 
viṣṇurupāṃśuryaṣṭavyaḥ ityādinā vividhavibhaktyarthānvayadarśanāt, 
dadhyādiśrutīnāmivārthapratipādakatvasiddherartha eva niyojanāt 
taddvāreṇaivānvayasiddherbhavatyeva devatālakṣaṇārthacodanātvamiti ||

	nanūktaṃ prāṇāntarasiddhārthapratipādakatvābhāvādeṣā-
manarthakatvamityabhyucca sayuktimāha-

kārakatvācca no yasmādapārthā kārakaśrutiḥ || 93 ||

	yāgasampradānakārakatvena tāvaddevatālakṣaṇo'rthaḥ 
śrūyamāṇo dadhyādivad upādānaśeṣabhāvena kāryānvayaṃ 
bhajatyeva tato yadīdantayā na pramāṇāntarasiddhaḥ tataḥ 
śrutisiddhyarthamaśrutopalabdhau yatnavatā bhavitavyaṃ na 
śrutiśaithilyamācaraṇīyam iti nyāyenātra kārakaśrutyanyathānupapattyā 
yūpāhavanīyādiśabdavācyārthavat śāstrasiddho'bhyupagantavya iti 
tatpratipādakatayā devatāśrutīnāmanarthakatvāsiddherdevatārthatvameveti 
|| 93 ||

	api ca-

dhyānaśruteśca sā bhinnā śabdato gamyate'nyathā |
pratyakṣatvena śabdasya vyarthā syād dhyānacodanā || 94 ||

	yasyai devatāyai havirniruptaṃ syāttāṃ manasā dhyāyet iti śruteḥ 
śabdasya pratyakṣatvena dhyānaśrutyānarthakyāt śabdato'nyārtharūpā 
devateti gamyate || 94 ||

	kiṃ ca-

stotrataścārtharūpāsau devateha yataḥ stutiḥ |
arthaṃ viśiṣṭamuddiśya viśiṣṭaguṇakīrtanam || 95 ||

yājyānuvākyābhyāmiṣṭvā devatāṃ stuvīta iti śruteḥ guṇino 
guṇābhidhānaṃ ca stutiḥ ityabhyupagamādartha eva devatā na 
tadabhidhānātmakaḥ śabda iti || 95 ||

	nāpi vigraho'syā nirākartuṃ śakyaḥ śrutisiddhatvādityāha-

rūpabhedavatī sā ca codyate yatpṛthak pṛthak |
tāṃ vai dhyāyecchuteśceti manasā sve hi vigrahe || 96 ||

	yat yasmāt rūpeṇākṛtyā tejasā prabhāveṇa devatāntarebhyo 
bhinnāṃ devatāmabhidhyāyet iti śrutyantareṇa codyate, ataḥ pūrvasyā api 
śrutestāṃ manasā dhyāyet svaśarīra evetyarthaḥ- iti sampadyate devatāyā 
śarīrasiddhiḥ || 96 ||

	ataḥ kīdṛśaṃ kasyāstadrūpamiti viśeṣākāṅkṣāyām-

rūpaṃ tasyāśca vijñeyamarthavādaiḥ samarpitam |

	yathā hi aktāḥ śarkarā upadadhāti iti śruteḥ, kena aktā iti 
viśeṣākāṅkṣāyāṃ tejo vai ghṛtam ityarthavādāt, 
dravyaviśeṣapratipattiḥ | evamatrāpi rūpaviśeṣākāṃkṣāyāṃ 
sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ ityarthavādato rūpaviśeṣapratipattiḥ ||

	ata eva-

	tadrūpakathanenaiṣāṃ stutyarthatvaṃ na hīyate || 97 ||

	arthavādā hi evamarthaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ stuvanto'rthavādā ityucyante, 
anyathā nirarthakatvena stobhādīnāmivārthavādatvāyogāt, ataśca 
vidhivākyairapekṣitatvāt arthavādakṛtaścaiṣāṃ janānāṃ mativibhramaḥ 
ityapyayuktamudghoṣyate bhavadbhiḥ || 97 ||

	yeṣāmapi vidhīnāṃ nārthavādato viśeṣasteṣām |

vedaprānteṣu vijñeyaṃ maraṇādiṣu ca sphuṭam |
devatānāṃ ca sadrūpamavaikalyāya karmaṇām || 98 ||

	evaṃ ca vedāntetihāsādīnāṃ siddhārthapratipādakatve'pi 
karmakāṇḍāpekṣitadevatāviśeṣākārapratipādakatvena tadekavākyatayā 
prāmāṇyamityavirodhaḥ |

tataśca yaduktam-

upākhyānādirūpeṇa vṛttirvedavadeva hi |
dharmādau bhāratādīnām ||

iti tadyuktameva tebhyo bhrāntyasambhavāt |

	evaṃ ca vedāntarbhāvenāpi asya śāstrasya prāmāṇyamityāha-

teṣveva parameśasya rūpaṃ vidyātparāparam |
yaccāpi paramaṃ rūpaṃ tacchaive tasya kīrtitam || 99 ||

	yathā śvetāśvaraśāravādau |

īśvaro dvividhaḥ proktaḥ paraścāpara eva ca |
tābhyāṃ paratarastvanyo yo nirvaktuṃ na pāryate || iti |

	tataśca tadapekṣitaparatareśvarasvarūpapratipādakatvenāsya 
tadekavākyatayā prāmāṇyaṃ bhavadbhirapyabhyupagantavyameva || 99 ||

	nanu vedāntānāmātmaviṣayapratipattikartavyatāniṣṭhatvenāva-
gaterīśvaraviṣayatvāsambhavāt kutastadekavakyatayāsya 
prāmāṇyamityāha-

ātmā draṣṭavya ityādi vidhiśceśvara eva hi |
sarvaṃ śāstrasvarūpaṃ vai śrūyate yadanantaram || 100 ||

	tatrāpi ātmā draṣṭavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ 
ityādāvātmaśabdeneśvara eva jñātavyatayocyate, yatastadanantaraṃ 
sarveśvaraḥ sarvānugrāhaka ityātmasambhavādīśvaraviṣayatvena 
sarvamīśvaraśāstraprasiddhaṃ tasya svarūpaṃ śrūyate || 100 ||

	kiṃ ca-

adhikāriṇyaṇau śāstraṃ pratyakṣatvādanarthakam |

	yadi tatrātmaiva vivakṣitaḥ syāt, neśvaraḥ tatastasmin 
vedāntajñānādhikāriṇi ātmani proktakramato jñātṛtayā 
svasaṃvedanasiddhe jñātavya iti śāstraṃ kṛtakatvenānarthakameva | 
yacchrūyate-

yastanna veda kimṛcā kariṣyati |
ya ittadvidastu ime samāsate || iti |

tato'trātmaśabdeneśvara eva jñātavyatayopadiśyata ityabhyupagantavyam |

	api ca-

nṛrudrasaṃjñe caikārthe dṛśyete hi kvacinmanau || 101 ||

	yathā rudreṣvatharvaśirasi sa evātmā sa eveśvara 
ityatrātmaśabdasyeśvare prayogo dṛśyata eva || 101 ||

kimata ityāha-

kriyārthaṃ sādhanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tatsvarūpāvabhāsakam |

ataśca ātmā jñātavyaḥ ityāderīśvarapratipattikartavyatāniṣṭhatvena 
tatprayukteśvarasvarūpapratipādanaikavākyatayāsya prāmāṇyaṃ 
bhavadbhirapi abhyupagantavyameva ||

	atra paraḥ

mūrtimattvena devānāṃ kṛtatvaṃ nanu tadbhavet || 102 ||

	yadāhuḥ-

carmopamaścetso'nityaḥ khatulyaścedasatsamaḥ | iti |
tataḥ pūrvoktakārakatvānupapattirasyeti || 102 ||

ācāryastu mūrtimattvamabhyupagamyāpyetat pratikṣipati-

na sālādiṣu taddṛṣṭaṃ svarūpaṃ pratipādayet |

	naitad anaikāntikatvāt, yathā hi sālakhadirādīnāmanityānāmapi 
atra khadire badhnāti ityādiśrutibhiḥ kārakatvam, evaṃ devatānāmapi 
proktaśrutibhirbhaviṣyatīti ko virodhaḥ |

atha naiṣāṃ kriyārthatvaṃ devatāsvapi tatsamam || 103 ||

	atha jāteriva nityāyāḥ stotrāṅgatvamityabhyupagamāt, anityānāṃ 
khadirādiviśeṣāṇāṃ kārakatvam, neṣyate tarhi devatātmano 
nityasyaivātrāṅgatvam, na tu taccharīrāṇāṃ parapurapraveśana 
ivopādeyaparityājyatvenānityānāmiti sarvaṃ samānam | ata eva 
yāgasādhanatvānyathānupapattyā yugapadvibhinnadeśavyavasthitā-
nekādhikāryanuṣṭhīyamānānekakratusādhanatvaṃ devatāyāḥ 
prākāmyato'nekanirmāṇakāyaracanena kalpyata iti aiśvaryasiddhiḥ || 103 ||

	nanvatra brahmendrarudrādīnāṃ bahūnāṃ devatātvena 
śrutermatibhedasambhavādīśvarāsiddhirityāha-

īśvare tu viśeṣo'sau tajjñānādeva gamyate |

	śrutita eveśvaraviṣayo viśeṣo'sau pratīyate | yataḥ śrūyate-

indraṃ mitraṃ varuṇamagnimāhuratho divyaḥ sa suparṇo garutmān |
ekaṃ sadviprā bahudhā vadanti agniṃ yamaṃ mātariśvānamāhuḥ || iti |

	eka eveśvaro'dhiṣṭheyabhedād anekatvena rājaniyogabhedeneva 
vyapadiśyate sa ca rudra eva | yacchrūyate eka eva rudro'vatasthe na dvitīyaḥ 
śataṃ śatā sahasrāṇi

ye rudrā adhibhūmyām iti tasyaiva bhagavato rudreṣu 
vaiśvarūpyapratipādanāt | nanu guṇatvena devatāyāḥ 
śruterdadhyādīnāmiva neśvaratā | na, sampradānakārakatvena tasyāḥ 
pradhānatvāt karturīpsitatamena karmaṇā yamabhipraiti sa sampradānam | 
yadyevam, sarvasyaiva karmaṇo darśapaurṇamāsādeḥ aindraṃ 
dadhyamāvasyāyām ityādiśrutisiddhendrādidevatārthatvād 
guṇakarmatvena na vidhiviṣayatā, taduktam-

yaistu dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate guṇastatra pratīyate | iti |

	na ātmopadeśavadāttithyavaccopapatteḥ | yathā hi ātmā jñātavyaḥ 
ityātmakarmako'pi bhāvārthaḥ yathā ca atithaye samabhyāgatāya 
mahokṣaṃ mahā vā ālabheta ityatithyarthamapi karma coditatvena 
anuṣṭheyatvād vidhiviṣayamiṣyate bhavadbhistadvadetadapi 
bhaviṣyatītyavirodhaḥ | kutastarhi phalam | devatāta eva bhavatāmiva vā 
niyogataḥ yathā hi grāmakāmo rājaputrasevāṃ kuryāt ityādau 
rājaputrasevāviṣayād rājaniyogataḥ phalam, evamatrāpi 
devatārādhanaviṣayāt niyogata eva phalamiti | nanu 
rājaputrādāvārādhanasampattiḥ pramāṇāntarasiddhā | bhavatu, iha tu 
śāstrasiddhā tadavikalakriyādyanuṣṭhānataḥ śraddhāprāmāṇyena vā 
pratīyata ityuktaṃ mahāmatibhiḥ devatākāṇḍe | trikāṇḍo hi vedavicāraḥ 
kriyākāṇḍadevatākāṇḍopaniṣatkāṇḍabhedena jaiminimuniprabhṛtibhiḥ 
sūtrakārairārabdhaḥ, sa tu 
karmakāṇḍamātradarśibhirmīmāṃsakairajñānataḥ 
karmamātraviṣayatvena nītaḥ | ata eva ta āhuḥ-

na buddhibhedaṃ janayedajñānāṃ karmasaṅginām | iti |

	ācāryo devatākāṇḍasiddhavākyārthavirodhena karmakāṇṣasyāpi 
devatāprāmāṇyaṃ yojayitumatroktavān-

īśvare tu viśeṣo'sau tajjñānādeva gamyate || iti |

	ata evopaniṣatkāṇḍadṛṣṭyāpyāha-

dharmaśāstre viśuddhiryā paramā puruṣasya hi || 104 ||

īśvarapraṇidhānācca yogaśāstre'pi paṭhyate |

	vedānte hīśvarādhyātmākṛtiprāṇavirāḍbhūtendriyeṣu madhyād 
īśvaropāsanayaiva mokṣaḥ śrūyate smṛtyādāvapi agnihotrādīṣṭavat 
pūrto dharma īśvarasaparyātmakaḥ-

pakveṣṭakacitaṃ samyagyaḥ karoti śivālayam |

iti ca śrūyate-

		pūrte mokṣaṃ vinirdiśet |

iti, vāyupurāṇādau ca-

anupāsyeśvaraṃ mokṣo baddhasyeva kuto bhavet |

iti, mahābhāratādiṣvapi itihāseṣu kuṇḍadhāropākhyānādau, gītāsu ca-

yo māmajamanādiṃ ca vetti lokamaheśvaram |
asammūḍhaḥ sa matyeṣu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || iti |

	īśvaropāsanayaiva pātañjaleṣvapi mokṣa iti na 
tadabhāvo'bhyupagantavyaḥ sarvāgamavirodhāt ||

	nanu yogaśāstreṣvīśvarapraṇidhānāt samādhilābhaḥ paṭhyate na 
muktirityāha-

kleśagrāmasya tanutā samādhistatphalaṃ phalam || 105 ||

	avidyāsmitārāgadveṣābhiniveśāḥ kleśāḥ, 
tatsamudāyasyāpacitataratvamupaśama eva samādhihetutvāt 
samādhiyogaścittajayātmaka iti, īśvarapraṇidhānasya taditi 
mokṣaphalameva samādhilakṣaṇaṃ phalaṃ na tato'nyadityavirodhaḥ || 105 ||

	nanu evaṃ sati sāvayavātmanā mūrtimattvena 
vedavedārthayoranityatvādaprāmāṇyamityāha-

sthitā santatirūpeṇa devatā tatra cākṛtiḥ |
śabdārthayoranityatvaṃ mūrtimattvena bādhyate || 106 ||

	yataḥ parapurapraveśipuruṣavad 
upādeyaparityājyaśarīrasantatyātmanā devatā vedārthabhūtā 
vyavasthitetyuktam, yataśca tatra śabde'rthe ca jātiḥ sthitā tato 
mūrtimattvena yat śabdārthayoratrānityatvamaprāmāṇyasādhanāyoktaṃ 
tad bādhyate, jāterevātra sthirāyāḥ pramāṇaprameyatvavyavasthāpanāt | 
yadāhuḥ jātirevāṅgaṃ iti mīmāṃsakāḥ || 106 ||

upasaṃhartumāha-

evaṃ ca kalpyamāne syāt ko doṣo yena saṃśritam ||
nāstikyaṃ devatāsattvaṃ gadadbhiḥ paṇḍitairapi || 107 ||

evaṃ vedasya devatāpradhānatve'pi prāmāṇyasiddherniṣprayojanameva 
devatāyāḥ abhāvakhyāpanena mīmāṃsakairnāstikapakṣa saṃśrita 
ityācāryaḥ-

prāyeṇa maurkhyānmīmāṃsā loke lokāyatīkṛtā |
tāmāstikapathe kartumayaṃ yatnaḥ kṛto mayā ||

ityetadapi darśayati || 107 ||

	yadyevamīśvarapraṇatitvena vedaḥ pramāṇaṃ 
vedācceśvarasiddhiritītaretarāśrayadoṣaḥ | yadāhuḥ evaṃ vedo'pi 
tatpūrvastatsadbhāvādau vacane sāśaṅko na pramāṇaṃ syāt iti | etat 
pratikṣipati-

īśoktatve'pi na�veṣṭā tatkṛtatvena mānatā |
anyonyāśrayatā tasmāddoṣo nānityavādinām || 108 ||

	na hi īśvarakṛtatvena pramāṇaṃ vedo'smābhirīṣyate 
tanukaraṇādibhiranaikāntikatvāt, api tu pramāṇaṃ sad īśvarakṛta 
itīśvarakṛtatve'pi śāstrasya nānyonyāśrayadoṣaḥ || 108 ||

	kathaṃ tarhyasya prāmāṇyamityāha-

śāstrasya mānatā siddhā nyāyataḥ prākprabhāṣitāt | 

	tanukaraṇabhuvanādivailakṣaṇyena pramājanakatvādeva 
pratyakṣāderivāsya prāmāṇyamityuktaṃ vākyāddhi sarvataḥ 
pūrvamarthe dhīḥ ityādinā | proktaṃ ca bhavadbhirapi-

svataḥ sarvapramāṇānāṃ prāmāṇyam | iti |
nityatvamanityatvaṃ ca prāmāṇye'nupakārakameva ||

	kimevamīśvaraprayuktatvamapi śabdasyātrānupakārakameva | na, 
ityāha-

īśoktatvādamānatvaṃ jātucinnopapadyate || 109 ||

	kṛtakatvāt rathyāpuruṣavākyavadaprāmāṇyāśaṅkā asya 
kevalamīśvarapraṇītatvena nivartyate | rathyāpuruṣāṇāṃ hi 
kāmakrodhādiyogana dṛṣṭaṃ viparītābhidhānam, īśvarasya tu 
tadabhāvāt pitrādeḥ putrādāviva sarvatropakārāyaiva pravṛtterna 
tathātvamityadoṣaḥ || 109 ||

nanu niṣprayojanamatra vedasyeśvarakartṛkatvasādhanamityāha-

samānatve tataḥ kṛtye parituṣṭaḥ pravartate |

	yo hi mohād vedasya nityatvena prāmāṇyamabuddhā 
kṛtakatvenātrāprāmāṇyamadhyāropya na pravartate, pravartamāno vā 
anyena niṣiddhyate, taṃ prati vedasyāpi kṛtakatvaṃ 
pratiśāstrasāmyapratipattaye tu dīkṣitānāṃ pravṛttyarthaṃ pratipādyata 
iti na niṣprayojanametaditi |

	punaḥ prayogāntarabhrāntyā kārikābhāgena paraḥ

asarvavitkathaṃ devaṃ jānāti

	yadāha-

sarvajño gṛhyate tāvannedānīmasmadādibhiḥ |
nāstyato'nupalabhyatvātpuṃsāmaśvaviṣāṇavat || iti |

	etadapyayuktamityāha-

					uktamānataḥ || 110 ||

sākṣācca sevanīyo hi yogastenādarāttataḥ |
khanyavādādisiddhena saṃskṛtākṣo'ñjanādinā || 111 ||

yathā paśyati sūkṣmādyapītareṣāmatīndriyam |
saṃskṛtātmā tu yogena tathā yogī prapaśyati || 112 ||

māheśvareṇa vastūni sarvāṇi parameṇa tu |

	āgamasiddhavastukramābhyāsajena parameṇa yogena 
samabhivyaktajñānātiśayo yogī sarvāṇyevātīndriyāṇi īśvarādīni 
vastūni paśyati yathā siddhāñjanādinā saṃskṛtanetraḥ puruṣaḥ 
puruṣamātrāgocarāṇi atyantavyavahitāni nidhānādīnīti 
yogipratyakṣasiddheśvare asmadādyagrahaṇamasiddhatvāt, 
nābhāvasādhanamiti |

	nanu yoga eva tāvanna siddhaḥ kutasteneśvaradarśanam, nātra 
asiddhatā | ityāha-

dṛṣṭā ca prātibheneha vākyārthavyāpṛtiṃ vinā || 113 ||

meye triṣvapi kāleṣu liṅgāni ca vinā matiḥ |
prātibhasya tathā yogī prakṛṣṭena tu sarvathā || 114 ||

minoti yugapatsarvaṃ tatsiddhyai yogacodanā |

	yathā śvaste bhrātāgamiṣyati iti āgamānumānābhyāṃ vinā 
prātibhenaiva yogena prātibhasya puṃsaḥ trikālāvisaṃvādinī 
matirdṛṣṭā tathaiva yogino'pi yogenaiveśvarādiviṣayā bhaviṣyatīti 
sambhāvanānumānasiddhatvāt, yogapratikṣepastāvadayuktaḥ, 
codanāsiddhatvācca | vedānteṣu hi

ātmānamaraṇiṃ kṛtvā praṇavaṃ cottarāraṇim |
dhyānanirmathanābhyāsādevaṃ paśyennigūḍhavat ||

ityādayo yogacodanāḥ smṛtītihāsapurāṇādiṣu ca santīti 
yogijñānamapratikṣepyameva |

	na ca teṣāṃ codanāvākyānāmaprāmāṇyamityāha-

vyabhicāro na yasya syādapramāṇaṃ kathaṃ hi tat || 115 ||

taccoditakriyākartā tatphalāptā ca no katham |

	aṅgulyagre hastiyūthaśatamāste ityādau vyabhicāradarśanāt 
laukikaṃ vākyamapramāṇamityuktam | na ca codanāvākyasya vyabhicāraḥ 
kasyacidapyasti ityetat pramāṇameva codanā 
siddheśvarādipramitikriyākartari mokṣaphale viśiṣṭe yogātmani 
svābhidheya iti |

	anyathā-

vedo'pi tatsamānatvānna mānaṃ nāpi bhogakṛt || 116 ||

	viśeṣābhāvāt karmakāṇḍapratipādakasyāpi jyotiṣṭomena yajet 
ityādervedabhāgasyāprāmāṇyaṃ svargādiphalakartṛtvābhāvaśca 
prasajyeta iti || 116 ||

	atra parābhiprāyaḥ-

iṣyate'trobhayaṃ taccet

	athāsmin bhāge prāmāṇyaṃ svargādiphalakartṛtvaṃ 
cetyubhayamiṣyate |

	ācārya āha-

				yoge'pyetattatheṣyatām |

nyāyasya samānatvād yogapratipādake'pi vedāntādau prāmāṇyaṃ 
mokṣaphalakartṛtvaṃ ca siddhamiti yoginaḥ pramāṇasiddhatvāt 
tajjñānasyeśvare pravṛttisiddherasmadādyadarśanamasiddhatvānna 
tadabhāvasādhanamiti |

	upasaṃharati-

itthaṃ sarvapramāṇānāṃ gocaro hara īritaḥ || 117 ||

	evaṃ cānumānāgamayogipratyakṣairīśvaraḥ siddha iti 
tadabhāvābhidhānādajñataiva bhavatāmiti || 117 ||

	adhunā īśvarasvarūpaṃ vipratipattinirākaraṇena pratipādayati |

sa ca svabhāvavaimalyāt kārmamāyīyavarjitaḥ |

	tatkāryasya kārmamāyīyasya pravāhato'nāditvāt so'pi 
tatkartṛtvādevānādisiddhaḥ svabhāvavimalatvenoktaḥ, ādisiddhasya hi 
muktātmanaḥ prāgbandhasadbhāvataḥ svābhāvavaimalyāsambhavāt | 
tataśca ye karmaviśeṣānuṣṭhānāt śatakratuvad īśvaram, ye tu 
pātañjalā buddhyādisāmarthyena, naiyāyikā vaiśeṣikāśca 
manoyogādāhuste pratikṣiptāḥ | tathābhūtasya puruṣavat 
saṃsāritveneśvaratvāyogāt | 

	nanu muktaśivavattasyāpīśvarāntarādaiśvaryamiti 
pravāhanityeśvaravādinaḥ | tān pratikṣipati-

parasmāttasya vaimalye muktātmāsau tataśca saḥ || 118 ||

kṛtastu vimalo yena svabhāvavimalo hyasau |

	bhavedetad yadīśvaratāyā vināśaḥ syāt, sa tu nāstīti vakṣyāmaḥ, 
tataścotpādyotpādakayordvayorapi īśvarayoḥ sannidhānāt pūrvaḥ 
pūrvataro vā ravabhāvavimalo'bhyupagantavya iti kutaḥ 
pravāhanityeśvaravādaḥ |

	ata eva-

vaimalyāttasya no karma nirmalaḥ karmakṛnnahi || 119 ||

malakarmavihīnatvānmāyīyaṃ no kalādikam |

	āṇavasya bandhasyābhāvāt kārmasyāpyabhāvastayorabhāvāt 
māyīyasyāpi, iti sarvabandhavihīno bhagavān siddhaḥ | karmābhāve'pi 
saṃskārataḥ cakrabhramavat dhṛtaśarīratvāt keṣāñcit samalānāmeva 
māyīyo bandho'stīti dvayoratropādānam | taduktaṃ svāyambhuvavṛttau 
malamāyākhyayuktānāmapi anugrahaḥ sambhavati iti ||

ata eva ca-

niṣkalatvātkalādyutthā rāgadveṣādayaḥ kutah || 120 ||

	māyīyabandhābhāvādeva tadātmakānāṃ tasya 
rāgadveṣādīnāmabhāvaḥ siddhaḥ, iti puruṣakṛtakarmānusāreṇa 
sukhaduḥkādipradatvaṃ na rāgadveṣādibhirityuktam-

		yena yādṛgvidhaṃ karma

ityādi || 120 ||

	kathaṃ tarhi-

sakalaṃ caiva yo vetti sadāśivam |

ityādau īśvaraḥ sakalaḥ prokta ityāha-

sakalo hi maheśāno gītaḥ śaktikalānvitaḥ |

sakalo hi saśarīraḥ kathyate, śaktaya eva tasya śarīramityuktam-

jñānakriye śivaḥ proktaḥ sarvārthe nirmale pare | iti ||

	kiṃ ca-

sakalo'pi ca śaktyaiva sarvaṃ vetti karoti ca || 121 ||

sakalasya ātmano'pi śaktireva śarīraṃ tayaiva sarvatropayogāt, 
tadupakārakatvācca, idaṃ bāhyamupacāreṇa śarīramityucyate || 121 ||

	tataḥ kimityāha-

śaktistena kalāpiṇḍaḥ sakalaśca maheśvaraḥ |

	tena śaktirevānekakāryopayogāt kalāpiṇḍaḥ śarīraṃ 
paramārthataḥ kathyata itīśvaraḥ sakalaḥ proktaḥ ||

	sa eva tu-

śaktipiṇḍakalāvatvācchaktimānniṣkalaḥ smṛtaḥ || 122 ||

	śaktiśarīratvādeva tasya bāhyakalādyātmakaṃ śarīraṃ nāstīti 
niṣkalaḥ | yaduktaṃ mokṣakārikāsu-

na kalādikalāpiṇḍasambaddho'sau yathā paśuḥ |
kadācidapi deveśastasmādgītastu niṣphalaḥ || iti ||

tataśca sakalaniṣkalayorna paramārthato bheda ityuktaṃ bhavati | yaduktaṃ 
tattvasaṅgrahe-

atra ca tattvadvitayaṃ bodhadhyānādisiddhaye gaditam |
hyamūrtiśca tadvāṃśceti ca leśāduktiśca śaktiśaktimatoḥ ||

iti || 122 ||

	atraiva matāntaramāha-

ceṣṭāvān sakalaḥ kaiścit tadviyuktastu niṣphalaḥ |

	yadā sthityādikarmāṇi vidhatte tadā sakalaḥ, samupahṛtakriyastu 
niṣphala iti kaiścit sakalaniṣkalayoravasthābhedena bhedaḥ smṛto na vastutaḥ 
| taddimuktam-

īśaḥ sadāśivaḥ śāntaḥ kṛtyabhedādvibhidyate | iti ||

	yadyevaṃ puruṣasyāpi evaṃ sakalaniṣkalātmakatvasambhavāttataḥ 
ko bhedo'syetyāha-

vimalatvācca savārthasambaddhe tasya dṛkkriye || 123 ||

	dṛkkriyāvārakasya malasyāpyabhāvādeva yugapat 
sarvārthasambaddhe tasya jñānakriye, puruṣasya malāvṛtatvāt krameṇeti 
bhedaḥ || 123 ||

	api ca-

sarvakartṛtvataścāpi sarvajñaḥ so'dhigamyate |

	na kevalaṃ vimalatvād yāvat prākpradarśitāt sarvakartṛtvādapi 
īśvaraḥ sarvajñaḥ siddhyati ||

	athamityāha-

kumbhakāro yathā vetti sarvaṃ saphalakārakam || 124 ||

sarvakartā tathā vetti sarvaṃ saphalakārakam |

	yathā prāggṛhītamūlyasaṃśuddhaye mṛdādinānena ghaṭaṃ 
kariṣyāmīti kumbhakārādiḥ kartā svakārye sarvajñaḥ san kartā dṛṣṭaḥ 
tathaiva bhagavānapi sarvakartṛtvāt sarvasmin svakārye jñaḥ siddhyatīti 
sarvajñaḥ proktaḥ ||

yadyevaṃ kartṛtvasya krameṇa darśanāt tato vijñānamapi krameṇa 
siddhyatīti yugapat sarvajñatvābhāvastasyetyāha-

mocyotpādyopasaṃhāryanirodhyapreryavastuṣu || 125 ||

ekakālabhaveṣveṣu yugapatkartṛtā matā | 

	kriyā hi kramikā na kārakatvaṃ kumbhakārādāvapi 
yugapadanekabhojanagamanādikriyāsu kartṛtvasiddheriti eṣu mocyādiṣu 
kāryeṣu nimittabhūteṣu bhavaviṣayā bhagavato yugapatkartṛtā yato'taśca 
jñānamapi yugapadeva siddhyatīti na virodhaḥ |

	ata eva tasya trikālāmalajñānasiddhirityāha-

tacca trikālakaṃ tena yaditthaṃ vetti śaṅkaraḥ || 126 ||

anenedaṃ kṛtaṃ karma deyaṃ cāsyedṛśaṃ phalam | 
evaṃ hi sarvakṛtyeṣu trikālaviṣayaṃ hi tat || 127 ||

boddhavyaṃ nānyathā śambhoḥ syāditaḥ kṛtakṛtyatā |

tadidamuktaṃ mumbhakāro yathā vetti ityādinā |

ata eva-

pramāṇaṃ tacca śaivākhyaṃ paśumānavilakṣaṇam || 128 ||

dvāradvāritayā kṛtyaṃ sākṣācceṣṭaṃ tu tatphalam |

	yathāvasthitaṃ sarvameva vastu tena pramīyate'taḥ 
paśumānavilakṣaṇaṃ tat pramāṇam | prayojanaṃ tu tasyeśvarajñānasya 
vidyeśvarādidvāreṇa pañcavidhajagatkṛtyasampādanaṃ sākṣācca 
vidyāvidyeśvarādyanugraha iti |

	paśupramāṇasyaitādvilakṣaṇaṃ svarūpamāha-

sadākṣaliṅgavākyaiśca vyaktaṃ jñānaṃ tu paudgalam || 129 ||

pratyakṣamanumānaṃ ca śābdaṃ cetyuditaṃ kramāt |

	indriyādyupāyatrayabhedāt pratyakṣānumānāgamabhedena 
trividhaṃ parimitaviṣayaṃ ceti |

	atha kathaṃ tadevopāyāpekṣaṃ na pārameśamityāha-

śaivaṃ na vyañjakāpekṣaṃ jñānamāvṛtihānitaḥ || 130 ||

gurubhiḥ paśumānebhyo gītaṃ tena vidharmakam |

mālādipāśairanāvṛtatvātpārameśaṃ nendriyādyupāyāpekṣamartheṣu 
pravartate- iti paśupramāṇavilakṣaṇamuktam |

	ata eva-

na sandehaviparyāsarūpaṃ tadanapekṣaṇāt || 131 ||

jñānametatpaśau yasmād dṛṣṭaṃ vyañjakadoṣataḥ |

	paśujñānavad indriyādyupāyānapekṣaṇāt na tat 
saṃśleṣadoṣairmithyātvājñānasaṃśayairyujyate- iti tadvilakṣaṇam |

	nāpi gṛhītagrāhitve smṛtirūpaṃ tadityāha-

sarvadaivoditatvācca smārtaṃ tadapi no bhavet || 132 ||

	grāhakātmano jñānasya prathamakāṇḍa eva nityatvenaikatayā 
pratipāditatvādadhyavasāyajñānasyaiva smṛtyādibhedaḥ sambhavati- 
ityato'pi tadvilakṣaṇam || 132 ||

	kimata ityāha-

itthaṃ ca mānarūpeṇa vihīnaṃ mānameva tat |

	ataśca saṃśayādirahitattve sati arthasākṣātkārātmakatvād 
indriyapratyakṣavat tadapi īśvarapratyakṣaṃ pramāṇameva |

	atra paraḥ-

nanvadehasya vijñānaṃ na kartṛtvaṃ ca yuktimat || 133 ||

śaṅkarasya yataḥ puṃsāṃ dehādau sati dṛśyate |

	vijñānasya dehāvinābhāvo dehapāratantryamasmadādiṣu siddham- 
ityarthād dehābhāvena saha virodhasiddheradehasarvajñatvaṃ 
prayogāntareṇātra niṣiddhyata iti apaunaruktyam | yadāha maṇḍano 
vidhiviveke-

			paratantraṃ bahirmanaḥ

iti ||

	etat pratikṣipati |

na yuktimaccharīrādiniṣedhe mānamīritam || 134 ||

sarvajñatvādisiddhau ca maheśasya yataḥ sphuṭam |

naitad yuktaṃ yato madhyamakāṇḍe kulālasamatāṃ patyuḥ ityādinā tasya 
śarīrādipratisiddham, atra ca sarvajñatvaṃ prasādhitamiti 
aśarīratvasarvajñatvayorvirodhābhāvāt, na sahāvasthānupapattiriti na 
viruddhopalambho'yaṃ sarvajñatvābhāvasādhanaḥ |

	nanu pāratantryaṃ bahirmanodṛṣṭāntasiddhamityāha-

saṃruddhatvācca tatpuṃsāṃ vyaktau dehādyapekṣate || 135 ||

	yadi yatra mano'ntaḥkaraṇamabhipretaṃ tad vyadhikaraṇāsiddho 
hetuḥ | tasya pāratantryāt sarvajñatvasya kā anupapattiḥ, na hi manoyogāt 
tatra sarvajñatvaṃ puruṣa iva upapadyata ityuktam | prātibhayogināṃ ca 
pūrvanayenendriyānapekṣayā svātantryeṇaiva manaso bāhyārthe 
pravṛttisiddheranaikāntikaśca | atha mano grāhakātmaiva vivakṣitam, 
tadindriyādipāratantryam, bhavatāṃ paramātmādāvadarśanād 
avidyāparuddhatvena siddhamitīśvare tadbhāvāt tasyāpyabhāva ityadoṣaḥ 
||

	evaṃ sarvakartā'pi sa evetyāha-

mantramantreśvarebhyo'pi maheśe mahatī kriyā || 139 ||

	mantramantreśānāṃ sarvajñatve'pi 
māyāviṣayasarvakartṛtvayogataḥ śuddhe'dhvani kartṛtvāsambhavāt, 
neśvaravat sarvakartṛtvamiti tebhyo'pi viśiṣṭa eva || 139 ||

	kiṃ ca-

muktebhyo'pi viśiṣṭo'sau svabhāvavimalatvataḥ |

	muktaśivānāṃ sarvajñatvavad 
īśvaratulyasarvakartṛtvayoge'pyanādisarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvābhāvāt, na 
tatsamatvam ||

	na kevalamanādireva-

vibhuścāsau yatastasya dṛkkriyālakṣaṇo guṇaḥ || 140 ||

sarvārthago vinā noyad guṇino guṇasannidhiḥ |

	sarvatra tacchaktikāryasya sthityādyanugrahāt tasya darśanāt 
śaktīnāmiva tasyāpi vyāpakatvaṃ śaktimatā vinā śakterasambhavo 
yataḥ ||

	kiṃ ca-

nityaśca taṃ vinā kālaḥ kaścitsambhāvyate na yat || 141 ||

na hi īśvararahitaḥ kaścit kālalavo'pi sambhāvayituṃ śakyaḥ tatkāryasya 
sthityāderanupapattitaḥ saṃsārasya 
āsamañjasyaprasaṅgātityavinaśvareśvarasiddhiḥ || 141 ||

	nanu tadaivānyo bhaviṣyatīti pravāhanityeśvaravādinaḥ pratikṣipati-

dṛkkriyāguṇavattvācca kāraṇābhāvataśca saḥ |

	nitya ityanuvartate | yo dṛkkriyāguṇayuktaḥ sa nityo yathā ātmā, 
dṛkkriyāguṇayuktaśceśvaraḥ tasmād avinaśvaraḥ | yaśca akāraṇaḥ sa 
nityo yathā ātmādiḥ, akāraṇaśca īśvaraḥ proktaḥ tato'pyavinaśvara iti 
na pravāhanityeśvaravādaḥ ||

	yadapyāhuḥ-

jagacca sṛjatastasya kiṃ prayojanamucyatām | 
prayojanamanuddiśya na mando'pi pravartate ||
evameva pravṛttiśceccaitanyenāsya kiṃ bhavet || iti |

	tatrāpyāha-

malādyabhāvatastasya ceṣṭā na svātmabhūtaye || 142 ||

yuktā nāpi svabhogāya sarvajñatvādiyogataḥ |

	nirmalatvena prāptaprāptavyasya na tāvat tasya puruṣasyeva svātmano 
mokṣārthā sarvajñatvācca nāpi bhogārthā pravṛttirupapadyate 
bhogasyājñānānubandhitvena sarvajñe'nupapatteruktatvāt ||

	nāpi krīḍārthetyāha-

krīḍārthā yujyate ceṣṭā sā noktavyatirekataḥ || 143 ||

vartate tanniṣedhena tasyāstena niṣedhataḥ |

	krīḍādyarthamapi na tasya ceṣṭā yujyate taddhetutvenoktasya 
malādervyatirekād abhāvāt, malādihetukā hi puruṣeṣu krīḍā dṛṣṭeti 
malādestatra niṣedhādeva tasyā api niṣedhaḥ | taduktaṃ bhavadbhirapi-

krīḍārthāyāṃ pravṛttau tu vihanyeta kṛtārthatā | iti ||

	itaśca na tasya svārthā krīḍādyarthā vā ceṣṭetyāha-

kiṃ ceśe vyāpṛte puṃsā bhuktirmuktiśca dṛśyate || 144 ||

ceṣṭātaḥ puṃsphalāyaiva svābhāvyāttasya gamyate |

	prāguktakrameṇa ca mokṣasyeśvaravyāpāraṃ vinānupapatteḥ tasya 
parārthameva ceṣṭā paropakārasvābhāvyādeva pratīyate nānyataḥ ||

	mukterīśvarakartṛtvaṃ pratijñātaṃ sādhayituṃ svarūpamāha-

muktiḥ pareśatulyatvaṃ mantramantreśatā parā || 145 ||

	sarvajñatvakartṛtve svabhāvaḥ puruṣasyetyuktam | 
atastatsvarūpāvāptiḥ parameśatulyatvaṃ parā muktiḥ | aparā tu 
vakṣyamāṇamantramantreśatulyateti || 145 ||

	sā ceśvareṇa kriyate, na tu jñānamokṣavādināmiva 
puruṣeṇetyāha-

svayaṃ karotyaṇurnaitāṃ pāśaruddhabalatvataḥ |

	nanu jyotiṣṭomādyanuṣṭhānena svargādibhogavat ātmā jñātavyaḥ 
ityādyanuṣṭhānāt puruṣa eva mokṣaṃ kariṣyatīti pāśarūddhabalatvasya 
mokṣakartṛtvena saha virodhāsiddheranaikāntikatā | kaḥ punaratra 
pāśo'bhimato bhavatām | nanvajñānameva, tat kiṃ vastu, avastu vā | 
avastutve tasya ca śaśaviṣāṇāderiva nityanivṛttatvāt tannivartako 
jñānānuṣṭhānavidhireṣo'narthaka eva | vastutve yadi puruṣasvabhāva eva 
tato nityatvavyāpakatvavad anivartyatvād anirmokṣaḥ, vyatireke 
tvāvārakasvabhāvaṃ cakṣuṣaḥ paṭalādivat ajñānahetutvād 
ajñānaśabdavācyaṃ dravyāntarameva tad abhyupagantavyam | dravyasya 
cavārakasya tamasaḥ paṭalāderiva jñānanivartyasvabhāvāsiddheḥ 
pāśaruddhabalatvasya mokṣakartṛtvena saha virodhaḥ siddha iti 
nātrānaikāntikateti ||

	tarhi kṛtaprayojanatvātpāśāḥ svayameva puruṣaṃ muñcantīti 
sāṃkhyāḥ | yadāhuḥ-

raṅgasya darśayitvā nivartate nartakī yathā nṛtyāt |
puruṣasya tathātmānaṃ prakāśyavinivartate prakṛtiḥ || iti |

	atrāpi āha-

pāśā api na kurvanti yataste tannirodhakāḥ || 146 ||

na cāpi bandhanaṃ badhyaṃ vimuñcaddṛśyate yataḥ |

nahyatyantaparipākāt kṛtaprayojanatvena niścitto'pi paṭalādiḥ 
svayamāvarakatvena vā nivṛttisvabhāvaḥ siddho yena mokṣe'pi etat 
kalpyamānamupapadyate ||

	yata etat tasmāt-

balīyān paśupāśebhyastanmocakatayā haraḥ || 147 ||

sa eva mocakastasmātsarvasmādbhavabandhanāt |

	cakṣurādeḥ paṭalādinivṛttāviva pāśanivṛttau īśvara eva hetuḥ 
nānyaḥ ||

	nanu śuktirajatādibhrameṣu jñānasyājñānanivṛttau puṃsāṃ 
sāmarthyaṃ siddham, ato mokṣo'pi tathaiva bhaviṣyatītyāha-

puṃsāṃ sāpekṣaśaktīnāṃ sadānāvṛtaśaktikaḥ || 148 ||

yujyate śaktyabhivyaktau nitrāṇāṃ bhānumāniva |

	iha prathamakāṇḍoktajñānavadajñānamapi 
dvividhamadhyavasāyātmakameva viṣayasādṛśyāvivekena 
śuktikārajatādiṣu viparītajñānātmakamutpadyata iti yuktaiva tasya 
pratipakṣabhūtāt samyag jñānāt nivṛttiḥ | yat 
punaranadhyavasāyātmakaṃ tat dravyāntarād andhakārāderbhavat 
svahetuvipakṣāt dravyāntarādevādityādernivṛttisvabhāvaṃ siddhaṃ na 
pratipakṣajñānāt | anadhyavasāyātmakaṃ ca pauruṣamajñānaṃ 
suṣuptāvasthāsu iha pūrvaṃ pratipāditam, adhyavasāyātmano 
bauddhatvāt | atastadapi svahetuvipakṣabhūtād īśvarādeva vastvantarāt 
nivṛttisvabhāvamanumīyata iti sa eva mokṣahetuḥ siddhyati na tvātmānaḥ, 
tajjñānasya tatra sāmarthyāsiddherityadoṣaḥ ||

	kathaṃ tarhi ātmā jñātavyaḥ ityādyāḥ śrutayaḥ ityāha-

eṣṭavyaṃ cedṛśaṃ tattvaṃ nānyathā muktisambhavaḥ || 149 ||

	tā api ata eveśvarapratipattikartavyatāniṣṭhā evābhyupagantavyā 
ityuktam, anyathā mokṣāyogāt ||

	nanu varamamokṣo'stu natvīśvara ityanirmokṣavādinaḥ | yadāhuḥ-

anāditvād bhavaḥ puṃsāṃ na kadācinnivartate |
tataḥ sukhāsukhaprāptinivṛttī śāstrataḥ smṛte || iti |

	atrāpi āha-

bhavitavyaṃ tayā puṃsāṃ sarvāvāptyarhatā dhruvam |

sarvāvaraṇanivṛttisvabhāvāḥ puruṣā āvaraṇaprakarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ 
mūḍhādyavasthāsu apakṛṣṭaprakṛṣṭatatsvarūpopalambhāt, 
meghāvṛtasavitṛvad iti nānirmokṣo'bhyupagantuṃ yuktaḥ 
tanneśvarāsiddhiḥ | na ca bhavasyānāditvaṃ karmahetutvāt | yaduktam-

karmataśca śarīrāṇi viṣayāḥ karaṇāni ca | iti ||

	na cāgamaviruddho'yaṃ pakṣa ityāha-

īśānmokṣo'nyathā naiva puṃsāmetānnirūpitam || 150 ||

	tathā ca śrīpauṣkare-

na mokṣaṃ yāti puruṣaḥ svasāmarthyātkadācana | iti |

	śrīmaddīkṣottare'pi-

			śivādevātmanāṃ mokṣaḥ iti

	īśvaravyāpārātmakatvācca dīkṣāyāḥ śrīmatsvāyaṃbhuve'pi 
nirūpitam-

dīkṣaiva mocayatyūrdhvaṃ śaivaṃ dhāma nayatyasau |

iti || 150 ||

	nanvevamapi deṣāñcidanirmokṣadarśanādīśvare 
rāgadveṣasambhāvanetyāha-

na rāgo nāpi ca dveṣastasyetyetacca yuktitaḥ |

	īśvarasya rāgadveṣādyabhāva iti nirūpitaṃ madhyakāṇḍa eva 
ityayamapi na doṣaḥ ||

	na cāpuruṣārthabhūtatvād vaiśeṣikādīnāmivāyamapi mokṣo na 
kenacidāśrīyata ityāha-

vāñchā cāṇoḥ sadaivāsti sarvasyaivottame pade || 151 ||

atastadgīyamānaṃ tu na kadācinna manyate |

	na hi sarvabandhanivṛttyā sarvaiśvaryaprāptau kaścit nirabhilāṣaḥ 
sambhavatīti ||

	yadyevamīśvarasya nirapekṣitvāt kathaṃ na yugapat sarveṣāṃ 
muktirityāha-

aṇvanugrahasāmarthyaṃ sarvathā cāsti śaṅkare || 152 ||

tathāpi yugapanmuktirnāṇūnāṃ tena dṛśyate |

kuta ityāha-

sṛṣṭau yoneryathā kālaṃ maheśāno vyapekṣate || 153 ||

protsāraṇe tathā kālaṃ malasyāsāvapekṣate |

	yathā hi māyātaḥ sarganimittaṃ karmaparipākakālāpekṣitvena 
bhagavato na yugapat sarvabhogapradatvamityuktaṃ prāk | evaṃ 
mokṣanimittaṃ malaparipākāpekṣitvāt, na yugapat sarveṣāṃ 
mokṣaprasaṅgo nirapekṣitvasyaivāsiddheḥ ||

	atra parābhiprāyamāha-

apare'pi svatantro'sau rocante ye yathā yadā || 154 ||

pumāṃsastasmai devāya teṣāṃ muktistadā tathā |

	svatantro hi parameśvaraḥ śaktatvenānyānapekṣaṇāt, 
anyāpekṣāyāṃ tu sāpekṣamasamarthaṃ bhavatītyasamartha eva īśvaraḥ 
syāt | ato yo yathā parāparamuktasambandhitayā tasmai rocate taṃ tathaiva 
yojayati, na tu malaparipākādi apekṣate ||

	etat pratikṣipati-

punarbhavaprasaṅgaḥ syātsvātantryāditi no yataḥ || 155 ||

	naitat, yato mokṣa iva saṃsāre'pi kartavye'sau svatantra eva iti 
muktasyāpi punaḥ saṃsāraṃ kuryāt | duṣkṛtino'pi svargaṃ sukṛtinaśca 
narakamiti mokṣaśāstrāṇāṃ satkarmānuṣṭhānānāṃ cānarthakyāt 
lokāyata eva upāsyo bhavatām || 155 ||

	atha tatra karmavipākāpekṣayā naiṣa prasaṅga ityucyate, yadyevam-

svabhāvastasya naitādṛk pramitaḥ śaṅkarasya hi | 

	na tarhi anapekṣasvabhāvaḥ siddho'sau- iti bhoge'pi 
karmavipākamiva mokṣe malavipākamapekṣiṣyate- iti ko virodhaḥ | na ca 
karmādyapekṣitaivāsvātantryam, api tu īśvarāntarāyattatā | sā ca tasya 
parameśvaratvādeva nāstītyuktaṃ madhyamakāṇḍe ||

	kiṃ cāgamaviruddho'yaṃ pakṣa ityāha-

uktaṃ ca tena tattulyaḥ prāpnoti na bhavāntaram || 156 ||

pratipādayituṃ śakyaṃ nāpi mithyeti tadvacaḥ |

tathā hi śrīmatsvāyambhuvādau-

anādyaśuddhiśūnyatvāt prāpnoti na bhavāntaram |

iti yaduktaṃ tatsvatantraśaktipātapakṣe nopapadyate punarbhavaprasaṅgasya 
tatrāvaśyambhāvādityuktaṃ yataḥ | na ca pārameśvaraṃ 
vacanamapramāṇamityuktaṃ prāgeva, tataḥ punarbhavaprasaṅgasiddherna 
svatantraḥ śaktipāto'pi tu malaparipākāpekṣa eveti na yugapat sarveṣāṃ 
mokṣaprasaṅgaḥ | yathā ca malapariṇaterapi naiṣa prasaṅgaḥ 
tathoktamācāryeṇa tattvatrayanirṇaya eveti tad evāvadhāryam ||

	rūpāntareṇa svātantryaṃ śaktipātasya 
samarthayutumāgamārthamākṣipati-

patyā pumarthasiddhyarthaṃ sṛṣṭā mantrāḥ sanāyakāḥ || 157 ||

teṣāmeko'pi tatkartuṃ śaknoti bahubhistu kim |

	saptakoṭisaṃkhyātā mantrāḥ, aṣṭau ananteśādayo mantreśā 
bhagavatā puruṣārthasiddhaye nirmitā iti āgamārtha ekenaiva 
tatsiddherayukta iti ||

	etat pratikṣipati-

anugraho'pi sambhūtisteṣāṃ cānugrahādaraḥ || 158 ||

kṛtye niyogaḥ śaivokto notpattiḥ kṛtyasiddhaye |

	bahūnāṃ hi tadānīṃ tulyayogyatayā 
ādaro'nugrāhyamantramantreśvaratvena yugapadanugraho na tu 
adhikārārthamevotpattiḥ- iti utpattau tāvanna doṣaḥ | nanu adhikāro 
bhaviṣyati | na, ata evātra śaktipātasya svātantryāsiddheradoṣaḥ |

	atra paraḥ-

nanvanugrahataḥ śaivājjātāstulyabalāstu te || 159 ||

preryaprerakabhāvo hi teṣāṃ tena na yujyate |

	mantramantreśvarāṇāṃ parameśvarānugrahāt muktātmanāmiva 
śivasamānatvameveti-

te'dhikāraṃ prakurvanti śivecchāvidhicoditāḥ |

ityādiśrutivirodhastaṃ pariharati-

sarvajñānodayastulyastvādhipatyaṃ viśiṣyate || 160 ||

nābhivyaktaṃ hi tattādṛk teṣāṃ yādṛṅmaheśvare |

	ata eva śivecchāvidhicoditattvaśrutyanyathānupapattyā na teṣāṃ 
muktaśivavat kriyāśaktyāpi śivasamatvamabhivyaktamiti pratīyate, api tu 
sarvajñānasaṃyutāḥ iti śruteḥ jñānaśaktyaivetyavirodhaḥ ||

api ca-

adhikāro hi saṃskārasteṣāṃ caivāvadhīyate || 161 ||

yataḥ kṛtādhikārāste vrajanti śivatulyatām |

	yataḥ parameśvareṇāsāvadhikārātmakaḥ saṃskārasteṣāṃ 
sāvadhireva vihitaḥ | yacchrūyate-

anantoparame teṣāṃ mahatāṃ cakravartinām |
vihitaṃ sarvakartṛtvaṃ kāraṇaṃ paramaṃ padam ||

iti tato'pi na śivasamānatvam ||

	atra matāntaramāha-

tulyatve'pyarthaniṣpattirnānyathetyapare jaguḥ || 162 ||

śiṣyā iva guroḥ śambhoste taccittaniyoginaḥ |

	śivatulyatve'pi mantramantreśvarāṇāṃ tadīyādhikārakāritvaṃ 
śiṣyāṇāmiva bhaviṣyatīti kecit | taistu dṛṣṭāntena sāmyamatra cintyam, 
śiṣyāṇāṃ hi gurusāmye'pi tadīyaniyogākaraṇāt pratyavāyayogyatā, 
na tu teṣāṃ śivasamatvaṃ yataḥ ||

	prākśarīrābhyupagameneśvarasya kārakatvaṃ pratipāditam, 
adhunā tatpratikṣepeṇāha-

icchāmātreṇa deveśakṛtyakṛnna prayāsataḥ || 163 ||

icchā ca tasya kṛtyeṣu na pratīghāṭamaśnute |

	mātragrahaṇamavadhāraṇārtham | icchayaivāvyāhataprasarayā 
bhagavataḥ kartṛtvaṃ na śarīrakaraṇādiprayāseneti 
teṣāmabhāvo'nupayogāt ||

	nanu yaḥ kartā nāsau śarīrādiprayāsānapekṣaḥ avyāhateccho vā 
yathā devadattādiḥ, kartā ceśvaro'tastathābhūta eveti | atrāpi āha-

prayāsakaraṇātpuṃsāmicchā vyāghātadharmiṇi || 164 ||

paśutvāttacca devasya niṣiddhaṃ nyāyavartmanā |

	sarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvasadbhāve'pi puṃsāṃ malāvṛtatvāt kārye 
śarīrādyapekṣitvamanīśvaratvaṃ cetyuktam | īśvarasya tu tadbhāvāt 
tayorapyabhāvaḥ ||

nanu icchāmātreṇa puṃsāṃ kartṛtvamadṛṣṭamityāha-

stobhādikarmasaṃsiddhiricchāmātreṇa dṛśyate || 165 ||

puṃsāṃ prakṛṣṭamantrādisvabhāvāviddhacetasām |

	siddhamantrāṇāṃ hi viṣādistobhādikāryamicchāmātreṇa 
puruṣāṇāmapi dṛśyata eva ||

	etadeva darśayati-

paśurbandhanabandhī sa prabhāvātiśayāddhi tat || 166 ||

icchayaiva karotīśaḥ kuryānneti kimadbhutam |

	bandhanānāṃ viṣādīnāṃ bandhaḥ stobhādiḥ prāguktaḥ sa 
yasyāsti sa paśuḥ kāryakāraṇayuktaḥ prāgukta eva puruṣo 
mantraprabhāvātiśayādicchāmātreṇa taṃ stobhādikaṃ karoti, na 
tvīśvaraḥ tathābhūtaprabhāvātiśaya ityāścaryam ||

	yadapi āhuḥ kecit-

na cānimittayā yuktamutpattuṃ hīśvarecchayā |
yadvā tasyā nimittaṃ vastadbhūtānāṃ bhaviṣyati || iti |

	tatrāpi āha-

icchāyāḥ kārakagrāmaspṛṣṭāḥ kecitpracakṣate || 167 ||

nimittaṃ vastu mocyādi tajjñānaṃ karaṇaṃ tviti |

	mocyotpādyādivastuyogyataivecchāyāḥ samutpāde 
nimittamasmadādau tathaiva siddheḥ, karaṇaṃ tu tadviṣayaṃ 
karturjñānamitīśvare'pi tathaivopapatterna kiñcidetat ||

	ekānekavibhedo'pi tasyā nāhetuka ityāha-

ekakālaviniṣpādyeṣvekarūpeṣu vastuṣu || 168 ||

ekaivecchā tathānekā kālarūpavibhediṣu |

	sthitisaṃrakṣaṇādiviṣayākārabhedābhedābhyāṃ sthityādāvapi 
kālabhedābhedābhyāmasyā bhedābhedāviti nāhetukau ||

	sā ca kriyāśakternānyetyāha-

vāmādikaḥ kriyāśakterbhedo yaḥ so'pi gamyate || 169 ||

icchāyāḥ sarvakṛtyāni tayaiveśaḥ karoti yat |

yataḥ sarvakāryeṣvasmādādīnāmapīcchaivāvinābhāvinī kārakatayā 
siddhetyuktaṃ madhyamakāṇḍe, tataḥ saiva 
kriyāśaktirvāmādibhedairbhinnā bhagavataḥ śāstre pratipāditā- 
ityavagantavyaṃ na tato'nyeti tasyā asiddheḥ ||

	sā ca niravayavātmasvarūpāvyatirekād ātmarūpāyā 
jñānaśakterapyabhinneti dṛṣṭāntena darśayati-

gurubhiḥ kamalāśliṣṭā tathaivārkasya dīdhitiḥ || 170 ||

tatprakāśaṃ vikāsaṃ ca karotīti dvidhocyate |
tathaiva śaktirekaiva saṃśleṣādiva vastuṣu || 171 ||

vijñaptiṃ tatkriyāṃ caiva karotīti dvidhocyate |

	yathā hi saviturdīdhitiḥ kamaleṣvāśliṣṭā prabodhaprakāśau 
vidadhātīti dvidhocyate, tathaiva bhagavataḥ śaktirvastuṣu prakāśavikāsau 
vidadhatī dvividhā- iti kāryabhedāt bhedena vyapadiśyate, na tu 
paramārthato bhedo'syāḥ syāditi |

	tathaiva-

jñeyādhiṣṭheyaniṣpādyabhedādekāpi bhidyate || 172 ||

vāmādibhiranekaistu bhedaiḥ śaktirna mukhyataḥ |

	jñānaśakterjñeyabhedenaiva kriyāśakteḥ kāryabhedena ca bheda 
ityuktaṃ puruṣaparīkṣāyām, tataśca vāmādibhedo'pi tasyāḥ 
kāryabhedopādhiko na mukhyata iti |

	yenaivamicchaiva kriyāśaktiḥ-

prayāsena vineśānaḥ kṛtyakṛttena gīyate || 173 ||

	tena kāraṇena svadehaspandādau kumbhakārādiriveśvaro'pi 
śarīrāntaraprayāsānapekṣatayā jagati kartā prokto nānyatheti || 173 ||

	atra paraḥ-

icchāmātrātkarotīti nanvecchā nāsya vidyate |

	yujyate etad vaktum, yadīcchāsya sambhavet | sā tu manovṛttitvādasya 
ca kāryakaraṇānabhyupagamena manaso'sambhavāt na sambhavatīti |

	etat pratikṣipati-

śakteḥ pravṛttiḥ kṛtyeṣu viśiṣṭā proktalakṣaṇā || 174 ||

vyāpāraḥ kāraṇasyecchā tanniyogāya cocyate |

necchā manovṛttirapi tu kartuḥ kriyāśakteḥ kāryāya pravṛttiricchā 
procyate sarvavyāpāreṣu pūrvabhāvitvenāsyāḥ siddhatvādityuktam, 
yataḥ śāstreṣvapi īśvarasya vyāpāratayā vyāpāraniyogāya 
vidyeśvareṣu cocyate | yaduktam-

te'dhikāraṃ prakurvanti śivecchāvidhicoditāḥ | iti |

	kathaṃ tarhi icchā manovṛttiḥ sāṃkhyādibhirgīyata ityāha-

tādṛgvyāpārasaṅgītā śaktiricchā kvacinmatā || 175 ||

	yathā hi adhyavasāyādinā buddhivyāpāreṇopodvalitatvāt, 
jñānaśaktireva buddhibodha ityucyate, na tu buddherjaḍatayā vastuto bodhaḥ 
sambhavatyevaṃ kriyāśaktirevecchātmikā manovyāpāreṇa 
manorājyādyavasthāsu siddhenāpūrvanirmāṇātmanā 
abhivyajyamānatvāt manovṛttiriti kvacit sāṃkhyādāvucyate, na tu 
paramārthatastasyāpūrvanirmāṇaśaktirupapadyate 
nirmāṇasyeśvararūpatvena jaḍe'nupapatteriti || 175 ||

	asmi/stu śāstre-

arthāntaraṃ vinā kṛtyaṃ kurvatī ca pragīyate |

	manolakṣaṇārthāntaraṃ vinā īśvarasya kāryaṃ kurvatī icchā 
gīyate, sakalasya tu manolakṣaṇārthāntarāpekṣayeti ca śabdārthaḥ |

	upasaṃharati-

ekā sarvārthagā śaktirbhinnā cecchā na jātucit || 176 ||

	evaṃ ca jñānakriyātmikā śaktirekaiva, naiva cecchā tato'nyeti 
siddham || 176 ||

	nanu kāryabhedādicchāyāyāstataḥ śaktyantaratvamastu, 
yadveṣyamāṇānāṃ prakāśarūpatvena sukhādiprakāśānāmiva 
bodhātmakatvāt jñānarūpatvameva, na kriyāśaktirūpatetyāśaṅkyāha-

śaktānuktaṃ yato'stīdaṃ tena nādhikakalpanā |

	kriyāśaktisiddhinimittaṃ yat sādhanamuktaṃ tadevecchāyāṃ 
yato'sti tena kāraṇena na tato'dhikā kācidicchā nāpi jñānarūpeti | 
etaduktaṃ bhavati- svadeha- spandādikriyāhetutvena 
svasaṃvedanātmakasādhanasiddhā kriyāśaktiricchaiva, vastutaśca

putrāderjñānaśaktyavabhāsitasya mamedaṃ syāt iti 
ātmasambandhitānirmāṇasvabhāvatvānna jñānarūpā, api tu 
kriyāśaktireveti | tadidamuktaṃ mantravārtike-

	śāktasya hi tanmanasastvicchā hyeṣā kriyāśaktiḥ | iti |

	tadevam-

icchāmātreṇa deveśaḥ kṛtyakṛnna prayāsataḥ |

iti siddham ||

	nanu siddhasādhanametadityāha-

na cāpi vimatirnāsti sarvasyaiṣāprasiddhitaḥ || 177 ||

	na hi mīmāṃsakabauddhalaukāyatikānāmetatsiddhamiti, na 
siddhasādhanam || 177 ||

	tarhi asiddha evāyaṃ pakṣa ityāha-

tathāpi vigatakṣānti grāhyaḥ pakṣo yathāgaman |

	yadyapi viṣayatāmayaṃ pakṣo bhavadbhirnītastathāpi vigatakṣānti, 
akṣāntikaṃ kṛtvāsmadupadarśitasyātra hetoratyantaṃ parīkṣāṃ vidhāya 
grāhyo noparodheneti | yaduktam-

parīkṣyākṣāntiko grāhyaṃ madvaco na tu gauravāt | iti ||

	nanu siddhasādhanametat iti sāṃkhyāḥ, yogināṃ sattvātmanaḥ 
karmajaiśvaryasya tairabhyupagamāt | yaduktam-

dharmeṇa gamanamūrdhvaṃ gamanamadhastādbhavatyadharmeṇa |
ūrdhvaṃ sattvaviśālastamoviśālaśca mūlataḥ sargaḥ |
madhye rajoviśālo brahmādistambaparyantaḥ || iti ||

	atrāpyāha-

sarvādhipatyamaiśvaryaṃ karmābhāvānna dharmajam || 178 ||

apāstātiśayācchambhostena svecchāvidhāyyasau |

	anyadeva tat yogināṃ parimitamaiśvaryaṃ karmajatvāt, 
kṣayātiśayayuktaṃ ca tairiṣyate | anyadeva tvidaṃ 
sarvārthaviṣayamādyāntarahitatvena pratipāditatvādakarmajam, 
icchāmātrakārakatvena pratipāditamiti na siddhasādhanatā ||

nanu tato'pi prakṛṣṭaiśvaryayukto'nyo bhaviṣyatītyāha-

svātmaiśvaryaprabhāvena tadyenāsyātiśayyate || 179 ||

maheśvaraḥ sa eva syādayaṃ na�veśvarastadā |

tarhi sa eva parameśvaro'smābhiriṣyata ityadoṣaḥ ||

na ca tato'pyanyo bhaviṣyatītyanavasthetyāha-

parimāṇavadaiśvarye sarvatrātiśayānvitam || 180 ||

kvaciccātiśayāpāstaṃ yatra tatsa maheśvaraḥ |

	yo'tiśayānvitaḥ sa sambhavatvakvacitprakarṣaparyantāvasthitiḥ, na tu 
anavasthāyukto yathā paramāṇuto mahanmahattvātiśayayuktaḥ kvacideva 
sthitaḥ paramamahattvāntaḥ parimāṇaḥ, 
atiśayānvitaścāyamaiśvaryaviśeṣa iti so'pi 
sambhavatkvacitprakarṣaparyantāvasthitiryatra tathābhūtaḥ sa eva 
parameśvara iti nānavasthā |

	prakaraṇamupasaṃhartuṃ prayojanamasyādāvāha-

evaṃ sarvottamaṃ śambhuṃ jñātvā sarvaphalapradam || 181 ||

sadālokayataḥ śreyaḥ sadyaḥ sarvaṃ ca nānyataḥ |

	evamatra pradarśitasādhakabādhakapramāṇasadasattāniścaya-
pūrvaṃ proktanayena darśanāntaraprasiddhebhyaḥ prakṛtipuruṣādibhyaḥ 
īśvarebhyaḥ sātiśayamīśvaramata eva sarvaphalapradaṃ jñātvā tameva 
bhāvayataḥ tata eva dīkṣākrameṇāvilambataḥ paraṃ śreyo muktiḥ 
siddhiśca bhavatīti prayojanasiddhiriti uktamasmābhiranyatra-

praṇamata śivanāthaṃ śāśvataṃ śarmaheto-
ritaraviṣayasevādurgrahaṃ proñjhya yatnāt |
na sa bhavati bhave'sminnāpi bhūto na bhāvī
taditaranutito yaḥ śarmayuktaḥ pumān syāt ||

iti ||

	adhunā upasaṃharati-

itthaṃ sarvaparīkṣāṃ tu puṃsparīkṣāpurassarām || 182 ||

cakāra mṛdubuddhīnāṃ hitāyātisphuṭāmimām |
sadyojyotiḥ samāno yaṃ śivena gurusattamaḥ ||
kṛpayaivānujagrāha hyugrajyotirguṇākaraḥ || 183 ||

evaṃ ca proktanayeneśvaraparīkṣākṣiptaiva naraparīkṣeti tatprayojanenaiva 
saprayojaneti siddham || 183 ||

darśanamātrācchrīmatsadyojyotistamo nihantyugram |
satatāvalokanena tu saddṛṣṭīścandra iva vivardhayati ||

nareśvaraparīkṣaṇe'tigahanatvato naiva yad
gatirjagati gamyate vitatabuddhivegiṣvapi |
imaṃ parahitecchayā tu nanu rāmakaṇṭhastataḥ
kutarkatamasāṃ nudaṃ vihitavānprakāśaṃ sphuṭam ||

|| iti śrīnārāyaṇakaṇṭhasūnubhaṭṭarāmakaṇṭhakṛtau 
nareśvaraparīkṣāprakāśe paradarśanaprasiddheśvarapratikṣepākhyaṃ 
tṛtīyaṃ kāṇḍam || 3 ||

sampūrṇaścāyaṃ nareśvaraparīkṣāprakāśaḥ ||
